Result: DAPF在整体性能上最优（准确率=0.83，宏F1=0.83），其[MASK]表示中最易恢复诊断信息；但令牌级解释忠实度不佳，归因结果主要集中于语言任务词汇、话语标记和转录伪影，扰动测试效果微弱甚至为负。

Conclusion: DAPF的掩码令牌接口能够有效确定诊断信息，但其令牌级解释缺乏忠实性。模型的性能优势与可解释性优势并不一致，提示在追求高性能的同时需要关注解释质量，或需设计能同时保证两者的一致化机制。

Abstract: Spoken-language analysis via prompt-based domain-adaptive models is a promising direction for low-resource, non-invasive dementia screening, but such models remain internally opaque. We study the interpretability of the Domain-Adapted models via Prompt-based Fine-tuning (DAPF) framework, which casts dementia detection as diagnosis-related masked-token prediction. We interpret DAPF and strong baselines using a variety of probing and analysis techniques, finding that DAPF achieved the best overall performance (accuracy=0.83 and macro-F1=0.83) with diagnosis most recoverable from its [MASK] representation. However, this representational advantage did not extend to token-level explanation faithfulness. DAPF attributions primarily reflected language task vocabulary, discourse markers, and transcription artifacts, with perturbation tests showing weak or negative effects. This suggests that its masked-token interface determines diagnosis information without producing faithful token-level explanations.

</details>


### [94] [DataKernelBench: Can LLMs Optimize Database Queries on GPUs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25061)
*Gokul Karthik Kumar,Yotam Perlitz,Corey Lammie,Andrea Giovannini,Katja Hose*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DataKernelBench 是一个将 SQL 翻译为 PyTorch TorchPlan 程序、通过执行引导修复评估 LLM 优化数据库风格算子的基准测试框架。在 TPC-H SF10 和 H100 上，最强全查询 CUDA 配置相比 torch.compile 实现 2.11 倍加速且全通过率；通过 Dask-cuDF 扩展至 TPC-H SF100 多 GPU 场景，实现 2.54 倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU 日益加速数据库系统，但查询级峰值性能仍常依赖手工编写内核。现有 LLM 内核基准测试侧重于机器学习算子，未覆盖不规则的、异构的、数据移动密集的数据库风格算子，缺乏系统的评测基准。

Method: 将 SQL 转换为可验证的 PyTorch TorchPlan 程序，在两个粒度（核心张量有界片段或完整查询）评估 LLM 生成 CUDA 或 Triton 代码的能力，并通过执行引导修复（execution-guided repair）迭代改进。针对超 GPU 内存数据，扩展 TorchPlan 集成 Dask-cuDF 实现按需分区加载。

Result: 在 TPC-H SF10 上评估十种专有和开源模型，最强全查询 CUDA 配置相比 torch.compile 取得 2.11 倍加速且达到全通过率。高性能实现普遍采用内核融合和执行策略变更；更强的模型受益于全查询特化；工作负载上下文比硬件上下文更重要。在 TPC-H SF100 四块 H100 上通过 Dask-cuDF 扩展实现 2.54 倍加速。

Conclusion: LLM 能够有效生成数据库风格 GPU 内核，全查询级别的特化优化比片段级优化带来更大收益，且工作负载上下文信息对代码生成质量影响显著。DataKernelBench 填补了 LLM 内核生成在数据库算子领域的基准空白。

Abstract: GPUs increasingly accelerate database systems, but query-specific peak performance still often relies on hand-written kernels. Existing LLM kernel benchmarks focus on machine learning operators, leaving irregular, heterogeneous, data-movement-heavy database-style operators untested. We introduce DataKernelBench, which translates SQL into validated PyTorch TorchPlan programs and evaluates LLMs that optimize either the core tensor-bounded snippet or the full query in CUDA or Triton through execution-guided repair. Across ten proprietary and open-weight models on TPC-H SF10 with an H100 GPU, the strongest full-query CUDA configuration achieves $2.11\times$ speedup over torch.compile at full pass rate. We find that higher-performing implementations commonly use kernel fusion and execution-strategy changes, stronger models benefit most from full-query specialization, and workload context matters more than hardware context. To handle data larger than GPU memory, we extend TorchPlan with Dask-cuDF for on-demand partition loading on TPC-H SF100 with four H100 GPUs, achieving $2.54\times$ speedup

</details>


### [95] [HealthBench-Psych: A Mental Health Subset of OpenAI's HealthBench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25071)
*Matthew Flathers,Phuong Anh Nguyen,Jill Noorily,Julian Herpertz,Meiting Chen,Jasreen Multani,Samuel Powell,Mason Granof,Mark Kalinch,John Torous*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了HealthBench-Psych和HealthBench-Psych-Hard，从HealthBench的5,000个带医生评分标准的对话中筛选出610个心理健康相关对话（占12.2%），经两轮盲审临床验证后，用三个LLM评审员评估20个前沿及开放模型，发现前沿模型集群在统计上并列、两个模型存在可测的拒答行为，且不同评审员排名高度一致（τ≥0.92），并公开了子集、流水线、模型响应、评分及分析代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用健康基准往往无法按临床专科细分，难以隔离如心理健康等特定领域表现；而现有心理健康评估多为学术定制基准，难以整合进开发者工作流。心理健康是重大公共卫生关切，数以百万计用户向LLM寻求心理支持，亟需一个可集成、专科细分的评估资源。

Method: 首先用透明LLM应用规则从HealthBench的5,000个医师标准对话中筛选心理健康相关内容，再通过两轮盲审（含隐藏的已知排除对照）由临床医生验证子集，最终得到610个对话。随后使用跨厂商的三个LLM评审员对20个前沿及开放模型进行评分，并分析排名一致性与拒答行为。

Result: 从5,000个对话中筛选出610个心理健康相关对话（12.2%语料）。在20个模型的评估中，发现前沿模型形成统计上并列的集群；两个模型表现出可测量的拒答行为；三个LLM评审员之间的排名高度一致（τ≥0.92）。

Conclusion: HealthBench-Psych及其Hard版本可作为心理健康专科评估的可复用资源，能够集成到开发者工作流中，并提供跨评审员稳定的排名信号。数据集、流水线、响应、评分及分析代码均已公开。

Abstract: General-purpose health benchmarks increasingly anchor claims about LLM medical performance, but they are not always resolved by clinical specialty, making domain-specific performance hard to isolate. Mental health is of acute public-health concern as millions of people turn to LLMs for psychological support, and most existing evaluations are bespoke academic benchmarks that are difficult to integrate into developer workflows. We introduce HealthBench-Psych and HealthBench-Psych-Hard. We screened HealthBench's 5,000 physician-rubric conversations for mental-health relevance with a transparent LLM-applied rubric, then validated the subset through two rounds of blinded clinician review with concealed known-exclude controls, yielding 610 conversations (12.2% of the corpus). Evaluating 20 frontier and open models under a cross-vendor panel of three LLM judges, we find a statistically tied frontier cluster, measurable refusal behavior in two models, and near-identical rankings across judges ($τ\ge 0.92$). We release the subset, pipeline, model responses, grades, and analysis code as a reusable resource.

</details>


### [96] [MTDiag: A Multi-Turn Diagnostic Dataset Towards Clinically Meaningful LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25085)
*Pia Chouayfati,Alexander M. Fichtl,Miriam Anschütz,George Doumat,Georg Groh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MTDiag，一个大规模多轮诊断对话数据集，由DDXPlus、MIMIC-IV和AJCR三个异构来源构建，覆盖常见急诊表现及长尾罕见病。数据集统一为基于UMLS概念标识符和ICD-10编码的规范模式，并发布了UserLM-8B话语生成管线和经医师验证的数据集，同时提出基于临床知识的多轮鉴别诊断评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现行医学领域LLM评估主要依赖静态QA基准或模板化对话，无法反映模型在真实动态临床交互中的诊断能力。已有研究表明LLM在多轮设置下准确性和可靠性显著下降，亟需一个能够评估LLM作为诊断代理的动态临床场景基准。

Method: 构建MTDiag数据集：从DDXPlus、MIMIC-IV和AJCR三个异构来源收集病例，统一为以UMLS概念标识符和ICD-10诊断编码为锚点的规范模式；开发基于UserLM-8B的话语生成管线，将结构化临床证据转换为自然语言话语；数据集经医师验证，并设计基于临床知识图谱的评估指标（超越单纯诊断准确率）用于多轮鉴别诊断任务评估。

Result: 发布了MTDiag多轮诊断对话数据集、规范模式（Schema）、UserLM-8B话语生成管线以及经医师验证的数据集，并引入了临床知识接地的新型评估指标来评估LLM作为诊断代理在多轮鉴别诊断中的表现。

Conclusion: 通过MTDiag及其知识接地评估指标，为评估LLM在动态临床交互中的诊断能力提供了更贴近真实临床实践的基准框架，弥补了既有静态基准的不足。

Abstract: Clinical diagnosis is fundamentally interactive and incremental, yet the dominant paradigm for evaluating Large Language Models (LLMs) in medicine remains static QA benchmarks or template-based dialogues. These benchmarks say little about whether a model can serve as a diagnostic agent in a dynamic clinical encounter, with LLMs showing significant accuracy and reliability degradation in multi-turn settings. To address this issue, we present MTDiag, a large multi-turn diagnostic dialogue dataset constructed from three heterogeneous sources: DDXPlus, MIMIC-IV, and published case reports (AJCR), covering common ED presentations as well as long-tail rare and atypical conditions. All cases are normalized into a canonical schema anchored in the most comprehensive and widely-adopted medical knowledge bases (UMLS concept identifiers, with ICD-10 diagnosis codes). We release the schema, a UserLM-8B-based utterance-generation pipeline, and the physician-validated dataset that converts structured clinical evidence into natural-language utterances. Importantly, we introduce and motivate clinical knowledge-grounded metrics for evaluating LLMs as diagnostic agents, beyond diagnostic accuracy, for the task of multi-turn differential diagnosis.

</details>


### [97] [Apples to Apples? Towards Comparable Crosslingual Language Model Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25089)
*Xiulin Yang,Ethan Gotlieb Wilcox,Catherine Arnett*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了多语言模型中跨语言评估方法的可靠性，发现多种广泛使用的归一化指标会因分词、编码和正字法差异引入跨语言偏差，而基于语义等价序列的句子级负对数似然能提供更有意义且一致的跨语言比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言NLP中，实现公平的跨语言模型评估仍然是一个根本性挑战。现有研究采用各种下游任务和内在指标进行评估，但缺乏对这些方法能否产生有意义的跨语言结论的实证调查。

Method: 使用在平行数据上训练的受控单语语言模型，系统地考察不同分词器词汇表大小和模型大小对跨语言评估的影响，并在多语言大语言模型上进一步验证发现。

Result: 多种广泛使用的归一化指标引入了根源于分词、编码和正字法差异的跨语言偏差；相比之下，基于语义等价序列的句子级负对数似然提供了更有意义且一致的跨语言比较。

Conclusion: 实现跨语言可比的下游评估存在挑战，建议采用句子级负对数似然作为更可靠的跨语言评估指标。

Abstract: Crosslingual evaluation of language models that enables fair comparisons remains a fundamental challenge in multilingual NLP. Existing studies adopt a variety of downstream tasks and intrinsic metrics with different theoretical justifications, yet there has been little empirical investigation into whether these approaches yield meaningful crosslingual conclusions. We systematically examine crosslingual evaluation approaches using controlled monolingual language models trained on parallel data with varying tokenizer vocabulary sizes and model sizes, and further validate our findings on multilingual LLMs. We further discuss challenges in achieving comparable downstream evaluation across languages. Our results show that several widely used normalized metrics introduce crosslinguistic biases rooted in tokenization, encoding, and orthographic differences. In contrast, sentence-level negative log-likelihood computed over semantically equivalent sequences provides more meaningful and consistent crosslingual comparisons.

</details>


### [98] [Less can be More: Relieving RAG Bottlenecks via Evidence Frontloading and Pressure-Adaptive Budgeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25115)
*Weibin Cai,Reza Zafarani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PACE框架，通过证据前置加载和压力自适应预算分配，解决RAG系统中上游重排序与下游生成之间的瓶颈漂移问题，在不影响证据召回率的前提下降低p95延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG效率优化方法主要聚焦于下游LLM生成环节（如上下文压缩或服务优化），但RAG是端到端系统，其瓶颈会在上游重排序和下游生成之间动态漂移。特别是在高查询率或大重排序预算下，上游重排序会成为主导瓶颈，而简单减少重排序预算虽能缓解瓶颈却可能丢失支撑证据、降低召回率。

Method: PACE由两个核心组件构成：（1）证据前置加载（Evidence Frontloading）：按边际证据覆盖率对候选文档重新排序，优先选择查询相关、互补且有助于形成多跳证据链的文档，该目标是单调子模函数，贪心选择具有(1-1/e)近似保证；（2）压力自适应预算分配（Pressure-Adaptive Budgeting）：根据重排序器和LLM的相对压力动态调整重排序预算。整个框架无需训练。

Result: 在三个多跳QA数据集和在线服务模拟实验中，PACE在重排序密集型工作负载下提升了证据召回率并降低了p95延迟。更关键的是，两个组件的结合揭示"少即是多"：证据密集的前置候选文档能用更少的重排序文档实现更高的最终召回率。

Conclusion: PACE通过将瓶颈意识引入RAG系统设计，证明了对上游重排序环节进行智能优化（而非仅优化下游生成）能显著提升端到端RAG系统的效率和效果，且训练无关的框架设计使其易于部署。

Abstract: Existing methods for improving Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) efficiency mainly optimize downstream LLM generation, such as context compression or serving optimization. However, RAG is an end-to-end system, and its bottleneck can shift between upstream reranking and downstream generation under different serving loads and reranking budgets.In this paper, we first empirically characterize this shifting-bottleneck behavior and show that upstream reranking can become the dominant bottleneck under high query rates or large reranking budgets. Reducing the reranking budget can relieve this bottleneck, but it may also drop supporting evidence and degrade recall. To address this problem, we propose \textbf{\textsf{PACE}} (\textbf{P}rioritized \textbf{A}daptive \textbf{C}overage of \textbf{E}vidence), a training-free framework that combines \textit{evidence frontloading} with \textit{pressure-adaptive budgeting}. \textsf{PACE} first reorders candidates by marginal evidence coverage, prioritizing documents that are query-relevant, complementary, and useful for forming multi-hop evidence chains. We show that this objective is monotone submodular, giving greedy selection a $(1-1/e)$ approximation guarantee. \textsf{PACE} then dynamically adjusts the reranking budget according to the relative pressure of the reranker and the LLM. Experiments on three multi-hop QA datasets and online serving simulations show that \textsf{PACE} improves evidence recall, reduces p95 latency under ranking-heavy workloads. More importantly, the two components together reveal that \textit{less can be more}: an evidence-dense top-ranked candidates enable higher final recall with fewer reranked documents.

</details>


### [99] [SelfGraphRAG: Bridging the Supervision Gap in Graph-Based RAG with Synthetic QA Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25123)
*Ben Lagnese,Manas Gaur*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SelfGraphRAG是一个无需人工标注的图检索增强生成框架，它直接从知识图谱结构生成问答对来训练查询条件下的图检索器，从而在多跳问答和分类任务上优于基于嵌入的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RAG方法未能充分利用知识图谱中编码的关系结构；监督式图检索通常需要带标签的问答数据，但对于新构建的图谱这类数据可能不可用，这限制了图检索器的训练。

Method: SelfGraphRAG直接从知识图谱结构自动生成问答对，利用这些数据训练查询条件下的图检索器。生成的问句覆盖多跳路径和局部邻域信息，提供无需人工标注的关系级监督信号。

Result: 在多跳问答和分类基准测试上，SelfGraphRAG相比基于嵌入的基线方法显著提升了检索精度和下游推理性能。

Conclusion: 知识图谱结构本身能够为图检索器的训练提供有效的监督信号，即使在缺少人工标注数据的情况下，也能训练出高效的图检索器，从而提升RAG系统的整体表现。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves large language models by incorporating external knowledge without retraining, but existing methods often underuse the relational structure encoded in knowledge graphs. Graph-based RAG can capture entity relationships, yet supervised graph retrieval typically requires labeled question-answer data that may not be available for newly constructed graphs. We address this limitation with SelfGraphRAG, a framework that generates question-answer pairs directly from knowledge graph structure and uses them to train a query-conditioned graph retriever. The generated questions capture multi-hop paths and local neighborhoods, providing relational supervision without manual annotation. Experiments on multi-hop question answering and classification benchmarks show that SelfGraphRAG improves retrieval precision and downstream reasoning performance over embedding-based baselines. These results suggest that knowledge graph structure can provide useful supervision for training graph retrievers when labeled data are unavailable.

</details>


### [100] [Belief Cascades Drive Persuasion in LLM Agent Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25152)
*Haoyi Qiu,Genglin Liu,Pranav Narayanan Venkit,Kung-Hsiang Huang,Saadia Gabriel,Chien-Sheng Wu,Nanyun Peng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个受控测试平台，用于在多智能体LLM网络中研究目标导向的劝说行为，发现劝说动态取决于拓扑、竞争、话题和模型先验之间的交互，且直接的暴露可可靠预测下一轮立场变化，而仅分析文本会遗漏重要的立场移动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统越来越普遍地用于辩论、协作研究、用户模拟和信息流中介，但agent之间的劝说能力作为一项基础能力却缺乏系统的量化评估手段。

Method: 构建一个受控测试平台，基于真实世界ego网络拓扑，在多种LLM骨干模型、多个网络图结构和55条政策陈述上，研究目标导向的劝说者如何改变网络中被试者的立场；通过信念探针、暴露溯源和行动日志来全面评估劝说效果。

Result: 发现劝说动态依赖于拓扑、竞争、话题和模型先验的交互作用；直接暴露能可靠预测竞争场景下的下一轮立场变化；同伴中继传递少量但可测量的劝说力量；仅分析帖子文本会遗漏重要信息——策略只在执行消息中部分实现，行动选择可能偏离消息内容，探针检测到的立场变化在文本中很少被明确陈述。

Conclusion: 应将以轨迹和暴露级别的方法来评估多智能体劝说，使用信念探针、暴露溯源和行动日志来确定谁影响了谁，以及可见语言是否反映了潜在的立场变化。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems increasingly debate answers, coordinate research, simulate users, and mediate information flows, making agent-to-agent persuasion a basic but undermeasured capability. We introduce a controlled testbed for studying how goal-directed persuaders shift elicited stances in networks of LLM agents grounded in real-world ego-network topologies. Across four LLM backbones, five graphs, and 55 policy statements, we find that persuasion dynamics depend on the interaction between topology, competition, topic, and model prior. Additionally, we show that direct exposure reliably predicts next-round stance change in competing runs, and peer relays carry smaller but measurable influence, showing that agents not assigned to persuade can still transmit persuasive force. Finally, analyzing post text alone misses important movement: planned strategies are only partly realized in executed messages, action choices can diverge from message content, and persuadees rarely state the stance shifts detected by probes. These results argue for evaluating multi-agent persuasion as a trajectory- and exposure-level process, using belief probes, exposure provenance, and action logs to identify who influenced whom and whether visible language reflects underlying stance movement.

</details>


### [101] [The Changing Geometry of Grammar: Dimensionality and Neighborhood Reorganization across Transformer Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25166)
*Samuele Vallisa,Federico Ravenda,Claudio Palominos,Rui He,Andrea Raballo,Antonietta Mira,Philipp Homan,Wolfram Hinzen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了Transformer表征中词性标签（PoS）如何塑造流形的局部几何结构，通过内在维度（ID）分析发现闭类词比开类词更早扩展和收缩，并证明几何特征可单独用于恢复词的语法角色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer表征在高层向量空间中动态演化，数据倾向于压缩在低维子流形上，而词的语法角色是否影响这种局部几何结构尚不清楚。理解这一点有助于深入解析Transformer如何编码和整合语法信息。

Method: (1) 逐层计算词性标签类别的内在维度（ID），比较闭类词与开类词的演化；(2) 通过邻域结构变化解释ID的扩展与收缩；(3) 对比编码器（ModernBERT, bigbird-roberta-large）和解码器（gemma-2-2B, Llama-3.2-3B）两类模型族的层间演化差异；(4) 仅使用几何特征进行词性分类任务，并解释下游分类中每种词性的语义内容如何逐层演化。

Result: 实验发现闭类词项比开类词项更早扩展且更早坍缩；ID的扩展收缩可由邻域结构变化解释；编码器与解码器在层间演化方式上存在系统性差异；纯几何特征即可恢复词的语法角色，并能用于解释下游任务中各词性的语义演化。

Conclusion: 词的语法角色系统性塑造了Transformer表征的局部流形几何，几何特征本身携带了足够的语法信息以重建词性标签，且不同模型架构族对上下文整合方式的不同导致了不同的几何演化模式。

Abstract: Transformer representations describe trajectories through high-dimensional vector spaces, which are shaped dynamically as tokens incorporate relational context across layers. Such data tend to concentrate on lower-dimensional sub-manifolds, a form of compression quantified by the Intrinsic Dimensionality (ID), the minimum number of independent variables needed to represent them without significant information loss. In this work, we ask whether the grammatical role of tokens, as marked by their part-of-speech (PoS) tag, shapes the local geometry of this manifold. To this end: (1) We investigate the layer-wise evolution of ID, finding that closed-class items expand earlier and collapse sooner than open-class ones; (2) We show its expansion and contraction to be explained by changes in the neighborhood structure, and hence in the relations between words within a sentence; (3) We compare encoders (ModernBERT, bigbird-roberta-large) and decoders (gemma-2-2B, Llama-3.2-3B), finding that the two families evolve differently across layers, consistently with how each integrates context;(4) We show that geometric features alone recover a token's grammatical role, and use them to interpret how the semantic content of each PoS evolves across layers in a downstream classification task.

</details>


### [102] [BanglaMamba: Exploring State Space Models for Bangla Fake News Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25190)
*M. K. Khalidi Siam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出BanglaMamba，一种基于Mamba状态空间模型的孟加拉语假新闻检测方法，在大幅提升推理吞吐量（约2.2倍）和降低峰值GPU内存（约49%）的同时，取得了与BERT类模型相当的性能（Macro-F1为0.9029），验证了Mamba架构在资源受限环境中作为Transformer高效替代方案的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型（如BanglaBERT）在孟加拉语假新闻检测中表现优异，但其二次方计算复杂度使其在处理长文档和资源受限环境时效率低下。作者旨在探索Mamba状态空间模型作为Transformer的高效替代方案，以在不显著牺牲性能的前提下降低计算开销。

Method: 提出BanglaMamba，基于Mamba状态空间模型构建孟加拉语假新闻检测系统。将其与预训练的BanglaBERT、从零训练的CustomBERT三个模型进行对比实验，评估指标包括Macro-F1分数、推理吞吐量和推理峰值GPU内存使用量；同时进行跨数据集评估以测试模型泛化能力。

Result: BanglaBERT取得最高Macro-F1（0.9260），BanglaMamba（0.9029）与从零训练的CustomBERT（0.9057）性能相当；BanglaMamba推理吞吐量约为BERT模型的2.2倍，峰值GPU内存使用降低49%；跨数据集评估中BanglaBERT泛化能力更强，表明大规模预训练的重要性。

Conclusion: Mamba状态空间模型在孟加拉语假新闻检测中能够提供与Transformer架构竞争的性能，同时显著提升计算效率，尤其适用于资源受限和长文档处理场景。但预训练规模仍然是提升泛化能力的关键因素。

Abstract: Fake news detection has become an important Natural Language Processing (NLP) task due to the rapid spread of misinformation through online news platforms and social media. While transformer-based models such as BanglaBERT achieve strong performance for Bangla text classification, their quadratic computational complexity makes them less suitable for long-document processing in resource-constrained environments. This paper investigates Mamba-based State Space Models (SSMs) as an efficient alternative for Bangla fake news detection. We propose BanglaMamba and compare it with pre-trained BanglaBERT and a similarly configured BERT model trained from scratch. Experimental results show that BanglaBERT achieves the highest Macro-F1 score (0.9260), while BanglaMamba (0.9029) achieves performance comparable to the from-scratch CustomBERT (0.9057) despite using a different architecture. Meanwhile, BanglaMamba achieves approximately $2.2\times$ higher inference throughput and 49% lower inference peak GPU memory usage than the BERT-based models. Cross-dataset evaluation shows that BanglaBERT generalizes better to an external dataset, highlighting the importance of large-scale pretraining. These findings demonstrate that Mamba-based SSMs can provide a competitive and computationally efficient alternative to Transformer-based architectures for Bangla fake news detection, particularly in resource-constrained settings.

</details>


### [103] [From Memorization to Absorption: Mixed-Policy RL for Continual Knowledge Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25243)
*Zhibo Hou,Fan Zhao,Zhiyu An,Wan Du*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出GRIN (Golden-GRPO Injection)，一个三阶段自学习框架，用于持续知识注入到大型语言模型中。通过混合策略强化学习（Golden-GRPO）而非传统监督微调，让模型不仅记住新知识，还能泛化到释义、文档组合和推理场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续知识注入方法依赖监督微调（SFT），虽然能记住训练格式中的注入事实，但在面对释义改写、多文档组合和推理任务时泛化能力不足。需要一种新的学习机制来超越简单的记忆，实现真正的知识吸收与泛化。

Method: 提出三阶段自学习框架GRIN，核心是Golden-GRPO——一种专为知识注入设计的混合策略强化学习算法。即使on-policy rollout在遇到新事实时失败，Golden-GRPO仍通过注入golden answer提供学习信号。同时构建了两个文档级基准：Blank（新知识获取）和Counter（反事实覆盖写），各自评估单事实回忆、多源检索和推理能力。

Result: 实验证明混合策略强化学习能够实现SFT无法达到的知识吸收效果。GRIN在更难的问题类型（推理等）上显著优于SFT和混合策略RL基线，在基础事实回忆上与它们持平。

Conclusion: GRIN验证了混合策略强化学习在持续知识注入中的优势，表明通过适当设计的RL方法可以超越SFT的"记忆"局限，实现对新知识的更深层次理解和泛化应用。

Abstract: Continual knowledge injection is essential for keeping large language models up-to-date in a fast-evolving world. Existing methods rely on supervised fine-tuning (SFT), which memorizes injected facts in their training format but fails to generalize across paraphrasing, document combinations, and reasoning. To address this, we propose Golden-GRPO Injection (GRIN), a three-stage self-learning framework for continual knowledge injection. Golden-GRPO is a mixed-policy reinforcement learning algorithm designed specifically for knowledge injection, which injects a golden answer to provide learning signal even when on-policy rollouts fail on novel facts. We further introduce Blank and Counter, two document-level benchmarks targeting novel acquisition and counterfactual overwrite respectively, each evaluating single-fact recall, multi-source retrieval, and inferential reasoning. Our experiments establish a clear empirical claim: mixed-policy reinforcement learning enables knowledge absorption beyond what supervised fine-tuning can achieve. GRIN substantially outperforms SFT and mixed-policy RL baselines on the harder question types while matching them on basic fact recall.

</details>


### [104] [Groundhog Bit-Flip Attack: Seeding Infinite Generation Loops in Mixture-of-Experts LLMs through Bit Flips](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25276)
*Huakang Lin,Tiancheng Zheng,Mingxuan Sun,Tianhong Xu,Fan Zhang,Yunsi Fei,Ruyi Ding*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Groundhog位翻转攻击（GBFA），首个针对MoE架构大语言模型的、基于位翻转的拒绝服务型可用性攻击。通过翻转路由层中与专家激活相关的比特位，禁用平均不到4个专家，即可使输出长度膨胀至原始长度的5912%，且保持语义保真度，揭示了MoE架构在鲁棒性方面的严重漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构通过路由机制选择性激活专家子网络，虽然提升了扩展性和效率，但这种自适应设计引入了新的攻击面：特定专家与某些token（如序列结束符）存在不成比例的相关性，攻击者可以利用轻量级扰动操纵模型行为，导致未定义的高计算开销（denial-of-wallet攻击）。目前缺乏针对MoE架构的位翻转可用性攻击研究。

Method: 1) 识别路由层中与相关专家激活关联的比特位；2) 通过翻转这些路由层比特位，禁用关键专家；3) 在三种LLM模式下（对话、推理、智能体任务）评估攻击效果；4) 在四个主流真实世界MoE-based LLM上进行验证。

Result: 在四个主流MoE-based LLM上，平均手动禁用不到4个专家即可使输出膨胀率达到5912%，且大多数测试样本达到最大token上限，同时保持较高的语义保真度。

Conclusion: MoE架构对位翻转攻击存在鲁棒性漏洞，GBFA作为一种可用性攻击工具，能够以极小的硬件扰动成本实现显著的计算资源消耗放大，突出了开发针对MoE架构安全防护机制的必要性。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures enable scalable and efficient large language models (LLMs) by selectively activating expert sub-networks through a routing mechanism. However, this adaptive design introduces a new attack surface: specific experts become disproportionately correlated with certain tokens (e.g., end-of-sequence), allowing adversaries to manipulate model behavior via lightweight perturbations. In this work, we present \textbf{Groundhog Bit-Flip Attack (GBFA)}, the first bit-flip-based \textit{ Denial-of-Wallet availability attack} against MoE-based LLMs. By identifying and flipping routing-layer bits associated with related expert activations, we demonstrate that GBFA substantially extends the decoding token usage across three different LLM modes: conversational, reasoning, and agentic tasks, while largely preserving semantic fidelity. Across four main real-world MoE-based LLMs, manually deactivating on average fewer than \textbf{4 experts} drives average output inflation to $\mathbf{5912\%}$, with the majority of test samples reaching max tokens. These results reveal a robustness vulnerability of MoE architectures to bit flip, and highlight the potential of GBFA as an availability attack against LLMs.

</details>


### [105] [Routed Graph Handoff: Adaptive Format Selection for Multi-Agent LLM Delegation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25277)
*Pratyay Banerjee,Ankit Chadha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Routed Graph Handoff方法，通过一个轻量级LLM路由器（155 tokens，仅0.15%开销）在结构化依赖图和自然语言之间为每次委派动态选择通信方式，在四个基准测试上匹配或超越纯自然语言方法的同时实现2-3倍压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统通过自然语言消息进行协调，消耗了40-60%的token预算，成本过高；而用结构化图替代虽能降低成本但无法应对需要自适应推理的任务。需要在效率和推理能力之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出Routed Graph Handoff框架：设计一个轻量级LLM路由器（每次仅155 tokens），动态决定每次委派采用类型化依赖图还是自然语言通信。此外还需要配合图感知的执行器提示（graph-aware executor prompt）来正确解析图语义。

Result: 在四个基准（1050+轨迹）上：τ-retail提升12.7pp且压缩3.2倍（p<0.01），BrowseComp提升8.7pp且压缩2.2倍（p<0.05），BFCL和AppWorld达到持平。无路由器时图通信在AppWorld上回归14.6pp，路由器以近零成本消除了该退化。Oracle分析显示还有8.6pp的提升空间。

Conclusion: 路由式图交接是一种高效的通信策略，既保留了结构化通信的成本优势，又不牺牲自适应推理能力；未来工作可探索执行时自适应路由以进一步挖掘潜力。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems coordinate through natural-language messages that consume 40--60\% of their token budget. Replacing these with structured graphs reduces cost but fails on tasks requiring adaptive reasoning. We propose \textbf{Routed Graph Handoff}, where a lightweight LLM router (155 tokens, 0.15\% overhead) selects between a typed dependency graph and natural language for each delegation. On four benchmarks (1,050+ trajectories), the routed system matches or exceeds NL-only on every task: \textbf{+12.7\,pp} on $τ$-retail at 3.2$\times$ compression ($p{<}0.01$), \textbf{+8.7\,pp} on BrowseComp at 2.2$\times$ compression ($p{<}0.05$), and parity on BFCL and AppWorld. Without the router, graph-only delegation regresses 14.6\,pp on AppWorld; the router eliminates this at near-zero cost. A graph-aware executor prompt is required: the same schema without interpretation guidance yields no gain. An oracle analysis reveals 8.6\,pp of additional headroom, motivating execution-time adaptive routing as future work.

</details>


### [106] [Provenance Before Prose: Claim-Locked Reporting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25336)
*Xiao Fan,Jingyuan Li,Hongbin Guo,Yubo Han,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"claim-locked reporting"（声明锁定报告）协议，将科学报告中的证据性内容（数字、方向、语言强度）在散文生成前固定下来，LLM仅生成连接性散文，从而解决大语言模型在统计报告中漂移数值、反转效应方向、将阈值对比表述为分类效应等可复现性问题。在fMRI功能连接报告和随机对照试验报告两个任务上，该协议相比确定性混合模板分别将可复现性提升37.4和20.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽然能流畅地表述统计证据，但其生成的统计报告仍会出现数值漂移、效应方向反转、将阈值化对比错误表述为分类效应等问题。作者将这些失败框定为控制问题：科学报告中承载证据的内容应由结构化统计结果决定，而非在散文生成过程中被采样。现有控制在文本层或槽位层面操作，即使使用确定性混合模板，因LLM仍决定渲染哪些发现和数字，跨种子的可复现率仅有61.1%。

Method: 提出"claim-locked reporting"协议，这是一种"先溯源后散文"（provenance-before-prose）的方法：在LLM生成任何连接散文之前，先固定每条可报告声明的证据来源、数值、方向及允许的语言强度。该流程保持混合模板的确定性优势，同时消除LLM对"报告哪些内容"的选择自由度，使其仅负责撰写连接性散文。实验在fMRI功能连接报告和Evidence Inference 2.0随机对照试验报告两个场景中进行，并采用跨运行可复现性作为压力测试指标。

Result: 在fMRI功能连接报告和随机对照试验报告上，claim-locked reporting相比混合模板的可复现性分别提升37.4和20.5个百分点。盲审人类审计支持所观察到的方向保持和治理趋势。在基于DeepSeek的fMRI成本分析中，claim-locked reporting取得了最低的token使用量和中位生成延迟。

Conclusion: 将证据性内容固定于散文生成之前能显著提升LLM统计报告的可复现性与治理能力，同时不牺牲甚至改善生成效率。这支持了作者的核心主张：科学报告中的证据承载内容应当绑定结构化统计结果而非在散文生成中被采样。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can fluently verbalize statistical evidence, yet statistical reports can still drift numerical values, invert effect directions, or restate thresholded contrasts as categorical effects. We frame these failures as a control problem: the evidence-bearing content of a scientific report should be fixed by structured statistical results rather than sampled during prose generation. We therefore use cross-run reproducibility to stress-test whether report-visible numbers and claims are bound before prose generation. Existing controls operate at the text or slot level; a deterministic hybrid template reproduces only 61.1% of report-visible numerical content across seeds because the LLM still selects which findings and numbers the template renders. We propose claim-locked reporting, a provenance-before-prose protocol that fixes the evidence source, numbers, direction, and allowed language strength of each reportable claim before the LLM writes only connective prose. Across fMRI functional-connectivity reporting and randomized controlled trial reporting on Evidence Inference 2.0, claim-locked reporting improves reproducibility over the hybrid template by 37.4 and 20.5 points, respectively. Blinded human audits support the observed direction-preservation and governance trends. In an fMRI cost analysis with DeepSeek, claim-locked reporting also yields the lowest observed token use and median generation latency.

</details>


### [107] [GUIDE: Generative Unsupervised Chinese Query Correction via Phonetic and Visual Shared-ID Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25343)
*Lei Yang,Binbin Huang,Jiwei Tan,Xuhui Sui,Chang Tu,Yi Wang,Han Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出GUIDE框架，一种基于"混淆-澄清"范式的无监督中文查询纠错方法，通过共享ID编码音/形易混淆字符并重构原始查询，结合时间衰减的查询频率加权目标，在QSpell 250K和KwaiSearch真实数据集上超越现有基线，在线A/B测试验证了纠错质量和下游互动指标的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中文查询纠错(CQC)对内容平台的搜索和查询推荐至关重要，但监督方法依赖昂贵的标注纠错对且难以随查询词汇演进维护；无监督方法在短查询场景下容易过度纠正歧义输入导致意图漂移，因此需要一种既能约束在合理混淆邻域内纠正又无需标注数据的方法。

Method: GUIDE采用confuse-then-clarify范式：首先将音似或形似的混淆字符用共享ID编码，通过编码器-解码器架构从无标注查询流中重构原始查询，将纠正限制在合理的混淆邻域内；同时引入时间衰减的查询频率加权目标函数，以支持对快速变化查询词汇的适应性。

Result: 在QSpell 250K和大规模真实数据集KwaiSearch上，GUIDE一致超越强基线；在线A/B测试进一步确认了纠错质量和下游用户互动指标的提升。

Conclusion: GUIDE通过混淆共享编码和频率加权的无监督生成框架，在无需标注数据的情况下有效实现中文查询纠错，并能在动态变化的查询词汇环境中保持良好适应性，验证了其在工业场景中的实际价值。

Abstract: Chinese query correction (CQC) is important for search and query recommendation on content platforms, but supervised methods rely on large annotated correction pairs that are costly to maintain as query vocabularies evolve. Unsupervised correction with language models is attractive, yet in the short-query setting, unconstrained generation often over-corrects ambiguous inputs toward high-frequency phrases, causing intent drift. We propose \textsc{GUIDE}, a generative unsupervised framework for CQC based on a confuse-then-clarify paradigm. \textsc{GUIDE} encodes phonetically or visually confusable characters with shared-IDs and reconstructs the original query with an encoder--decoder architecture, which constrains correction to plausible confusion neighborhoods while learning from unlabeled query streams. A time-decayed, query-frequency-weighted objective further supports adaptation to rapidly changing query vocabularies. Experiments on \textit{QSpell 250K} and a large-scale real-world dataset (\textit{KwaiSearch}) show that \textsc{GUIDE} consistently outperforms strong baselines, while online A/B testing further confirms gains in correction quality and downstream engagement.

</details>


### [108] [Short Horizons and Sparse Concepts: a Mathematical View of the Readout in the J-lens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25347)
*Shi-Qi Yan,Kai-Xuan Ding,Chao-Hong Tan,Qian Chen,Wen Wang,Xiangang Li,Zhen-Hua Ling*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对Jacobian透镜（J-lens）提供了严格的数学视角，将其重新定义为从中间激活到未来读出的一阶因果转移算子，揭示了其全局近似行为、偏差以及Jacobian能量分布的稀疏因果几何，并基于理论提出改进与解耦方法以增强中间概念读取能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: J-lens已被提出用于从语言模型中读取可言语化的表征，但其原理和数学含义缺乏详细和理论化的讨论，作者旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 作者将J-lens从一个启发式探针重新诠释为一阶因果转移算子，研究Jacobian矩阵作为下游映射的最优局部线性逼近，分析其全局近似行为与偏差，并进一步分析Jacobian能量分布以揭示其稀疏的因果几何结构，将未来输出的期望分解为短视界预测和稀疏概念预测，并据此提出改进与解耦策略。

Result: 研究发现Jacobian的因果几何高度稀疏：能量随深度衰减、集中在极小比例中，且可分解为对角路径和特定关键位置。基于该理论提出的改进策略和解耦方法显著增强了J-lens读出正确中间概念的能力。

Conclusion: J-lens本质上是关于未来读出的期望算子，其因果结构是稀疏的，通过将未来输出期望分解为短视界与稀疏概念预测，可为J-lens可视化思维过程中的概念提供更直观的归因与解释，并启发有效的改进方法。

Abstract: The Jacobian lens (J-lens) has been proposed as a way to read verbalizable representations from language models. However, its principle and meaning lack a detailed and theoretical discussion. We provide a mathematical view of this interpretation and of its assumed causal structure. Besides treating the J-lens as a heuristic probe, we further regard it as a first-order causal transfer operator from intermediate activations to expected future readouts. We study the Jacobian matrix as the optimal local linear approximation of the downstream mapping, analyze its global approximation behavior and bias, and identify its mathematical meaning as an expectation over anticipated future readouts. Further analysis of the Jacobian energy distribution reveals that its causal geometry is highly sparse. The energy decays with depth, concentrates in an extremely small proportion, and decomposes into diagonal pathways and specific critical positions. This decomposition further resolves the expectation of the J-lens over future outputs into short-horizon and sparse concept predictions, providing a more intuitive attribution and explanation for the ability of the J-lens to visualize concepts during the thinking process. Based on the theory, we propose a simple but effective improvement strategy and decoupling method for the J-lens, which significantly enhances the ability of the J-lens to read out correct intermediate concepts.

</details>


### [109] [Leveraging Speech Acts for Low-Data and Cross-Domain Conversation Derailment Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25359)
*Angela Yifei Yuan,Christine De Kock,Christopher Leckie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出利用言语行为（speech act）作为辅助学习信号，结合文本语义构建语用表达，以提升对话脱轨预测在低数据及跨域场景下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对话脱轨预测方法在数据量有限和跨领域场景下表现不佳，而新平台和小型社区往往缺乏足够的标注数据，亟需一种能够减少词汇噪声、提升泛化能力的方法。

Method: 提出建模对话的语用表示（pragmatic representations），将言语行为信息作为辅助学习信号，与文本语义表示联合训练，以降低词汇层面的干扰并增强模型的跨域泛化能力。

Result: 在三个数据集上的实验表明，该方法在整体性能上有所提升，尤其在低数据和跨域设置下改进更为显著。

Conclusion: 将言语行为作为辅助信号引入对话脱轨预测可有效缓解低数据和跨域场景下的性能退化问题，为资源受限平台提供了可行的建模思路。

Abstract: Conversational derailment forecasting aims to predict when online discussions will escalate into hostility, enabling proactive moderation. Existing approaches often struggle in low-data settings and to generalize across domains. This poses a challenge for new platforms and smaller communities where annotated data is limited. We propose modeling pragmatic representations of conversations to reduce lexical noise and improve generalizability. Specifically, speech act information is used as an auxiliary learning signal alongside textual semantics. Experimental results show improved performance across three datasets, particularly in low-data and cross-domain settings.

</details>


### [110] [Adaptive Triggering for Bias Correction in LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25379)
*Nayoung Kim,Mickey Mancenido,Huan Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将LLM推理过程中的偏见缓解问题形式化为在线变点检测问题，通过CUSUM统计量监控逐步偏见信号，仅在累积证据超过阈值时注入定向纠正，从而解决了"何时干预"这一时序难题，并分别针对开源模型和托管模型设计了白盒与黑盒两种偏见信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 思维链提示会暴露并放大LLM中间推理中的人口统计刻板印象，而仅在最终答案层面去偏无法解决这一失败模式。缓解生成过程中的偏见面临根本性的时序问题：干预过晚会让偏见推理传播，过度干预则会破坏原本正确的推理。现有方法要么事后评估完整推理链，要么在预设步骤干预，缺乏对"推理轨迹何时提供足够证据值得纠正"的决策机制。

Method: 将纠正决策形式化为在线变点检测问题：逐步偏见信号更新CUSUM统计量，仅当累积证据跨越在保留数据上标定的检测器特定阈值时注入定向纠正。框架实例化两种信号：基于下一token概率的白盒信号（适用于开源模型）和基于LLM评判者的黑盒信号（适用于托管模型）。

Result: 在gpt-4o-mini上，自适应黑盒触发恢复了固定间隔干预下丢失的大部分消歧上下文准确性，同时所需干预次数大幅减少，且即使使用独立评判者结论依然成立；在六个开源模型上，白盒信号在所有六个模型上提升了歧义词项准确性，但在五个模型上降低了消歧词项准确性，因为该信号无法区分无根据的刻板印象依赖与正确的、与刻板印象一致的证据。

Conclusion: 自适应触发机制相比固定间隔干预更具效率和有效性，但白盒信号存在固有局限：无法区分合理的刻板印象一致证据与无根据的偏见依赖，这需要更精细的信号设计或混合方法加以解决。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought prompting can expose and amplify demographic stereotypes within an LLM's intermediate reasoning and create a failure mode that final-answer debiasing alone cannot address. Mitigating such bias during generation presents a fundamental timing problem: intervening too late allows biased reasoning to propagate, while unnecessarily intervening can disrupt otherwise correct reasoning. Existing approaches largely avoid this decision by either evaluating completed reasoning chains post hoc or intervening at predetermined steps, leaving open when a developing reasoning trajectory provides sufficient evidence to warrant correction. We formulate this decision as an online change-point detection problem. A per-step bias signal updates a CUSUM statistic and a targeted correction is injected only when accumulated evidence crosses a detector-specific threshold calibrated on held-out data. We instantiate the framework with a white-box signal derived from next-token probabilities and a black-box signal obtained from an LLM judge, enabling deployment with both open-weight and hosted models. On gpt-4o-mini adaptive black-box triggering recovers most of the disambiguated-context accuracy lost under fixed-interval intervention while requiring substantially fewer interventions. That result holds even with an independent judge. Across six open-weight models, the white-box signal improves ambiguous-item accuracy on all six but reduces disambiguated-item accuracy on five because it cannot distinguish unsupported stereotype reliance from correct, stereotype-congruent evidence.

</details>


### [111] [OmniPhys: A Unified Multimodal Benchmark for Physics Understanding and Generation from Chinese Educational Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25398)
*Hao Chen,Yumin Lin,Nadila Yushanjiang,Xin Lin,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了OmniPhys，一个大规模的多模态物理理解与推理基准，包含15,246道题目和19,850张图像，覆盖中学到大学水平的物理问题，不仅支持传统评估，还能系统评估模型生成结构化物理图表的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在多样的视觉和文本推理任务上表现出色，但在物理领域的进展受到缺乏全面基准的严重阻碍。现有基准无法充分覆盖物理学科中复杂的多模态理解和推理需求，因此亟需一个专门用于评估物理领域多模态智能的基准。

Method: 从中国教育语料中构建了覆盖中学至大学水平的OmniPhys基准，包含15,246个问题和19,850张图像，并带有细粒度的标注以支持对推理过程和知识使用的分析。该基准不仅进行常规评估，还系统评估模型在物理领域生成结构化物理图表（即真实物理问题求解的基础组成部分）的能力。

Result: 广泛评估揭示了当前MLLMs在多模态物理理解方面存在关键差距，尤其是在复杂推理和视觉生成方面表现不足。

Conclusion: 作者发布OmniPhys作为一个基础资源，旨在推动多模态智能在物理和科学领域的发展。代码和数据已在GitHub上公开。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated strong abilities in solving diverse visual and textual reasoning tasks. However, their development in the physics domain is significantly hindered by the lack of a comprehensive benchmark. To fill this gap, we introduce OmniPhys, a large-scale benchmark for multimodal physics understanding and reasoning, covering middle school through university-level problems from Chinese Educational Corpora. OmniPhys consists of 15,246 questions and 19,850 images, accompanied by detailed annotations that support fine-grained analysis of reasoning processes and knowledge usage. Beyond conventional evaluation, OmniPhys is a benchmark that systematically evaluates multimodal outputs in the physics domain, including models' ability to generate structured physics diagrams, which constitute a fundamental component of authentic physics problem solving. Extensive evaluations reveal critical gaps in the capabilities of current MLLMs, especially in complex reasoning and visual generation. To address this, we release OmniPhys to serve as a foundational resource for advancing multimodal intelligence in physics and scientific domains. Codes and data are available at https://github.com/ECNU-RAIL/OmniPhys-EMNLP2026.

</details>


### [112] [DCGC: Draft-Conditioned Global Correction for Complex Reasoning with Masked Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25428)
*Minhae Oh,Nakyung Lee,Jungwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DCGC，一种基于掩码扩散模型（MDM）的全局修正框架，利用上游求解器生成的欠佳草稿作为辅助上下文，通过任务特定SFT和动态双CFG推理机制来纠正LLM推理轨迹中的早期错误，在数学、代码和知识推理基准上超越标准采样和简单CFG变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的自回归生成特性会导致早期推理错误在后续步骤中不断传播放大，而纠正这些有缺陷的推理轨迹仍然是一个重大挑战。现有方法缺乏对全局推理轨迹的有效修正能力。

Method: DCGC框架包含两部分：(1) 基于掩码扩散模型（MDM）的架构，将上游求解器生成的不完美草稿作为辅助上下文输入；(2) 推理阶段引入动态双CFG（Dynamic Dual-CFG）机制，分离纯问题分支与问题-草稿联合分支，并通过相对置信度差距来缩放草稿条件残差。

Result: 在数学、代码和知识推理基准上，DCGC优于标准采样和更简单的CFG变体；额外结果表明该方法可迁移至不同扩散骨干网络。在真实测试场景中（无ground-truth失败标签），DCGC通过纠正低一致性上游输出提升了全测试集准确率。

Conclusion: DCGC作为一个无需验证器的全局修正模块，能够有效提升困难推理实例的准确率，为LLM推理错误的纠正提供了新的可行范式。

Abstract: Correcting flawed reasoning traces remains a significant challenge for Large Language Models (LLMs), whose autoregressive generation can propagate early mistakes into subsequent reasoning. We introduce DCGC, a Masked Diffusion Model (MDM) framework for global correction that uses an imperfect solution draft from an upstream solver as auxiliary context. DCGC combines task-specific Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) with a novel inference-time mechanism called Dynamic Dual-CFG. This mechanism separates problem-only and joint problem-draft branches and scales the draft-conditioned residual using a relative confidence gap. Across math, code, and knowledge reasoning benchmarks, DCGC outperforms standard sampling and simpler CFG variants, with additional results suggesting transfer to different diffusion backbones. In test-time setting where ground-truth failure labels are unavailable, DCGC improves full test set accuracy by correcting low-consensus upstream outputs, highlighting its utility as a verifier-free global correction module for difficult reasoning instances.

</details>


### [113] [MathAdv: What Theorem Provers Know, Reason, Formalize, and Generalize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25449)
*Jiaxin Yuan,Connor Martinez Lockhart,Xiaoyu Liu,Jiaqi Wang,Chenghao Deng,Xiayimei Han,Vlasios Mastrantonis,Dmitrii Gudin,Shaopeng Zhu,Abdirisak Abdullahi Mohamed,Bilal Hamdi Aytekin,Jiewen Lang,Zezheng Song,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MathAdv诊断基准，涵盖13个数学领域，在Lean 4定理证明基础上增加三种辅助任务，用于组件级评估定理证明模型，揭示了形式化瓶颈、领域差异、自然语言引导的双面性以及鲁棒性不足等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有定理证明基准过度关注总体证明准确率，数学覆盖范围狭窄，且缺乏对等价改写鲁棒性的评估，无法全面诊断模型的能力与失败模式。

Method: 构建跨13个领域（本科至研究生水平）的MathAdv基准，在Lean 4定理证明任务之外设计三种辅助任务：多选题（探测数学知识）、填空题（隔离非形式化推理）、专家构造的变换（测试对问题表述的鲁棒性）。

Result: 评估当代定理证明器得出四项发现：形式化是主要瓶颈；性能在不同数学领域间差异显著；自然语言提示帮助通用LLM但可能阻碍专用证明模型；数学等价改写暴露了显著的鲁棒性缺陷。

Conclusion: 组件级评估能揭示总体定理证明准确率所掩盖的模型能力与失败模式，为定理证明模型诊断提供更细粒度的视角。

Abstract: Formal theorem proving enables machine-verifiable evaluation of mathematical reasoning, yet existing benchmarks often emphasize aggregate proof accuracy, concentrate on a narrow range of mathematics, and provide limited evidence of robustness to equivalent reformulations. We introduce MathAdv, a diagnostic benchmark spanning 13 domains across undergraduate- and graduate-level mathematics. Alongside Lean 4 theorem proving, MathAdv provides up to three auxiliary tasks: multiple-choice questions that probe mathematical knowledge, fill-in-the-blank problems that isolate informal reasoning, and expert-crafted transformations that test robustness to problem presentation. Our evaluation of contemporary theorem provers yields four findings: formalization remains a major bottleneck; performance varies substantially across mathematical domains; natural-language guidance helps general-purpose LLMs but can hinder proof-specialized models; and mathematically equivalent reformulations expose substantial robustness limitations. Together, these results show how component-wise evaluation can reveal model capabilities and failure modes that aggregate theorem-proving accuracy obscures. The dataset and evaluation scripts are available at https://github.com/margotyjx/MathAdv.git.

</details>


### [114] [VietAIDetector: An Open-Source Zero-Shot Detector for Vietnamese AI-Generated Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25478)
*Trieu Hai Nguyen,Van-Dung Hoang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了VietAIDetector，一个专门用于检测越南语AI生成文本的开源工具，基于Zero-Shot方法并构建在越南语特定语言模型之上，通过Gradio网页界面支持多种文本输入格式，在跨域数据集上表现优于主要为英语设计的现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来区分AI生成文本与人类撰写文本仍然是一个挑战，而现有检测方法主要针对英语开发，缺乏专门针对越南语的AI文本检测工具。

Method: 该工具采用基于Zero-Shot的方法检测AI生成文本，无需领域特定的训练数据，建立在先前VietBinoculars和Binoculars研究基础上，并使用越南语特定的语言模型作为核心组件。工具通过Gradio网页界面支持原始越南语文本、常见文本文件格式、扫描文档以及超长文本的输入，并允许用户根据F1分数、准确率或TPR@0.05FPR需求选择最优检测阈值。

Result: 该工具在跨域数据集上进行了评估，展现出优于主要为英语开发的现有方法的检测性能。

Conclusion: VietAIDetector提供了一个公开可用的、针对越南语优化的AI文本检测解决方案，结果通过网页界面展示，支持用户审查可疑文本并可下载为PDF报告，工具已公开发布在GitHub上。

Abstract: In recent years, distinguishing between AI-generated text and human-written text has remained a challenge. In this paper, we introduce VietAIDetector, an open-source tool designed specifically for detecting Vietnamese AI-generated text. It allows users to interact through a Gradio web interface with inputs ranging from raw Vietnamese text to common text file formats, including scanned documents and exceptionally long texts that exceed the context size of the employed Large Language Models (LLMs). The core component of the tool employs a Zero-Shot approach to detect AI-generated text without requiring domain-specific training data, building upon the previous VietBinoculars and Binoculars research. The tool is built upon a Vietnamese-specific language model and has been evaluated on out-of-domain datasets, demonstrating superior performance compared to existing methods primarily developed for English. Additionally, users can select optimal detection thresholds based on F1 score, accuracy, or TPR@0.05FPR requirements. The results are presented through the web interface, allowing users to easily review and verify suspicious texts or download them as a PDF report. The tool is publicly available at https://github.com/trieuntu/VietAIDetector

</details>


### [115] [ReliableRAG: Combating Misinformation in Retrieval-Augmented Generation via Reliability-Guided Reasoning Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25487)
*Jinpu Jiang,Xuan Wu,Wenhao Song,Bo Yang,You Zhou,Hongwei Ge,Heow Pueh Lee,Yanchun Liang,Chunguo Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ReliableRAG，这是首个通过细粒度三元组级可靠性评估来缓解多跳问答中欺骗性错误信息影响的框架，在三组多跳QA数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统在多跳问答中容易受到检索文档中欺骗性错误信息（语义与问题相关但事实错误）的误导。现有方法依赖隐式对齐或显式约束，但缺乏细粒度的信息可靠性评估能力，难以抵御此类误导。

Method: ReliableRAG包含三个步骤：(1) 从源文档中提取信息片段并表示为结构化三元组；(2) 通过结合查询-三元组语义相关性与三元组可信度来量化可靠性，仅保留top-K可靠且不冗余的三元组；(3) 基于筛选后的三元组自回归地构建稳健推理链，整合可信证据并过滤错误信息，生成忠实于可靠信息的准确答案。

Result: 在三个多跳QA数据集上的实验表明，ReliableRAG在欺骗性错误信息注入场景下优于现有方法，显著提升了RAG系统的事实可靠性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 细粒度的三元组级可靠性评估能够有效识别和过滤欺骗性错误信息，从而增强多跳QA中RAG系统的可靠性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has emerged as a powerful architecture for Question Answering (QA) by integrating external information into Large Language Models (LLMs). However, false, inaccurate, and misleading information in news and social media poses a serious challenge to real-world RAG systems, especially in multi-hop QA, where complex multi-step reasoning can be misled by even a single deceptive misinformation segment in the retrieved documents. Existing approaches mainly rely on implicit alignment or explicit regulation, but their limited ability to assess fine-grained information reliability makes them vulnerable to deceptive misinformation that is semantically relevant to the question yet factually incorrect, leading to erroneous answers. To address this limitation, we propose ReliableRAG, which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first reliability-driven framework that mitigates deceptive misinformation in multi-hop QA through fine-grained evaluation of individual triples. ReliableRAG first extracts information segments from source documents and represents them as structured triples. It then quantifies triple reliability by combining query-triple semantic relevance with triple credibility, retaining only the top-$K$ reliable and non-redundant triples. Based on these refined triples, ReliableRAG autoregressively constructs robust reasoning chains to consolidate trustworthy evidence and filter deceptive misinformation, producing accurate answers faithful to reliable information. Experiments on three multi-hop QA datasets show that ReliableRAG outperforms existing methods, substantially improving the factual reliability and robustness of RAG systems under deceptive misinformation injection.

</details>


### [116] [TOPAS: Workflow-Aware Prefix-State Scheduling for Multi-Agent LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25523)
*Hongqiu Ni,Han Tian,Chi Zhang,Guopeng Li,Haisheng Tan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出TOPAS调度器，在多智能体LLM服务中联合决策前缀缓存保留与请求调度，平衡前缀局部性与工作流进度，在任务级JCT上显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前缀缓存在多智能体LLM推理中引入权衡：保留长系统提示词的KV缓存可加速后续调用，但占用GPU内存降低并发批处理能力。现有调度器在多阶段工作流中要么优先前缀局部性，要么优先整体工作流进度，在共享KV缓存预算下孤立优化任一目标会因下游延迟或频繁前缀替换而延长任务级JCT。

Method: TOPAS（Task-Oriented Prefix-Aware Scheduler）联合决定保留哪些智能体前缀在缓存中及调度哪些请求执行。其通过评分候选决策后状态，权衡每个任务最长剩余服务路径的预期缩减与下游前缀重用的近期收益，并考虑前缀移动和抢占的成本；同时引入任务级老化机制防止饥饿。实现于SGLang框架中。

Result: 在三个合成DAG和两个MetaGPT软件开发工作流上评估：与每个工作负载和指标的最佳基线相比，合成工作负载上平均/99百分位JCT降低最多39.8%/49.4%；MetaGPT-SOP上平均JCT降低9.8%；MetaGPT-TL上平均/99百分位JCT降低22.0%/26.6%。

Conclusion: 通过联合优化前缀缓存保留和请求调度，TOPAS能够有效平衡前缀重用与工作流进展之间的矛盾，显著减少多智能体LLM服务工作负载的任务级完成时间。

Abstract: Prefix caching introduces a fundamental tradeoff in multi-agent large language model (LLM) serving: retaining a long system-prompt key-value (KV) cache for an agent accelerates future calls, yet it reduces the GPU memory available for batching concurrent requests. In multi-stage workflows, existing schedulers tend to prioritize either immediate prefix locality or overall workflow progress. However, under a shared KV cache budget, optimizing either objective in isolation can prolong tasklevel job completion time (JCT) through downstream delays or frequent prefix replacement. To strike a balance, we here propose TOPAS, a Task-Oriented Prefix-Aware Scheduler that jointly decides which agent prefixes to keep in the cache and which requests to schedule for execution. TOPAS scores candidate post-decision states by trading off the expected reduction in each task's longest remaining service path against the near-term benefit of downstream prefix reuse, accounting for the costs of prefix movement and preemption. A task-level aging mechanism is also incorporated to prevent starvation. We implement TOPAS within the SGLang framework and assess its performance on three synthetic DAGs and two MetaGPT software-development workflows. Compared with the best performing baseline for each workload and metric, TOPAS reduces the mean/p99 JCT by up to 39.8%/49.4% on the synthetic workloads, while lowering mean JCT by 9.8% on MetaGPT-SOP and mean/p99 JCT by 22.0%/26.6% on MetaGPT-TL.

</details>


### [117] [ClueWeaver: Reward-Guided Dual-Agent Evidence Reasoning for Compact LLMs on Literary Long Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25531)
*Jihao Zhu,Zhiwei Yang,Wenxiao Zhang,Junqian Zhao,Qi You,Fangqi Wang,Zheyuan Deng,Hanzhe Yang,Yu Liu,Jin B. Hong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ClueWeaver是一个面向长叙事问答的证据感知双智能体框架，通过Finder和Interpreter两个组件的分工协作，在紧凑型本地语言模型上实现比端到端提示更优的问答性能，同时提供可检查的证据选择和带段落引用的推理轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人文社科研究需要精读长叙事材料（如小说、剧本、档案等），但许多用户难以获得昂贵的商业长上下文模型。紧凑、可本地部署的语言模型虽为实用替代方案，但直接将整个长上下文输入模型不仅成本高、难以检查，还容易遗漏稀疏的关键证据。

Method: ClueWeaver采用双智能体框架：Finder通过检索引导的分段方式识别包含答案关键线索的段落；Interpreter从所选证据中推导答案，生成带段落ID引用的推理依据，并对高风险问题执行内部自校准。两个智能体均通过奖励引导的强化学习优化——Finder的奖励强调证据保留和忠实的段落ID引用，Interpreter的奖励强调正确性、依据性和简洁解释。这种分解使证据选择和推理过程比端到端提示更具可检查性。

Result: 在多个长上下文叙事问答和声明验证场景的实验表明，ClueWeaver显著提升了本地端到端语言模型的性能，同时提供了证据覆盖和段落引用的推理轨迹。

Conclusion: 证据-推理分解的双智能体架构是紧凑型本地模型处理长叙事问答的有效范式，兼顾了性能提升和推理过程的可检查性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Humanities and social science research requires close reading of long narrative materials such as novels, scripts, archives, and case reports, yet many users have limited access to costly proprietary long-context models. Compact, locally deployable language models are a practical alternative, but directly feeding them an entire long context remains costly, hard to inspect, and prone to missing sparse evidence. We present ClueWeaver, an evidence-aware dual-agent framework for long-narrative question answering with compact local models. A Finder identifies passages containing answer-critical clues through retrieval-guided segmentation, while an Interpreter derives the answer from the selected evidence, produces rationales with paragraph-ID citations, and applies an internal self-calibration pass for high-risk questions. Both agents are optimized with reward-guided reinforcement learning: Finder rewards emphasize evidence retention and faithful paragraph-ID references, and Interpreter rewards emphasize correctness, grounding, and concise explanations. This decomposition makes evidence selection and reasoning more inspectable than end-to-end prompting. Experiments across multiple long-context narrative question answering and claim verification settings show that ClueWeaver substantially improves local end-to-end language models while providing evidence coverage and paragraph-referenced reasoning traces. Code is available at https://github.com/Ameame1/ClueWeaver.

</details>


### [118] [Virgil: Navigating Explainability for Transformer-based Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25555)
*Martino Ciaperoni,Sezer Kutluk,Benedetta Muscato,Marta Marchiori Manerba,Fosca Giannotti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Virgil是一个交互式系统，通过统一的界面和知识库帮助实践者和研究者（包括非专家）发现、比较和导航用于transformer语言模型的可解释性工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着transformer语言模型在高风险应用中的部署，可解释性变得至关重要。但可解释性工具生态正在快速演变，变得更加丰富同时也更加碎片化，难以导航。作者希望解决这一"工具过载"问题。

Method: 构建一个名为Virgil的交互式系统，基于精选的知识库（curated knowledge base），在统一界面中使用户能够发现（discover）和比较（compare）transformer语言模型的可解释性工具，面向包括非专家在内的实践者和研究者。

Result: 该系统通过统一界面降低了对可解释性工具进行探索和比较的认知门槛，使非专家用户也能浏览该工具生态。

Conclusion: Virgil提供了一种系统化的方式来导航日益碎片化的transformer语言模型可解释性工具生态，通过精心策划的知识库和统一交互界面，降低了该领域的入门门槛。

Abstract: Explainability for transformer-based language models is becoming crucial as these systems are deployed in high-stakes applications. As a result, the ecosystem of explainability tools is rapidly evolving, becoming richer, but also more fragmented and harder to navigate. To address this challenge, we present Virgil, an interactive system that lets practitioners and researchers, including non-experts, navigate explainability tools for transformer language models. Supported by a curated knowledge base, the system enables users to discover and compare explainability tools within a unified interface.

</details>


### [119] [EgoArgus: Benchmarking VLMs as Situational Assistants for Modality-Grounded User Supports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25561)
*Yu-Chien Tang,Yu-Hsiang Liu,An-Zi Yen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出EgoArgus，一个用于评估以自我为中心（第一人称）的视觉语言模型（VLM）助手在日常对话中协调视觉证据与用户语言信息能力的人标注数据集，涵盖五种对话视频日常场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有以自我为中心的基准主要孤立评估视觉理解能力，未检验模型在视觉证据与用户提供的语言信息之间存在帮助、不相关或冲突时，能否正确仲裁两者并决定是否干预，而这是VLM作为日常助手的关键能力。

Method: 构建EgoArgus数据集，包含五种对话-视频日常场景的人标注数据，用于评估VLM在理解与决策任务中协调多模态信息的能力，并通过实验测试当前VLM的表现及现有模态偏置缓解方法的有效性。

Result: 当前VLM作为可靠的以自我为中心的助手仍具挑战性，尤其在需要识别哪种模态可信以及决定何时应该干预的任务上表现不佳；现有模态偏置缓解方法对性能提升作用相当有限。

Conclusion: EgoArgus揭示了VLM在日常助手的多模态协调与决策能力上的不足，并为从业人员部署VLM作为日常助手提供了相关洞察。

Abstract: VLMs are increasingly positioned as daily assistants that perceive first-person environments, follow user dialogue, and decide how to help. Existing egocentric benchmarks mainly evaluate visual understanding in isolation, leaving open whether models can arbitrate between visual evidence and user-provided language when the two are helpful, irrelevant, or conflicting. We introduce EgoArgus, a human-annotated dataset for evaluating egocentric assistants on understanding and decision tasks in five dialogue-video daily scenarios. Our results demonstrate that it is still challenging for current VLMs as reliable egocentric assistants, which requires identifying which modality is trustworthy and deciding when intervention is warranted. Deeper analysis also shows that existing modality bias mitigation methods are quite restricted to enhance performance, providing insights to aid practioners into the deployment of current VLMs as daily assistants.

</details>


### [120] [Controllable Affective Generation via Latent Vector Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25569)
*Xixian Yong,Siyuan Chang,Yingying Zhang,Xian Wu,Xiao Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EmoVec提出了一种轻量级框架，通过对比激活加技术在推理时向残差流注入情感特定方向向量，实现对LLM生成情绪强度的连续可控调节，无需更新模型权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型经过对齐后往往产生情感扁平化的输出，限制了其在情感敏感应用中的效果，需要一种不修改模型权重的可控情感生成方法。

Method: EmoVec从配对的中性响应和情感条件响应中通过对比激活加技术提取情感特定方向，并通过任务特定去偏和主子空间移除进行精炼。推理时将向量以静态或场景自适应缩放方式注入最终残差流，实现情感强度的连续控制。

Result: 在三个LLM和八种情感上的实验表明，EmoVec能一致提升情感显著性，同时基本保持语义内容、流畅性和连贯性。消融研究和人工评估进一步验证了向量纯化和自适应缩放的有效性。

Conclusion: EmoVec被证实是部署LLM中进行情感控制的实用推理时方法，能在不更新模型权重的情况下实现有效的情感调节。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often produce emotionally flattened responses after alignment, limiting their effectiveness in affect-sensitive applications. In this paper, we propose EmoVec, a lightweight framework for controllable affective generation via latent vector steering. EmoVec extracts emotion-specific directions from paired neutral and emotion-conditioned responses using contrastive activation addition, and further refines them through task-specific debiasing and principal subspace removal. During inference, these vectors are injected into the final residual stream with static or scenario-adaptive scaling, enabling continuous control over emotional intensity without updating model weights. Experiments across three LLMs and eight emotions show that EmoVec consistently improves emotional salience while largely preserving semantic content, fluency, and coherence. Ablation studies and human evaluation further confirm the effectiveness of vector purification and adaptive scaling, establishing EmoVec as a practical inference-time method for affective control in deployed LLMs.

</details>


### [121] [Generative vs. Encoder Large Language Models for ASR Evaluation: A Comparative Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25574)
*Thibault Bañeras-Roux,Shashi Kumar,Driss Khalil,Sergio Burdisso,Petr Motlicek,Shiran Liu,Mickael Rouvier,Jane Wottawa,Richard Dufour*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文比较了基于编码器和解码器的大语言模型在自动语音识别（ASR）评估中的表现，重点分析BERTScore和SemDist两种嵌入度量在不同LLM架构、层和池化策略下的效果，同时探索生成式LLM在假设选择和错误分类中的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的词错误率（WER）无法良好地反映语义相似性，而基于嵌入的度量虽然与人类判断的相关性更好，但编码器和解码器LLM在ASR评估中各自的作用尚未被充分探索。

Method: 论文进行了两方面比较研究：(1) 在编码器模型中评估BERTScore和SemDist在不同LLM、层和池化策略下的表现；(2) 在解码器模型中测试生成式LLM的两种应用场景：通过提示进行的成对假设选择，以及直接的定性错误分类。

Result: 两种嵌入度量在适当配置下均能与人类判断实现强相关性；基于编码器的度量仍具高度竞争力；生成式LLM在假设比较中表现强劲，并提升了ASR评估的可解释性。

Conclusion: 编码器与解码器LLM在ASR评估中各有优势：编码器度量保持竞争力，生成式LLM则擅长假设比较并增强评估的可解释性，两者互补。

Abstract: Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) is typically evaluated using Word Error Rate (WER), which poorly reflects semantic similarity. While embedding-based metrics correlate better with human judgments, the respective roles of encoder and decoder-based Large Language Models (LLMs) remain underexplored. This paper presents a comparative study of both families for ASR evaluation. We analyze BERTScore and SemDist across different LLMs, layers, and pooling strategies, showing that both metrics can achieve strong correlation with human judgments when properly configured. For decoder models, we investigate generative LLMs in two settings: pairwise hypothesis selection via prompting and direct qualitative error classification. Our results show that encoder-based metrics remain highly competitive, while generative LLMs perform strongly in hypothesis comparison and improve the interpretability of ASR evaluation.

</details>


### [122] [Cross-Dataset Stability of Expert-Informed Skill Prompting and Fine-Tuning for Chinese Metaphor Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25579)
*Yufeng Wu,Meichun Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究比较了四种方法（BERT微调、QLoRA大语言模型微调、直接零样本提示、带程序化Skill的零样本提示）在中英文句子级隐喻识别中跨数据集的表现，发现专家信息驱动的Skill提示方法在跨数据集性能稳定性上表现最佳（范围仅4.08点），而微调方法在原生测试集上准确率最高（BERT-FT达91.76 Macro-F1）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐喻识别性能在不同文本分布和标注策略的数据集之间差异显著，前人工作主要关注任务特定的参数适应以获得最优准确率，但忽略了跨数据集性能的稳定性。本文探究一个固定专家信息的程序化方法能否比任务特定的参数适应产生更均匀的跨数据集表现。

Method: 在中文句子级隐喻识别任务上比较四种预设条件：BERT微调（BERT-FT）、基于QLoRA的大语言模型微调（LLM-FT）、直接零样本大语言模型提示（LLM-ZS）、带冻结程序化Skill的零样本提示（Skill-ZS）。Skill将语境意义、基本意义、对比和比较等已确立的隐喻判断标准操作化。评估使用CMRE Test（原生测试集）及两个外部数据集CCIME和CMC，微调取三颗种子均值，零样本采用确定性配置。

Result: 微调在原生测试集上最强，BERT-FT达91.76 Macro-F1；LLM-FT在外部数据平均分最高（83.52），Skill-ZS紧随其后（82.92）且拥有最高的外部最低分（82.64）和最小的跨数据集范围（4.08点）。在匹配的零样本对比中，加入Skill在所有数据集上减少了隐喻预测，显著降低CCIME的假阳性但增加了CMRE Test和CMC的假阴性。

Conclusion: 专家信息的Skill提示可作为实现更均匀跨数据集性能的补充路径，而微调方法在原生数据准确率上仍具优势。这是首个在同一跨数据集评估中将专家信息程序化Skill与任务特定微调进行比较的中文句子级隐喻识别研究。

Abstract: Metaphor-identification performance can change markedly across datasets that differ in text distribution and annotation policy. We examine whether a fixed expert-informed procedure produces a more even cross-dataset profile than task-specific parameter adaptation. Four prespecified conditions are compared for Chinese sentence-level metaphor identification: BERT fine-tuning (BERT-FT), QLoRA-based large language model fine-tuning (LLM-FT), direct zero-shot LLM prompting (LLM-ZS), and zero-shot prompting with a frozen procedural Skill (Skill-ZS). The Skill operationalizes established criteria involving contextual meaning, basic meaning, contrast, and comparison. Evaluation covers CMRE Test and two external datasets, CCIME and CMC. Fine-tuned scores are means over three seeds, whereas each zero-shot score comes from one deterministic configuration. Fine-tuning remains strongest on the native test set: BERT-FT reaches 91.76 Macro-F1. LLM-FT has the highest external mean (83.52), while Skill-ZS is close at 82.92 and has both the highest external floor (82.64) and the smallest observed range across all three datasets (4.08 points). In the matched zero-shot comparison, adding the Skill reduces metaphorical predictions on every dataset. This sharply lowers false positives on CCIME but increases false negatives on CMRE Test and CMC. The results position expert-informed Skill prompting as a complementary route to more even observed cross-dataset performance, while fine-tuning retains its advantage in native-data accuracy. To our knowledge, this is the first study to compare an expert-informed procedural Skill with task-specific fine-tuning in the same cross-dataset evaluation of Chinese sentence-level metaphor identification.

</details>


### [123] [GRIP: Granular Reward-Guided Parameter Interpolation for Efficient Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25583)
*Lam So,Canhui Wu,Han Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出GRIP框架，通过奖励引导的参数插值方法，在推理模型和指令微调模型之间进行模块级融合，在不进行完整模型重训练的情况下实现高效推理，达到准确率与效率的更好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理型大语言模型通过生成冗长的思维链获得强推理能力，但推理成本和延迟显著增加；而指令微调模型回答简洁但推理能力不足。这种准确率与效率之间的不匹配促使作者探索一种无需完整模型重训练的轻量级方法，结合两者的优势。

Method: GRIP框架包括：(1) 针对具有相同架构的推理模型和指令模型，为每个模块分配可学习的插值比例；(2) 仅优化插值比例参数，保持两个源模型完全冻结；(3) 使用奖励信号训练插值比例，奖励信号偏好既正确又简洁的回答。

Result: 实验表明，GRIP在准确率-效率权衡上优于固定插值或基于搜索的模型合并基线，并揭示了与高效推理相关的模块级融合模式。

Conclusion: 通过模块级奖励引导的参数插值，可以在不大幅增加训练成本的前提下，实现推理准确率与推理效率的更优权衡，并提供了对高效推理模块融合模式的深入理解。

Abstract: Reasoning-oriented large language models often achieve strong problem-solving performance by generating long chains of thought, but this behavior substantially increases inference cost and latency. In contrast, instruction-tuned models tend to answer more concisely, yet often lack comparable reasoning ability. This accuracy-efficiency mismatch motivates a lightweight approach that combines the strengths of both models without full model retraining. In this paper, we propose GRIP (Granular Reward-guided Interpolation of Parameters), a reward-guided parameter interpolation framework for efficient reasoning. Given a reasoning model and an instruction model with identical architectures, GRIP assigns learnable interpolation ratios to individual modules and optimizes only these ratios while keeping both source models frozen. The interpolation ratios are trained with a reward signal that favors responses that are both correct and concise. Experiments show that GRIP achieves a better accuracy-efficiency trade-off than fixed or search-based merging baselines and further reveals module-wise fusion patterns associated with efficient reasoning.

</details>


### [124] [JIT-Agent: Scaling Harness Intelligence via Just-in-Time Harness Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25593)
*Guibin Zhang,Leo Lu,Fangzhou Xie,Kang Zhu,Junhao Wang,Zhifei Xie,Zhaochen Yu,Zihang Liu,Zhongxiang Sun,Qiankun Li,Yue Liao,Heng Chang,Xiaobin Hu,Qibing Ren,Wangchunshu Zhou,Shuicheng Yan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: JIT-Agent是首个专为即时智能体框架（harness）生成而训练的模型，能够在运行时为任意现成的智能体大语言模型合成任务自适应的框架，将框架智能确立为一个可训练、可迁移、可累积的智能体能力维度，与模型扩展正交。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体能力并非仅由底层模型决定，智能体框架（包括记忆管理、规划策略、动作协议和工具/技能编排）往往对最终性能的贡献超过基础模型本身。然而框架设计目前依赖手动、任务特定且不可扩展的方式，缺乏自动化的框架生成方案。

Method: 将智能体框架形式化为遵循固定四模块协议的可组合、可机器生成的工件；训练JIT-Agent实现三项能力：为给定任务定制框架、修复框架以保证稳定可靠执行、通过从历史框架配置档案中提取性能信号进行自我进化。

Result: 配备JIT-Agent后，DeepSeek-V4-Flash在DeepSearchQA上超过GPT-5.6达+9.1分、在OdysseyBench上超过+4.3分；GLM-5.2提升高达+20.2分。JIT-Agent生成的框架在性能上与OpenCode和Claude Code等成熟智能体运行时竞争，并在DeepSeek V4、Mimo-V2.5和Qwen3.6等多尺度模型家族上持续改进。

Conclusion: JIT-Agent是首个专为即时框架生成而构建的模型，证明框架智能是独立于模型规模的可训练、可迁移且可累积的智能体能力维度，为智能体系统的可扩展设计开辟了新的方向。

Abstract: Agent capability is not determined by the model alone. The agent harness, encompassing memory management, planning strategy, action protocol, and tool/skill orchestration, can dominate the contribution of the underlying foundation model. Yet harness design remains manual, task-specific, and fundamentally unscalable. We present JIT-Agent, a harness intelligence model trained to synthesize task-adaptive agent harnesses on the fly for arbitrary off-the-shelf agentic LLMs. We formalize the agent harness as a composable, machine-generatable artifact governed by a fixed four-module protocol, and train JIT-Agent to customize harnesses for a given task at hand, repair harnesses for stable and reliable execution, and self-evolve by distilling performance signals from an expanding archive of prior harness configurations. Equipped with JIT-Agent as a harness helper, DeepSeek-V4-Flash surpasses GPT-5.6 on DeepSearchQA (+9.1) and OdysseyBench (+4.3), while the already strong GLM-5.2 gains up to +20.2 points. Across controlled evaluations, JIT-Agent-generated harnesses are performance-competitive with mature agent runtimes such as OpenCode and Claude Code and consistently improve multi-scale model families of DeepSeek V4, Mimo-V2.5, and Qwen3.6. To our knowledge, JIT-Agent is the first model purpose-built for just-in-time harness generation, establishing harness intelligence as a trainable, transferable, and compounding dimension of agent capability orthogonal to model scaling.

</details>


### [125] [From Specialization to Generalization: Instruction-tuned LLMs for Robust Harmful Content Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25605)
*Lukas Edman,Daryna Dementieva,Alexander Fraser*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过指令微调的方式，将基于Qwen3的通用大语言模型在统一整合的36个英文仇恨言论数据集上进行专门微调，实现了在领域内基准上的最先进性能，并在跨领域和跨语言泛化上取得显著提升，挑战了此前LLM在仇恨言论检测上不及编码器模型的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究显示提示式LLM在仇恨言论检测上相比BERT等编码器模型仅有边际提升，LLM在此敏感领域的有效性存疑。作者希望重新审视这一问题，探索通过指令微调是否能让LLM在该任务上实现突破。

Method: 统一整合36个涵盖多种标注方案的英文仇恨言论数据集，基于Qwen3构建通用大语言模型，通过指令微调（instruction tuning）专门针对仇恨言论缓解任务进行微调。

Result: 在领域内基准上取得了最先进（SOTA）的性能，同时在跨领域和跨语言泛化方面实现了大幅提升，而这些恰恰是编码器专家分类器常常表现不佳的领域。

Conclusion: 通过指令微调，LLM能够克服此前在仇恨言论检测中表现不佳的局限，在领域内、跨领域及跨语言场景中均展现出优于编码器模型的能力，说明LLM在该敏感任务上的潜力尚未被充分挖掘。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate impressive performance across a wide range of general NLP tasks; however, their effectiveness in sensitive domains, such as hate speech detection, remains less clear. Prior studies comparing prompted LLMs with state-of-the-art encoder-based models (e.g., BERT variants (Roy et al., 2023; Dönmez et al., 2024)) have shown only marginal gains, suggesting that LLMs may not excel in hate speech detection or mitigation. In this work, we revisit this question through the lens of instruction tuning. By thoroughly unifying 36 English hate speech datasets spanning multiple labeling schemes, we fine-tune a generalist LLM, based on Qwen3 (Qwen Team, 2025), specifically for hate speech mitigation. Our results demonstrate not only state-of-the-art performance on in-domain benchmarks but also substantial improvements in cross-domain and cross-lingual generalization--areas where encoder-based specialist classifiers often struggle.

</details>


### [126] [AWM: Answerable Working Memory for Long-Document VQA Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25618)
*Dongzhuoran Zhou,Yuqicheng Zhu,Yule Liu,Zhen Yang,Rui Lu,Yuxiao Dong,Jie Tang,Evgeny Kharlamov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"仅记忆可答性"（memory-only answerability）诊断指标，用于评估长文档VQA智能体工作记忆的质量，并在此基础上提出Answerable Working Memory (AWM)及AWM-GRPO训练方法，将工作记忆的可答性信号纳入GRPO奖励中，在保持最终答案优先的同时提升记忆质量。实验表明该方法在MMLongBench-Doc和LongDocURL上分别比RAG基线提升8.1和11.9个点的最终答案准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长文档VQA评估主要关注最终答案正确性和证据页面访问情况，忽略了工作记忆本身的质量。智能体可能访问了正确页面并给出正确答案，但其写入工作记忆的内容过于笼统或不完整，一旦移除页面上下文就无法支撑后续回答，存在"记忆质量盲区"。

Method: 1) 提出memory-only answerability诊断：仅从问题和终端工作记忆出发，判断读者能否回答问题。2) 基于该诊断构建AWM（Answerable Working Memory），将终端工作记忆视为可回答的证据工件。3) 提出AWM-GRPO，将该信号融入GRPO奖励中，为"答案正确且终端工作记忆可回答"的轨迹分配更高优势，同时保持最终答案优先级。

Result: 在MMLongBench-Doc上，即使提供了黄金证据页面，仍有42.5%的正确答案无法仅从终端工作记忆中回答。AWM-GRPO在MMLongBench-Doc和LongDocURL上分别比RAG基线提升8.1和11.9个点的最终答案准确率，并将"记忆缺失但答案正确"（memory-missing-correct）的比例比仅用答案奖励的GRPO降低2.7个点。

Conclusion: 工作记忆的可答性是长文档VQA中一个关键但此前被忽视的维度。通过将记忆质量信号纳入强化学习奖励，可以同时提升最终答案准确性和记忆的支撑能力，弥补现有评估框架中的盲区。

Abstract: Long-document visual question answering increasingly relies on VLM agents that retrieve candidate pages, inspect page images, write findings to working memory, and synthesize answers. Working memory should carry answer-supporting evidence across page inspections for later grounded answering, yet existing evaluation mainly checks final-answer correctness and evidence-page access. This creates a memory-quality blind spot: an agent may reach the right page and answer correctly while leaving behind memory too generic or incomplete to support answering once page context is removed. We introduce \emph{memory-only answerability}, a diagnostic that asks whether a reader can answer from the question and terminal working memory alone. Building on this diagnostic, \emph{Answerable Working Memory} (AWM) treats terminal working memory as an answerable evidence artifact, and AWM-GRPO incorporates this signal into the GRPO reward while preserving final-answer priority. Under GRPO, this reward assigns higher advantages to answer-correct trajectories whose terminal working memory remains answerable. On \textsc{MMLongBench-Doc}, even when gold evidence pages are provided, 42.5\% of correct answers still cannot be answered from terminal working memory alone. AWM-GRPO improves final-answer accuracy over the RAG baseline by 8.1 and 11.9 points on \textsc{MMLongBench-Doc} and \textsc{LongDocURL} and reduces the memory-missing-correct rate by 2.7 points over answer-only GRPO.

</details>


### [127] [AutoVerifier: Residual-Guided Non-Parametric Optimization for Reference-Based Answer Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25637)
*Zebei Zhao,Zhihao Shi,Minqi Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoVerifier，一种基于残差引导的非参数优化方法，用于从验证器反复出现的错误中学习隐式归纳偏置（如答案等价性判断），通过规则卡片记录、回放验证后提升为代码模块或提示指导，在四个验证器基准上大幅超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参考型验证器在处理不同答案形式（如$1+3.14$ vs $1+\pi$）的等价性判断时，其正确性依赖于问题和评分标准，这种隐式假设（验证器归纳偏置）难以被显式建模，导致验证准确性受限。

Method: AutoVerifier采用残差引导的非参数优化方法：从验证器反复出现的错误中学习归纳偏置，将其记录为规则卡片；通过回放验证检测无直接回归后，将规则卡片提升为代码模块或提示指导，保证更新过程可审计、可编辑、可复用。

Result: 在四个验证器基准上的实验表明，AutoVerifier大幅优于现有的最先进验证器（state-of-the-art verifiers）。

Conclusion: AutoVerifier有效地将验证器的隐式归纳偏置显式化并自动化学习，显著提升验证准确性，且更新过程具有可审计性和可复用性。

Abstract: Reference-based verifiers are important for evaluating reasoning models and providing accurate outcome rewards in reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards. To improve verification accuracy, prior work has explored rule-based, model-based, and tool-augmented verifiers for checking answer equivalence across diverse answer forms. However, the equivalence of answer forms such as $1+3.14$ and $1+π$ may depend on the question and scoring criterion. We frame such implicit assumptions as verifier inductive biases. To address this challenge, we propose AutoVerifier, a residual-guided non-parametric optimization method that learns these biases from recurring verifier errors. Specifically, AutoVerifier records these biases in rule cards and promotes them to code modules or prompt guidance only after replay validation detects no direct regressions, keeping accepted updates auditable, editable, and reusable. Experiments on four verifier benchmarks demonstrate that AutoVerifier outperforms state-of-the-art verifiers by a large margin.

</details>


### [128] [Unmatched Does Not Mean False: Incomplete Reference Sets Can Reverse Calibration Rankings in Open-Ended Theory-of-Mind Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25654)
*Zhexi Feng,Wuxi Chen,Bingrui Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了开放式心智理论（ToM）追踪器中"有限参考集+匹配器"评估管线的根本缺陷：由于参考标签仅覆盖有限信念，匹配失败的真实信念被错误标记为假，这种代理标签不仅会扭曲概率和置信度指标，还会逆转严格正确的评分规则下的模型选择结果。作者通过多组独立实验（259条固定信念、NQ-open DPR-BERT、OpenToM叙事）验证了该问题，并提出TriSource-Restore框架来修复评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式ToM追踪器的输出信念是无限的，而现有评估依赖有限参考集和匹配器，导致无法匹配但正确的输出被误标为假。这种代理标签可能系统性扭曲模型评估，甚至逆转基于严格正确评分规则的模型选择——但当参考标签被重新编码或使用盲审时，结果完全相反。这种评估失真会误导研究者和实践者的模型选择，亟需识别和修复。

Method: 作者采用多种手段：1) 固定259条信念和配对分数，对比参考标签重编码与盲审下的Brier风险，测试冻结源先验规则和Platt重校准器；2) 在NQ-open DPR-BERT管线的301个问题上比较精确匹配与人类正确性下的校准误差；3) 在OpenToM叙事上审计未匹配信念的字面真实性；4) 通过精确分解将失真归因于遗漏的真实信念，并推导闭式准则对12个已发布系统进行分类；5) 提出TriSource-Restore：将全帧参考标签和冻结自动判断锚定到概率抽样的人类试点，保持名义覆盖率、缩窄区间并修复置信度，附加速率门控。

Result: 参考重编码将加权流行率从0.783降至0.295，并使Brier风险方向逆转（源先验规则在参考标签下领先0.227，在盲审下落后0.152，覆盖全部六个作者场景）。ICE特定反转在301题NQ-open管线中出现：平均置信度基线在精确匹配下改善ICE 0.045，但在人类正确性下恶化0.074，两个区间均排除零。OpenToM叙事中90-96%的未匹配信念字面为真，配对方向再度逆转。闭式准则正确分类12个已发布系统；冻结审计回顾重放显示50次注释尝试恢复排序方向的概率至少0.996。

Conclusion: 有限参考加匹配器的评估管线会产生系统性代理标签偏差，将真实信念误标为假，从而扭曲置信度指标并逆转模型选择。该问题在多数据集和多模型上普遍存在。TriSource-Restore通过锚定到概率抽样的人类试点提供了一种可行的修复方案，能够保持标称覆盖率、缩窄区间并修复置信度，但需满足基础速率部署门控条件。

Abstract: Open-ended Theory-of-Mind (ToM) trackers emit valid beliefs absent from finite references. A finite-reference-plus-matcher pipeline marks unmatched outputs false, creating proxy labels that can reverse proper-score model selection on fixed outputs. Holding 259 beliefs and paired scores fixed, reference recoding lowers weighted prevalence from 0.783 to 0.295 and reverses strictly proper Brier risk: a frozen source-prior rule leads native confidence by 0.227 under reference labels and trails by 0.152 under blinded adjudication, in all six authored scenarios. A reference-only Platt recalibrator reverses further. An ICE-specific reversal appears in a released 301-question NQ-open DPR-BERT pipeline: its average-confidence baseline improves instance-level calibration error by 0.045 under exact match but worsens it by 0.074 under human correctness, with both intervals excluding zero. On independently authored OpenToM narratives, 90-96% of audited unmatched beliefs are literally true and the paired direction again reverses. An exact decomposition attributes the distortion to omitted truths, and a closed-form criterion correctly classifies comparisons from twelve released systems. Frozen-audit retrospective replay shows 50 attempted annotations recover ranking direction with probability at least 0.996. TriSource-Restore anchors full-frame reference labels and frozen automatic judgments to a probability-sampled human pilot, maintains at least nominal coverage, narrows intervals, and repairs confidence subject to a base-rate deployment gate.

</details>


### [129] [Reconstructing the Right Episode: Evaluating Interleaved Conversational Memory Beyond Long Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25655)
*Zhexi Feng,Ruiyi Zhang,Yongbo Yang,Pengtao Xie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SCALE-QA基准测试，用于评估聊天助手在无分段、多主题混合的对话线程中基于早期因果证据完成后续任务的能力，并提出了TSIM记忆重建方法，在多个后端上均超越强基线5.6-17.6个准确率百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文和记忆基准测试通常暴露会话或主题边界，或仅探测直接的个人记忆问题，这低估了更难的助手记忆场景——平坦的混合主题线程中，系统必须推断哪个早期片段使后续任务决策有效。现有方法无法有效应对这种"片段完整性失败"（episode integrity failure）问题。

Method: 1) 构建SCALE-QA基准：3,000个经审查的问题，覆盖10个领域，采用确定性四选一多项选择评分，包含确定性运行时构建器；支持128k全量测试和1M分层400题诊断测试。2) 提出TSIM（时间-语义交错记忆重建）方法：将对话轮流分割为连贯片段，通过层级多视角记忆栈索引，包含确定性片段级摘要和聚类路由视图。

Result: SCALE-QA挑战了强RAG基线和长上下文LLM；在三种开源和专有LLM后端上，TSIM在所有后端设置中均取得最高准确率，比最强对应基线提高5.6-17.6个准确率百分点。

Conclusion: 无分段混合主题线程中的因果证据推理是聊天助手记忆系统的关键挑战；SCALE-QA提供了可复现的评估平台，TSIM证明了通过时间与语义交错的片段级记忆重建可以有效缓解该问题。

Abstract: Conversations with chat assistants increasingly span many topics in a single long-running thread, challenging memory systems. Existing long-context and memory benchmarks often expose session or topic boundaries, or probe direct personal-memory questions. These settings understate a harder assistant-memory regime: a flat mixed-topic thread where the system must infer which earlier episode makes a later task decision valid. We introduce SCALE-QA, a constraint-grounded task QA benchmark for flat unsegmented threads targeting episode integrity failure. The dataset contains 3,000 audited questions across 10 domains, uses deterministic four-way multiple-choice grading, and includes a deterministic runtime builder; experiments use all 3,000 questions through 128k and a stratified 400-question diagnostic at 1M. SCALE-QA questions are ordinary task-oriented requests whose correct answer depends on causally related evidence introduced earlier in the conversation. We also propose Temporal-Semantic Interleaved Memory Reconstruction (TSIM), which segments the turn stream into coherent episodes and indexes them through a hierarchical multi-view memory stack with deterministic episode-level summary and cluster-routing views. Experiments show that SCALE-QA challenges strong RAG baselines and long-context LLMs alike; across three open-source and proprietary LLM backends, TSIM achieves the highest accuracy in every backend setting, gaining 5.6-17.6 accuracy points over the strongest corresponding baseline.

</details>


### [130] [Think-Probe-Respond: Improving Large Language Models as Judges of Research Idea Novelty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25660)
*Tim Schopf,Tobias Schreieder,Akiko Aizawa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型在自动化新颖性判断中尽管推理理由与人类专家相似，但最终判断结果常出现校准偏差，主要体现为系统性偏向"中等新颖"的倾向。作者提出TPR（Think-Probe-Respond）轻量方法，在推理阶段从隐藏状态中探测潜在的新颖性判断并用其调控最终回答，在强基线上提升了22.30%的判断性能。




<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化新颖性判断可加速科学发现，大语言模型被日益用于此任务。然而作者发现一个被忽视的局限：模型生成的推理理由与人类专家高度接近，但最终新颖性判断结果却常与人类专家显著分歧，这种失校准源于模型系统性偏向将想法判断为"中等新颖"。

Method: 提出Think-Probe-Respond (TPR)方法：在模型推理（思考）阶段，从隐藏状态中探测（Probe）潜在的新颖性判断，然后利用探测到的判断来调节（条件化）最终响应（Respond）。这是一种轻量级方法，无需大规模微调。

Result: 在多个强基线对比下，TPR将新颖性判断性能提升了22.30%，并有效缓解了"中等新颖"偏差问题。

Conclusion: 通过探测隐藏状态中的潜在判断来条件化最终输出，能够显著改善大语言模型在新颖性判断任务上的校准性，有效纠正系统性"中等新颖"偏差。

Abstract: Automated novelty judgment can accelerate scientific discovery by enabling efficient evaluation, refinement, and comparison of research ideas. While large language models are increasingly adopted for this task, we investigate a previously overlooked limitation in their judgment capabilities: despite generating reasoning rationales that closely mirror those of human experts, their final novelty judgments often diverge substantially. We demonstrate that this miscalibration stems from a systematic bias towards judging ideas as "medium novel".
