These interactions are essential to the safety of air transportation: failures in their execution can create severe operational hazards, as evidenced by past fatal accidents. Assessing whether an instruction has been followed requires relating what was said to the aircraft concerned, its state, and the obligations that pilots must meet. We present a runtime verification framework that monitors such procedures by checking controller-pilot exchanges, surveillance data, and onboard observations. The framework parses radio communications into events linked to the entities they concern and merges them with surveillance and onboard observations into a time-stamped trace. The ICAO-derived obligations as formalized as temporal formulas with explicit time bounds and evaluated over execution traces. Every violation is reported along with the breached obligations and the observations that support the verdict. With real traffic, the complete pipeline reaches an F1 of 0.85 against blind human-annotated violations; in 1,495 synthetic situations derived from two public corpora, the monitor logic returns the expected verdict in every case. In two historical accidents reconstructed from official investigation reports, the monitor identifies the same procedural deviations documented by the investigators.

</details>


### [198] [How Robust Are Automated Fact-Checking Systems? A Cross-Benchmark Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25934)
*Aida Usmanova,Zangir Iklassov,Markus Leippold,Ricardo Usbeck*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次对完整的"检索-验证"两阶段自动事实核查流水线进行了跨数据集交叉评估，涵盖九个模型和四个数据集（科学、开放网络、气候领域），发现模型排名高度依赖领域和评估指标，且检索仍是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AFC评估存在两大缺陷：一是忽略了简单基线，二是系统仅在单一基准上开发，无法保证跨领域泛化能力。此前研究要么只评估检索阶段，要么只评估单阶段验证，缺乏对完整两阶段流水线的跨数据集评估。

Method: 对九个模型进行基准测试，包括随机基线和稀疏基线、微调Transformer、零样本LLM以及AVeriTeC 2025共享任务中排名最高的两个系统；在覆盖科学、开放网络和气候领域的四个数据集上评估完整的检索-验证流水线。

Result: 三个关键发现：(1) 在ClimateCheck上，仅用声明和微调模型优于零样本LLM和AVeriTeC 2025顶级系统，表明噪声证据会降低真实性预测性能；(2) 系统排名强烈依赖领域和指标——SciFact最优模型(macro-F1 0.70)在ClimateCheck上降至0.31，AVeriTeC 2025冠亚军根据评估指标和数据集互换排名；(3) 用黄金标注替换检索证据可使真实性准确率提升14-22个百分点，证实检索仍是主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了AFC系统跨领域泛化的严重不足，强调了检索环节的核心瓶颈地位，并发布了代码、预处理数据集和全部结果以支持可复现的AFC研究。

Abstract: Automated fact-checking (AFC) systems retrieve evidence and predict claim veracity, yet evaluations omit simple baselines, systems are developed for a single benchmark and cannot be trusted to generalise across domains. No prior work cross-evaluates the full two-stage retrieve-then-verify pipeline across diverse datasets, complementing retrieval-only studies (Thakur et al., 2021) and single-stage benchmarking studies (Calamai et al., 2025). We benchmark nine models, ranging from random and sparse baselines to fine-tuned transformers, zero-shot LLMs, and the two highest-ranked systems from the AVeriTeC 2025 shared task, across four datasets spanning scientific, open-web, and climate domains. Three findings stand out: (1) on ClimateCheck claim-only and fine-tuned models outperform zero-shot LLM and top-performing AVeriTeC 2025 systems, highlighting that noisy evidence can degrade veracity prediction; (2) system rankings are strongly domain- and metric-dependent: the best model on SciFact (macro-F1 0.70) drops to 0.31 on ClimateCheck, while the AVeriTeC 2025 winner and runner-up swap rankings based on evaluation metrics and datasets; (3) replacing retrieved evidence with gold annotations improves veracity accuracy by 14-22 points across models, confirming retrieval remains primary bottleneck. We release code, pre-processed datasets, and all results to support reproducible AFC research.

</details>


### [199] [Candidate supply and answer selection shape the value of LLM judging in multi-agent systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25937)
*Jia-Hao Ji,Sijie Li,Jiabei Cheng,Zixi She,Jin-Tai Yu,Zhiyuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将多智能体推理概念化为"候选生成-同伴通信-最终选择"的演化流水线，发现即使正确答案已存在于候选池中，系统仍可能收敛到错误答案。通过对15,336道题和81,390个固定候选池的分析，提出三个发现：正确答案常被淹没于流行错误中；判断器可靠性随任务、生成器和答案稀有度变化；将答案频率与判断器评估结合的选择规则可将准确率从63.82%提升至70.82-70.95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统有时已具备回答正确的能力（候选池中存在正确答案），却仍报告错误结果。由于生成、通信和最终答案选择规则通常同时变化，难以解释这一现象。作者希望将这三个环节解耦，诊断正确答案"丢失"的机制，为设计能保护正确答案的多智能体架构提供依据。

Method: 将多智能体推理建模为进化流水线（候选生成→同伴通信→终端选择），类比"模因漂移"现象。分析了来自MMLU-Pro、GPQA、MedXpertQA和MuSR的15,336道题（Humanity's Last Exam单独分析），并重放81,390个固定候选池（来自16,278道题的五个基准）。通过改变最终答案选择规则（结合答案频率与LLM判断器评估）来测试是否改善报告答案。

Result: 三个主要发现：(1) 正确答案经常已出现在生成候选池中，但系统仍可能收敛并报告错误答案；(2) 判断器可靠性并非模型的固定属性，而是随任务、生成器以及正确答案稀有度而变化；(3) 将答案频率与判断器评估结合仅改变最终选择规则，即可将准确率从63.82%提高到70.82-70.95%，主要通过"拯救"那些被流行错误以数量压制的正确答案。

Conclusion: 通过隔离生成、识别和选择三个环节，本研究为设计多智能体架构建立了诊断基础，旨在保护已生成的正确答案不被丢失。生成更多候选样本的价值取决于额外样本是否使正确答案"存在、频繁或可识别"。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) sometimes already have the potential to answer correctly, but still report a wrong answer. Explaining this outcome is difficult because generation, communication and final answer-selection rules usually change simultaneously. We conceptualize multi-agent reasoning as an evolutionary pipeline of candidate generation, peer communication and terminal selection, wherein consensus without quality control can exhibit patterns of memetic drift. We study two questions: (1) when an LLM judge provides effective selection pressure by supplying a signal of answer correctness for candidates generated in a multi-agent system, and (2) when using that signal improves the reported answer. To map judge reliability, we analysed 15,336 questions from MMLU-Pro, GPQA, MedXpertQA and MuSR, with Humanity's Last Exam analysed separately. To test these rules, we replayed 81,390 fixed candidate pools drawn from 16,278 questions across five benchmarks. We report three findings. (1) A correct answer is often already present among the generated candidates, but the system can still converge on and report a wrong answer. (2) Judge reliability is not a fixed trait of the model, but varies with the task, the generator and how rare the correct answer is. (3) Combining answer frequency with the judge's evaluation changed only the final answer-selection rule and raised accuracy from 63.82% to 70.82-70.95%, primarily by rescuing correct answers that were outnumbered by popular errors. In the systems studied here, the value of generating more candidates depends on whether those extra samples make correct answers present, frequent or recognisable. By isolating generation, recognition and selection, these findings establish a diagnostic basis for designing multi-agent architectures that protect generated correct answers from being lost.

</details>


### [200] [LivingRAG: Augmenting Graph RAG with Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25960)
*Yuzhuo Cui,Zongye Zhang,Qingjie Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LivingRAG提出了一种带有可写、可复用推理经验的图RAG框架，将已验证的推理经验存储起来，供后续相关查询在检索和生成阶段复用，从而提升多跳问答的准确率并减少生成token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图RAG系统将每个查询孤立处理，推理完成后丢弃LLM响应中的有效推理信息，导致后续相关查询需要从头开始检索证据和推理，造成计算浪费且无法利用历史推理模式。

Method: LivingRAG在基于图的检索骨干上增加可写经验存储（experience store），通过两种方式在推理中复用已验证经验：1) 存储的图信号帮助检索模块定位在早期相关查询中有用的实体和段落；2) 存储的摘要为答案生成提供参考推理模式。同时分析线上QA流，利用共享实体、图邻域和问题模板来识别可复用信号。

Result: 在多跳QA基准上的实验表明，LivingRAG相比强RAG基线提升了准确率，且在复用了相关先前经验时减少了完成token的使用量。

Conclusion: 通过将推理经验持久化并复用，图RAG系统可以打破查询孤立处理的限制，实现跨查询的知识累积和推理加速，为流式QA场景下的高效RAG提供了新思路。

Abstract: Graph-based RAG improves multi-hop question answering by organizing evidence as a knowledge graph. However, most existing RAG systems process each query in isolation and discard useful reasoning from the LLM's response after inference. As a result, later related queries need to retrieve evidence and reason from scratch. We propose LivingRAG, a Graph RAG framework with writable and reusable reasoning experience. LivingRAG adds a writable experience store to a graph-based retrieval backbone, enabling verified experiences to be reused during inference in two ways. Stored graph signals help retrieval find entities and passages that were useful in earlier related queries. Stored summaries provide a reference reasoning pattern for answer generation. We analyze online QA streams and find reusable signals from shared entities, graph neighborhoods, and question templates. Experiments on multi-hop QA benchmarks show that LivingRAG improves accuracy over strong RAG baselines and reduces completion-token use when relevant prior experience is reused.

</details>


### [201] [Quantitative Analysis of $ω$-Regular Robust MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25968)
*Ali Asadi,Krishnendu Chatterjee,Ehsan Kafshdar Goharshady,Mehrdad Karrabi,Alipasha Montaseri,Ali Shafiee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有线性定义不确定集的鲁棒马尔可夫决策过程（RMDP）的定量奇偶校验问题，证明了代理和环境均存在纯记忆最优策略，并提出了一种结合定量一步改进和定性几乎必然改进的策略迭代算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究仅解决了RMDP中奇偶目标的定性分析（几乎必然或正概率满足问题），而精确的定量分析（求保证满足概率的上确界）尚未解决。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: （1）证明了代理和环境双方均存在纯记忆最优策略；（2）为线性定义的鲁棒马尔可夫链提出多项式时间定量奇偶算法，并将其作为子程序用于RMDP的策略迭代算法，该算法将定量一步改进与定性几乎必然改进相结合；（3）通过实验与显式归约为随机博弈的方法进行对比。

Result: 提出了首个解决RMDP（含线性定义不确定集）定量奇偶问题的算法：鲁棒马尔可夫链的多项式时间算法以及RMDP的策略迭代算法，并通过实验验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作将RMDP中奇偶目标的定性分析推进到精确的定量分析，给出了纯记忆最优性保证及多项式时间可解性，为RMDP的定量验证提供了理论基础和实用算法。

Abstract: Robust Markov Decision Processes (RMDPs) generalize classical MDPs by allowing uncertainty in transition probabilities and optimizing against their worst-case realization. We consider $(s,a)$-rectangular RMDPs with \emph{linearly defined} uncertainty sets and study parity objectives, which are a canonical representation of $ω$-regular objectives. An uncertainty set is linearly defined if it is described by linear inequalities over the transition distribution together with auxiliary variables, which capture the standard $L_1$ and $L_\infty$ balls as well as general polytopic uncertainty sets.
  The quantitative value is the supremum, over all agent policies, of the satisfaction probability guaranteed against the adversarial environment. Previous work studied the qualitative analysis, namely the almost-sure (resp. positive) problem that asks whether a single agent policy guarantees satisfaction with probability one (resp. positive probability) against every environment policy. In this work, we solve the exact quantitative problem.
  Our contributions are threefold. First, we show that both the agent and the environment admit pure memoryless optimal policies. Second, we give a polynomial-time algorithm for quantitative parity on linearly defined robust Markov chains and use it as a subroutine in a policy-iteration algorithm for RMDPs. The algorithm combines quantitative one-step improvements with qualitative almost-sure improvements. Finally, we report experiments comparing our approach with the explicit reduction to stochastic games.

</details>


### [202] [SciMIF: Understanding Multimodal Instruction Following in Scientific Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25973)
*Ye Shen,Yuting Zheng,Dun Pei,Zijian Chen,Wenlong Zhang,Qi Jia,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SciMIF基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)在科学领域中遵循复杂指令的能力。该基准基于22个任务、5个科学学科，设计了包含10个约束组的分类体系，并通过高保真指令注入流程与扩充现有科学数据集。实验发现不同学科间性能差异显著（化学更具挑战性），模型规模增大并未带来约束遵循的相应提升，且当前模型在细粒度约束和深层学科知识应用方面仍存在严重不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学领域中对指令遵循能力的评估对于MLLMs有效赋能科研至关重要，但当前缺乏专门针对多模态科学指令遵循的基准。作者旨在填补这一空白，从功能需求和学科特异性两个维度系统评估MLLM在科学场景下的指令遵循表现。

Method: 首先，基于5个代表性科学学科中22个不同任务的广泛分析，提出涵盖10个约束组的综合分类体系（既包含通用功能需求，也包含学科特异性特征）。然后，依据该分类体系设计一条高保真指令注入流程，系统性地扩展现有科学数据集构建SciMIF基准，并在多个SOTA闭源和开源MLLM上进行全面评估。

Result: 实验揭示了不同科学学科之间存在显著性能差异，其中化学对当前MLLMs构成更大挑战；模型规模扩大并未带来约束遵循能力的相应提升；现有模型在细粒度约束和需深度应用学科知识的指令上仍表现严重不足。数据和代码已开源。

Conclusion: SciMIF填补了多模态科学指令遵循评估的空白，为未来在严格科学应用中提升MLLMs的指令遵循能力奠定了关键基础。

Abstract: Understanding instruction-following capabilities in scientific domains is essential for effectively leveraging Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to advance the development of scientific fields. In this work, we introduce SciMIF, a novel benchmark designed to evaluate the capability of MLLMs in following complex scientific instructions. Specifically, based on an extensive analysis of 22 distinct tasks across 5 representative scientific disciplines, we propose a comprehensive taxonomy comprising 10 constraint groups that captures both general functional requirements and discipline-specific characteristics. Guided by this taxonomy, we develop a high-fidelity instruction injection pipeline to systematically augment existing scientific datasets. We conduct comprehensive experiments on multiple state-of-the-art closed-source and open-source MLLMs. Our findings reveal significant performance disparities across different scientific disciplines, with chemistry posing greater challenges for current MLLMs. Furthermore, we observe that increasing the model scale does not yield corresponding improvements in constraint adherence, and current models still struggle severely with fine-grained constraints and instructions requiring the deep application of disciplinary knowledge. SciMIF fills the current void in evaluating multimodal instruction adherence within scientific domains, laying a crucial foundation for future enhancements of MLLMs in rigorous scientific applications. Data and code will be released at https://github.com/shenye7436/SciMIF .

</details>


### [203] [Multi-Granularity Context-Enhanced RAG over Multimodal Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25986)
*Zongyu Wu,Yilong Wang,Xiaochen Wang,Minhua Lin,Zhichao Xu,Fenglong Ma,Xiang Zhang,Suhang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CEMMKG框架，通过为多模态知识图谱中的每个图像补充局部和全局范围的文本上下文（局部上下文超越周边文本语义相关句子，全局上下文提供段落摘要），并引入多粒度局部上下文设计，以解决现有MMKG-based RAG方法中图像与文本之间的语义鸿沟问题，从而提升多模态GraphRAG的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MMKG-based RAG方法通常采用独立处理各模态后再融合的通用流程，导致文本上下文在视觉信息提取和多模态知识融合阶段使用有限，造成图像与文本之间的语义鸿沟，限制了多模态GraphRAG的性能表现。

Method: 提出CEMMKG（Context-Enhanced MMKG）框架：1）为每个图像在局部和全局两个范围补充互补的文本上下文；2）局部上下文不仅包含周边文本，还纳入与图像语义相关的句子；3）全局上下文提供整个段落的摘要；4）引入多粒度局部上下文设计，在不同细节层次上捕获语义相关信息。

Result: 在视觉中心数据集上的大量实验验证了CEMMKG能有效利用上下文信息提升MMKG-based RAG性能，并且在不同MMKG-based RAG方法上的有效性证明了其广泛的适用性。

Conclusion: CEMMKG通过上下文增强的多模态知识图谱构建，有效弥合了图像与文本之间的语义鸿沟，显著提升了多模态GraphRAG的性能，且具有跨方法的广泛适用性。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is widely used to mitigate hallucination issues in large language models (LLMs) and multimodal large language models (MLLMs). In particular, knowledge graph (KG)-based RAG leverages structured knowledge to provide (M)LLMs with high-quality external information. Building on these works, recent studies have explored multimodal knowledge graphs (MMKGs) as knowledge bases for GraphRAG. This enables Graph RAG to integrate knowledge across multiple modalities, thereby further enhancing its performance. However, existing MMKG-based RAG methods generally follow a common pipeline in which different modalities are largely processed independently before being fusion. As a result, textual context is only used to a limited extent during visual information extraction and subsequent multimodal knowledge fusion. This brings a semantic gap between images and text which limits the multimodal GraphRAG performance. To address this issue, we propose a novel framework for constructing a Context-Enhanced MMKG (CEMMKG) to better support multimodal GraphRAG. The proposed CEMMKG enriches each image with complementary textual context at both local and global scopes. Local context goes beyond the surrounding text by incorporating sentences that are semantically related to the image, while global context provides a summary of the entire passage. We further introduce a multi-granularity design for the local context, allowing it to capture semantically relevant information at different levels of detail. Extensive experiments on the selected vision-centric dataset validate that CEMMKG is effective in leveraging contextual information to improve MMKG-based RAG performance. Moreover, its effectiveness across different MMKG-based RAG methods demonstrates its broad applicability.

</details>


### [204] [ProgRouter: Online Progress-Guided Orchestration for Multi-Agent LLM Workflows under Quality-Cost Tradeoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25992)
*Somgyuan Li,Ahmed M. Abdelmoniem,Shiqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProgRouter是一个在线进度引导的LLM路由框架，在多步骤多智能体工作流中自适应选择最优LLM代理，以在保持任务求解质量的同时满足时间和成本预算。实验表明其在多个基准上相对关键基线降低了运营成本且维持了强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM工作流虽然能通过协作推理解决复杂开放式任务，但反复的LLM调用和长上下文积累导致可观运营成本。现有级联路由方法仅做一次性查询级决策，无法适配多步工作流中动态、状态依赖的特性（每一步正确的LLM取决于不断演进的任务进度、剩余难度和成本效率要求），因此需要一种能随工作流进展自适应路由的方法。

Method: ProgRouter包含三个核心组件：(1) 多视图任务进度评分器，结合粗粒度工作流结果区间与子任务完成度、进度趋势和工作流状态质量的细粒度信号；(2) 双路径任务进度预测器与自适应元门控机制，用于估算每个候选路由LLM的进度增益；(3) 在线逐步路由决策机制，综合权衡进度增益、任务时间预算和长期运营成本效率。

Result: 在HumanEval Plus、MBPP、MATH-500和ASQA四个基准上（涵盖智能体代码生成、数学推理和检索增强长形式问答），ProgRouter相较关键基线降低了运营成本，同时保持了强任务求解性能。

Conclusion: ProgRouter证明了基于进度引导的在线逐步路由在保持多智能体工作流任务质量的同时，能够有效控制运营成本，弥补了传统查询级级联路由在动态多步场景下的适应性不足。

Abstract: Multi-agent large language model (LLM) workflows have emerged as a powerful paradigm for solving complex, open-ended tasks through collaborative reasoning among specialized LLM agents, but they incur substantial operating costs due to repeated LLM invocations and long-horizon context accumulation. Existing cascade routing methods make one-shot, query-level decisions and cannot adapt to the dynamic, state-dependent nature of multi-step workflows, in which the right LLM at each step depends on evolving task progress, remaining task difficulty, and cost-efficiency requirements. We present ProgRouter, an online progress-guided routing framework that adaptively selects LLM agents across workflow steps to preserve task-solving quality while adhering to time and cost budgets. ProgRouter introduces a multi-view task progress scorer that combines coarse workflow outcome regimes with fine-grained signals on subtask completion, progress trends, and workflow state quality. Then, a dual-path task progress predictor and an adaptive meta-gating mechanism estimate the progress gain for each candidate routed LLM. ProgRouter makes online step-wise routing decisions that balance progress gain, task time budgets, and long-term operating cost efficiency. Experiments on HumanEval Plus, MBPP, MATH-500, and ASQA, spanning agentic code generation, mathematical reasoning, and retrieval-augmented long-form question answering, demonstrate that ProgRouter reduces the operating cost relative to key baselines while maintaining strong task-solving performance.

</details>


### [205] [AsymSpec: Context-Asymmetric Speculative Decoding for Agentic LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26004)
*Sheng Liang,Yongyue Zhang,Nathanael Brian,Hang Lv,Hao Wang,Chen Zhang,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AsymSpec非对称投机解码框架：轻量级草稿模型读取完整上下文，大型验证模型在压缩视图上运行，通过对比δ融合和发散感知接受门控，在保持约90%全上下文精度的同时实现1.3-1.7倍吞吐加速和0.2-0.3倍计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic LLM流水线因上下文累积面临高昂推理成本，现有压缩方法会降低任务精度，而投机解码要求草稿模型与验证模型共享相同上下文，无法解决精度-开销权衡问题。

Method: AsymSpec打破草稿模型与验证模型上下文对称性：轻量级草稿模型读取完整输入，大型验证模型操作压缩视图；通过对比δ-fusion对logits进行融合，并采用发散感知接受门控机制保持验证稳定性和高草稿接受率。

Result: 在四种Agent能力及两个端到端Agent基准上，AsymSpec平均达到约90%的全上下文精度，在孤立文本能力上以0.2-0.3倍计算成本实现1.3-1.7倍吞吐加速。

Conclusion: 非对称上下文访问在压缩丢弃关键推理信号的场景下能带来显著收益，为Agentic LLM的精度-开销权衡提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Agentic LLM pipelines face escalating inference costs as context accumulates across retrieval, tool use, and multi-turn interactions. To control latency, deployments routinely compress inputs, but this degrades task accuracy. Speculative decoding (SD) accelerates generation losslessly, yet it assumes the drafter and verifier share an identical context, preventing SD from resolving the accuracy-overhead trade-off. We propose AsymSpec, an asymmetric speculative decoding framework that breaks this symmetry: a lightweight drafter reads the full input while the large verifier operates on the compressed view. The drafter steers the verifier via a contrastive $δ$-fusion of logits, modulated by a divergence-aware acceptance gate that preserves verification stability and high draft acceptance rates. Evaluated across four agentic capabilities and two end-to-end agent benchmarks, AsymSpec reaches $\approx 90\%$ of full-context accuracy on average, delivering $1.3$--$1.7\times$ throughput speedups at $0.2$--$0.3\times$ the compute cost on isolated text capabilities. These results show that asymmetric context access yields substantial gains precisely when compression discards critical reasoning signals.

</details>


### [206] [Imitation Learning for Connection-Tableau Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26009)
*Fredrik Rømming,Mantas Bakšys,Martin S. Fixman,Sean B. Holden*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将自动定理证明过程统一建模为形式演算诱导的转移系统中的策略，并用图神经网络评分证明编辑步骤，通过模仿学习训练，在固定步数预算下比leanCoP多解决46%的问题且步数减少一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动定理证明器逐步构建证明，每一步需要选择添加和删除的内容，但传统搜索方法缺乏可推广的学习策略，难以在不同问题间迁移知识。

Method: 将定理证明构造视为形式演算（子句连接tableaux）诱导的转移系统中的策略，统一leanCoP风格搜索和plCoP/rlCoP风格规划为同一接口上的有状态策略；配备图神经网络对证明编辑步骤进行评分，从跨问题共享的结构中学习，并通过模仿学习从已找到的证明中训练。

Result: 在M2k、MPTP2078-bushy和TPTP v9.2.1上，在固定步数预算内，学习策略比leanCoP多解决高达46%的问题，并且达到证明所需的步数减少一个数量级。

Conclusion: 通过图神经网络和模仿学习训练的策略可以显著超越传统符号回溯方法，在更少步数内解决更多问题，验证了将证明构造建模为策略学习问题的有效性。

Abstract: An automated theorem prover builds a proof step by step, choosing at each point what to add and what to remove. We cast this construction as a policy acting in a transition system induced by a formal calculus, which fixes which steps are sound: for clausal connection tableaux, leanCoP-style search and plCoP/rlCoP-style planning then become stateful policies over one interface, and policy-learning methods apply directly. We equip such policies with a graph neural network that scores proof edits from structure that transfers across problems, train it by imitation learning from found proofs, and measure how performance holds as we remove search scaffolding, from full symbolic backtracking to a policy the network drives alone. Within a fixed step budget on M2k, MPTP2078-bushy, and TPTP v9.2.1, learned policies solve up to 46% more problems than leanCoP, and reach proofs in an order of magnitude fewer steps.

</details>


### [207] [Trace Integrity for LLM Data Agents: A Vision for Auditable Structured Reasoning in Real-World Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26036)
*Srimonti Dutta,Akshata Kishore Moharir*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Trace Integrity（迹完整性），一个评估LLM数据代理可靠性的部署级标准。传统基于答案准确率的评估不足以衡量结构化数据任务中LLM代理的可靠性，因为一个基准测试的正确答案可能来自无效的计算轨迹。作者引入执行合约（Execution Contracts）作为结构化工件来操作化Trace Integrity，并提出了CAIT（正确答案/无效轨迹）率作为新的评估指标。在BIRD Mini-Dev上的实验表明，答案准确率、迹有效性和静默失败风险是三个截然不同的评估信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在结构化数据任务中，LLM数据代理可能产生看似正确的答案，但其背后的计算轨迹可能是无效的（如推理过程不明确、无法执行或与Schema不符）。现有的答案准确率（Answer Accuracy）作为可靠性信号存在根本不足，因为它无法捕捉"正确答案背后的无效计算"这一部署级失败模式。作者将此定义为"结构鸿沟"（Structure Gap）：自然语言推理和自由格式的理由说明无法可靠地指定真实系统所需的操作符级程序。

Method: 1) 定义Trace Integrity，包含七个维度：显式性、可执行性、Schema有效性、操作符忠实性、可重放性、答案一致性和可审计性。2) 引入执行合约（Execution Contracts），一种结构化工件，将用户意图绑定到Schema元素、操作符计划、假设、可执行查询、验证状态和最终答案关联。3) 提出CAIT（Correct Answer / Invalid Trace）率，衡量答案级评估将计算上不支持的结果计为成功的频率。4) 在BIRD Mini-Dev基准上进行实证比较：直接SQL、操作摘要+SQL、合约优先SQL三种策略。

Result: 在BIRD Mini-Dev上，Direct SQL、Operation Summary + SQL和Contract-First SQL的答案准确率分别为20%、22%和24%，而它们的Trace Integrity通过率分别为39%、43%和40%。CAIT率分别高达55%、59.1%和45.8%，表明很大比例的"正确答案"实际上是计算上无支撑的。这些数据证实了三种策略下答案准确率与迹有效性之间存在显著脱节。

Conclusion: 真实世界的LLM数据代理不应仅以输出是否匹配参考答案来评估，而应以输出是否由可审计的计算支撑来评估。答案准确率、迹有效性和静默失败风险是三个独立的评估维度，部署系统需要综合考量。Trace Integrity方法论为构建更可靠的LLM数据代理提供了新的评估框架和具体操作手段。

Abstract: Answer accuracy is an insufficient reliability signal for LLM data agents. In structured-data tasks, a benchmark-correct answer can be produced by an invalid trace. This paper introduces Trace Integrity, a deployment reliability criterion for evaluating whether the computation recorded behind an answer is explicit, executable, schema-valid, operator-faithful, replayable, answer-consistent, and auditable. We identify the Structure Gap as the deployment failure mode that makes Trace Integrity necessary: natural-language reasoning and free-form rationales do not reliably specify the operator-level programs required by real-world systems. We operationalize Trace Integrity with execution contracts, structured artifacts that bind user intent to schema elements, operator plans, assumptions, executable queries, verification status, and final-answer linkage. We also introduce CAIT (Correct Answer / Invalid Trace) Rate, which measures how often answer-only evaluation counts computationally unsupported outputs as successes. In an empirical demonstration on BIRD Mini-Dev, Direct SQL, Operation Summary + SQL, and Contract-First SQL achieve answer accuracies of 20%, 22%, and 24%, while their Trace Integrity Pass Rates are 39%, 43%, and 40% and their CAIT Rates remain high at 55%, 59.1%, and 45.8%, showing that answer accuracy, trace validity, and silent-failure risk are distinct evaluation signals. Real-world LLM data agents should, therefore, be evaluated not only by whether their outputs match a reference answer, but by whether those outputs are backed by auditable computation.

</details>


### [208] [SwarmWorld: Stigmergic technological evolution in societies of language-model agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26081)
*Subhadeep Pal,Fiona Y. Wang,Markus J. Buehler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在SwarmWorld环境中，初始同质的LLM代理如何在无预设角色或指令的情况下，通过共享物理环境自我组织成技术社会，并构建出比强隔离搜索基线更广泛、更具韧性的技术组合的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大多多代理系统依赖直接对话、预定义角色或集中式工作流，尚不清楚去中心化代理能否在没有明确分工的情况下自主构建功能性技术并超越独立搜索的性能。

Method: 在SwarmWorld仿真中，初始同质的LLM代理在固定动作和材料模式下探索空间环境、处理资源、测试材料、构建持久人工制品并编写可执行控制器，由确定性模拟器在代理被移除后评估其在不同扰动下的表现。通过将认知与后果分离（代理提出架构和控制器，模拟世界决定功能），系统自然地涌现出分工与技术积累。

Result: 共享社会能发展出比强best-of-N孤立搜索基线更广泛、更坚韧的技术组合，尽管孤立搜索在最强单体工件上仍具竞争力。代理分化为探索、建造、维护和协调等行为并随世界成熟而转变；技术通过协作建造、可执行继承和持久代理-工件网络积累，且多数重用始于物理观察而非交流。显式文化机制能增强协作和组织，但功能收益取决于结果和时间尺度；物理stigmergy单独即可支撑能干的群体，而交互驱动的是持久技术生态而非普遍更优的个体发明。

Conclusion: 去中心化、无预设角色的LLM代理能够通过物理环境中的stigmergy自发涌现出具有分工和技术积累的社会，实现群体层面的技术韧性；然而，社会协作的优势主要体现在技术生态的广度与持续性上，而非单个工艺品的极致性能。

Abstract: Collective intelligence can emerge when individuals coordinate through a shared environment, allowing local actions to accumulate into durable social organization. Language-model agents offer a new substrate for this process, yet most multi-agent systems rely on direct conversation, predefined roles, or centralized workflows. It remains unclear whether decentralized agents can build functional technologies and outperform independent search. Here, initially homogeneous LLM agents in SwarmWorld self-organize without assigned roles or recipes into evolving technological societies. Agents explore a spatial environment, process resources, test materials, construct persistent artifacts, and write executable controllers evaluated by a deterministic simulator under unseen disturbances after the agents are removed. SwarmWorld splits cognition from consequence: agents propose architectures and controllers within fixed action and material schemas, while the simulated world determines function. Shared societies develop broader, more resilient technological portfolios than a strong best-of-N isolated-search baseline, although isolated search remains competitive for the strongest artifact. Agents differentiate into exploration, construction, maintenance, and coordination behaviors, transitioning as the world matures. Technologies accumulate through collaborative construction, executable inheritance, and persistent agent-artifact networks, with most reuse beginning through physical observation rather than communication. Explicit cultural mechanisms amplify collaboration and organization, but functional benefits depend on outcome and timescale. Physical stigmergy alone supports capable societies, while interaction drives persistent technological ecologies rather than universally superior individual inventions.

</details>


### [209] [Planetary Prediction Engine: Autonomous Geospatial Prediction via Intelligent Data Selection and Foundation Model Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26088)
*Evelyn Ma,Rama Kumar Pasumarthi,Kishwar Shafin,Mandar Sharma,Mimi Sun,Hamed Sadeghi,Dav M. Ebengo,Mbulayi Onesime,Rouslan Solomakhin,John Wamburu,William Ogallo,Aisha Walcott-Bryant,Sanxing Chen,Arbaaz Muslim,Yael Mayer,Ronald Ho,Roy Lee,Ruth Alcantara,Abdoulaye Diack,Monica Bharel,Lambert Rosique,Jeremy Amez-Droz,Christopher Haire,James Manyika,Yossi Matias,Niv Efron,Gautam Prasad,Shravya Shetty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了行星预测引擎（PPE），一个从自然语言查询直接端到端执行行星尺度地理空间建模的自主AI系统，能够自动完成多模态数据检索、融合与模型选择，在多个跨领域任务中显著超越专家基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球性挑战（粮食安全、灾害风险、疾病暴发、社会经济脆弱性）亟需高保真度地理空间建模，但现有预测行星尺度模型受限于碎片化的数据生态，需要大量人工进行数据检索、多模态数据整理融合以及迭代模型选择，技术门槛高且效率低下。

Method: PPE是一个自主AI系统，端到端处理从自然语言查询到预测结果的全流程：1) 实时合成多模态数据集，跨开放网络与地球观测平台（Data Commons、Google Earth Engine）检索时空相关协变量；2) 融合地理空间基础模型嵌入（PDFM、AlphaEarth）；3) 同时搜索任务定制模型架构族，并配备自动化过拟合防护机制。

Result: PPE在多样化任务、地域和科学领域中持续超越现有最优方法或人工调优专家基线：美国空间回归中，21个CDC健康指标平均R²从60.0%提升至76.8%，FEMA国家风险指数从60.0%提升至64.9%，社会脆弱性指数从58.6%提升至66.2%；数据稀缺下的空间降尺度中，尼日利亚粮食安全指标R²从31.5%提升至66.1%，准确率翻倍；2026年刚果（金）Bundibugyo埃博拉疫情流行病学预报中，Recall@10达83.3%（五次周预报识别出18个新入侵卫生区中的15个），较公开最优模型提升10.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过将自主多模态行星数据发现与定向模型优化相结合，PPE降低了行星尺度分析的技术门槛，实现了快速、定制化、专家级的地理空间模型部署能力，为行星预测领域提供了一种可扩展的自主解决方案。

Abstract: Addressing critical global challenges, from food security and disaster risk to disease outbreaks and socio-economic vulnerability, demands high-fidelity geospatial modeling. However, building predictive planetary models remains bottlenecked by a fragmented data ecosystem, requiring manual data retrieval, multimodal data curation and fusion along with iterative model selection. We present the Planetary Prediction Engine (PPE), an autonomous AI system that executes this end-to-end workflow directly from natural-language queries. PPE synthesizes multimodal datasets on the fly, retrieving spatiotemporally relevant covariates across open-web and Earth observation platforms (Data Commons, Google Earth Engine) and fusing them with geospatial foundation model embeddings (PDFM, AlphaEarth). Simultaneously, it searches over task-tailored model architecture families with automated overfitting guards. Across diverse tasks, geographies, and scientific domains, PPE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art or manually tuned expert baselines. For US spatial regression, PPE improves mean $R^2$ across 21 CDC health indicators (76.8% vs. 60.0%), FEMA national risk indices (64.9% vs. 60.0%), and the Social Vulnerability Index (66.2% vs. 58.6%). For spatial downscaling in data-scarce settings, PPE integrates localized proxies to double baseline accuracy in Nigerian food security indicators ($R^2$ of 66.1% vs. 31.5%). For epidemiological nowcasting of the 2026 DRC Bundibugyo Ebola outbreak, PPE achieves a Recall@10 of 83.3% (identifying 15 of 18 newly invaded health zones across five weekly forecasts), a +10.3 percentage-point improvement over the public state-of-the-art modeling (~73%). By combining autonomous multimodal planetary data discovery with targeted model optimization, PPE lowers the technical barrier to planetary-scale analytics, enabling rapid, customized, expert-level deployment.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [210] [Sequential Object Placement Optimization with Convex Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25162)
*Yuezhe Zhang,Xiangyu Lyu,Sohan Rudra,Davide Tateo,Georgia Chalvatzaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SOPO-CD，一个将机器人物体装箱问题建模为可微非线性优化的顺序优化框架，在分解的自由空间中求解物体放置。通过证明凸物体放入凸包等价于约束物体顶点位于凸包内，实现了闭式约束与导数计算（200ns内），并在毫秒级内完成最优放置，相比经典网格搜索方法提速100倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人物体装箱是物流和工业部署中的核心挑战，其难点在于组合搜索的维度灾难以及处理不规则形状物体的动态和接触约束。现有启发式与学习方法假设空间具有有限的离散化分辨率，随着离散化精度提高计算效率急剧下降。本文旨在消除这些假设，实现高效且精确的物体放置优化。

Method: 将物体放置问题重构为分解自由空间中的可微非线性优化问题。核心理论贡献是证明凸物体放入凸包等价于约束该物体所有顶点位于凸包内部。基于此，约束及其导数可以表示为闭式形式并在200ns内完成计算。作者实现了一个自定义求解器，在紧密约束空间中以毫秒级速度获得最优放置。

Result: 相比于经典网格搜索方法实现了100倍的加速。框架成功泛化到2D七巧板、2D俄罗斯方块和3D装箱问题，展现了强大的计算性能和装箱利用率。此外，还通过Allegro Hand和Xarm机器人在线解决了真实世界的七巧板拼图任务。

Conclusion: SOPO-CD通过消除离散化假设，将物体装箱从组合搜索转化为连续可微优化问题，显著提升了计算效率和可扩展性，并在多种2D/3D装箱任务以及真实机器人操控中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Robotic object packing has been a core challenge for robotic deployment in logistics, industry, etc., due to the curse of dimensionality in combinatorial search and the difficulty of dealing with dynamic and contact constraints for irregularly shaped objects. Current heuristic and learning-based methods assume a limited spatial discretization resolution of space, and computation becomes extremely inefficient as discretization accuracy increases. In this work, we eliminate these assumptions by introducing SOPO-CD, a sequential optimization framework that frames object placement as a differentiable nonlinear optimization problem in a decomposed free space. We prove that placing a convex object inside a convex hull is essentially constraining the vertices of the object inside the convex hull. The constraints and their derivatives can be written in closed form and calculated within $200$ns. We implement a custom solver that achieves optimal placement within tightly constrained space in milliseconds; a $100 \times$ speedup compared to a classical grid search method. We generalize our framework to 2D Tangram, 2D Tetris, and 3D Bin Packing, and have demonstrated strong computational performance and packing utility. We also demonstrate solving a real-world Tangram puzzle online using an Allegro Hand and an Xarm.

</details>


### [211] [Control-Oriented Learning for Dynamic Tracking and Stability Analysis of Soft Pneumatic Actuators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25171)
*Nithin S. Kumar,Eric J. Barth*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向控制的软气动执行器数据驱动建模与控制框架，将执行器行为分解为非线性静态平衡模型和线性残差动态模型（基于EDMDc识别），实现前馈补偿、任务空间反馈控制及局部闭环稳定性分析。实验在低速（~10 mm/s）下实现约1 mm RMSE轨迹跟踪，高速（~100 mm/s）下低于10 mm RMSE，并能以超过25 m/s²的峰值加速度稳定跟踪动态参考轨迹同时实时避障，且稳定性分析被实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软气动执行器具有固有柔顺性和安全交互的优点，但其高度非线性的分布动力学特性使其难以建模和控制，现有方法无法同时兼顾精确建模、实时控制和稳定性保障，亟需一种控制导向的实用框架。

Method: 将执行器行为分解为两部分：非线性静态平衡模型用于捕捉稳态非线性特性，以及基于扩展动态模式分解（EDMDc）识别的线性残差动态模型用于描述偏离平衡点的动态行为。该混合表示支持前馈补偿、任务空间反馈控制，并通过增广线性模型提供局部闭环稳定性分析。

Result: 实验结果表明：低速轨迹跟踪（~10 mm/s）误差约1 mm RMSE，高速跟踪（~100 mm/s）误差低于10 mm RMSE；系统能以超过25 m/s²的峰值加速度稳定跟踪用户生成的动态参考轨迹，同时实现实时避障；提出的稳定性分析方法能准确预测稳定、临界和不稳定运行状态。

Conclusion: 结构化、控制导向的学习方法为软执行器控制提供了一个准确且实用的框架，兼顾了建模精度、实时控制性能和稳定性保障，证明了在软机器人控制中结合物理结构与数据驱动学习的有效性。

Abstract: Soft pneumatic actuators offer inherent compliance and safe interaction but remain difficult to model and control because of their highly nonlinear, distributed dynamics. We present a control-oriented data-driven modeling and control framework that decomposes actuator behavior into a nonlinear static equilibrium model and a linear residual dynamics model identified using Extended Dynamic Mode Decomposition with control (EDMDc). This representation enables feedforward compensation, task-space feedback control, and local closed-loop stability analysis through an augmented linear model. Experiments achieve approximately 1 mm root mean square error (RMSE) during low-speed (approximately 10 mm/s) trajectory tracking and below 10 mm RMSE at higher speeds (approximately 100 mm/s). The framework further achieves stable tracking of highly dynamic user-generated references with peak accelerations exceeding 25 m/s^2 while simultaneously performing real-time obstacle avoidance. Finally, the proposed stability analysis is experimentally validated by accurately predicting stable, marginal, and unstable operating regimes. These results demonstrate that structured, control-oriented learning provides an accurate and practical framework for soft actuator control.

</details>


### [212] [CRESSim-Neo: A Batched GPU Simulation Engine for Surgical Robotics and Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25192)
*Yafei Ou,Ahnaf Naheen,Tleukhan Mussin,Hans Jarales,Melwin Moncy,Mahdi Tavakoli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CRESSim-Neo是一个面向手术机器人和机器人学习的批量GPU仿真引擎，结合了刚体、变形组织、流体和绳索的位置动力学仿真，以及批量渲染、手术专用感知和GPU驻留数据流水线。在RTX 4090上，该引擎对8192个并行CartPole环境每秒可达203万个环境步，并支持组织操作、液体抽吸、缝合、缆索驱动机器人和超声图像合成等应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术机器人学习需要大规模、高效且可扩展的仿真平台，以支持包括组织变形、流体交互和超声感知在内的复杂手术场景。现有仿真引擎往往无法同时兼顾物理保真度、批量并行性和与GPU学习框架的高效集成。

Method: 采用位置动力学（PBD）方法统一仿真刚体、可变形组织、流体和绳索；实现批量渲染、手术专用感知模块和GPU驻留数据流水线；通过DLPACK实现与PyTorch的零拷贝集成，支持GPU驻留的机器人学习。

Result: 在NVIDIA RTX 4090上，8192个并行CartPole环境达到每秒203万个环境步；成功扩展到包括组织变形、流体交互和超声感知在内的批量手术仿真场景，验证了视觉与手术机器人学习任务的适用性。

Conclusion: CRESSim-Neo提供了一个统一且可扩展的手术仿真、合成数据生成和手术机器人学习平台，兼顾了仿真精度、并行效率和深度学习框架的无缝集成。

Abstract: We introduce CRESSim-Neo, a batched GPU simulation engine for surgical robotics and robot learning. CRESSim-Neo combines position-based simulation of rigid bodies, deformable tissues, fluids, and strands with batched rendering, surgery-specific sensing, and a GPU-resident data pipeline. The engine supports applications including tissue manipulation, fluid suction, suturing, cable-driven robots, and ultrasound image synthesis. Direct access to physics and rendering buffers enables GPU-resident robot learning and zero-copy PyTorch integration using DLPack. We demonstrate CRESSim-Neo across rigid-body, deformable-body, and fluid simulation tasks, including vision-based and surgical robot-learning scenarios. On an NVIDIA RTX 4090, the engine achieves up to 2.03 million environment steps per second for 8192 parallel CartPole environments, and scales to batched surgical scenarios involving tissue deformation, fluid interaction, and ultrasound sensing. Overall, CRESSim-Neo provides a unified and scalable platform for surgical simulation, synthetic data generation, and surgical robot learning.

</details>


### [213] [Longitudinal Robot Learning from Demonstration with Care Providers in a Home Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25196)
*Nina Moorman,Julianna Schalkwyk,Vriksha Srihari,Qingyu Xiao,Kamel Alrashedy,Hongseok Jeong,Kiersten Lange,Matthew B. Luebbers,Matthew Gombolay*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了非专家终端用户在家庭环境中（无现场机器人专家反馈）通过示教学习（LfD）教导机器人时遇到的关键障碍，通过人类受试者实验评估了两种示教指导形式（预训练和自适应反馈），并提出了开源相关数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LfD可用性评估大多在受控实验室环境中进行，且有机器人专家在场，缺乏对非专家用户在真实家庭环境中独立使用LfD时障碍的研究，生态效度不足。

Method: 以护理人员为受试群体，在家庭环境中进行多轮访问的人类受试者实验；通过两种先前开发的示教指导形式（预训练和自适应反馈）为无专家反馈的非专家用户提供支持；最终构建并拟开源护理人员教导机器人辅助任务的多轮家庭访问LfD数据集。

Result: 识别了非专家终端用户在无现场专家反馈的家庭环境中通过示教教导机器人时的关键障碍；实验在真实家庭环境多维访问中进行，提高了评估的生态效度；产出了护理人员示教辅助任务的LfD数据集并拟开源。

Conclusion: LfD系统在非实验室真实场景下的部署需要针对非专家用户提供额外示教指导，本研究通过预训练和自适应反馈的方式缓解了无专家在场时的障碍，并为后续研究贡献了可用的开源数据集。

Abstract: Learning from demonstration (LfD) methods enable non-expert end users to teach robots novel skills without explicit programming. However most evaluations of the usability of LfD with non-experts has been conducted in controlled laboratory environments with a robotics experimenter present. In this work we identify non-expert end users' key barriers when teaching robots via demonstration without live robotics expert feedback in a home environment. In our human subjects experiment we support the non-expert end users through two forms of demonstrator guidance developed in prior work: pre-training and adaptive feedback. Towards the ecological validity of the evaluation, we conduct this experimentation over multiple visits, with a population of care providers. Finally, we propose to open source the resulting LfD dataset of care providers teaching a robot assistive tasks over multiple visits to a home environment.

</details>


### [214] [Development of a Voice-Controlled Tendon-Driven Bionic Hand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25222)
*Urja Kohli,Shagata Chanda,Kritika Gandhi,Charu Nigam,Aditi Surya Kamal,Pooja Bhati*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种基于肌腱驱动方法和Arduino微控制器的自动仿生手设计，通过伺服电机实现手指协调运动，支持手势执行（张手、握拳、捏取、半弯曲）和蓝牙语音命令控制，实验表明系统具有稳定、可重复的运动行为和对不同形状物体的可靠抓取能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手部功能受损会严重影响个体日常生活能力，因此设计稳定且可控的辅助支撑装置是一个重要的研究领域。

Method: 采用基于肌腱的驱动方法，由伺服电机带动手指运动并辅以标定角度控制；使用Arduino微控制器编程实现预设手势；通过蓝牙收发架构实现语音命令界面，将训练指令即时转换为手指动作。

Result: 手指完全弯曲序列约需7-8秒；多次驱动循环中运动保持重复性和一致性，无明显张力损失或控制精度下降；对不同形状和尺寸的物体均能稳定抓取，表明手指间具有一致的协调性。

Conclusion: 该仿生手系统提供了可预测且稳定的控制行为，具备简单高效的机械结构与控制架构，在手部辅助支持领域具有应用潜力。

Abstract: The impairment of the hands can seriously affect the abilities of every individual to perform the every-day activity, so the design of stable and controllable support devices is a significant field of study. This paper is about the design and implementation of an automated bionic hand which is dedicated to the coordinated finger movement through the simplified and efficient actuation mechanism. The method that the proposed system was designed on is the tendon-based method whereby the servo motors generate the movement of the fingers, with assistance of the angular control which is calibrated. An actuation is controlled by a microcontroller that will be programmed by use of an Arduino-based microcontroller to carry out programmed gestures that include open hand, fist, pinch and half flexion. It has an interface that is voice command enabled to make it easy to interact with a Bluetooth based sender receiver architecture which offers an option of executing trained commands which are immediately converted to finger actions. To explore the motions behavior, finger coordination and control response to the input, the behavior of the experiment system is tested. The actuation of the fingers was found to take a total of about 7-8 seconds to achieve full flexion of all fingers in a sequence. The system showed repetitive and constant motion throughout several actuation cycles without loss of any apparent tension or precision of control. There was a stable grasp of objects of different shapes and sizes, which implied consistent coordination between the fingers. These findings indicate that the proposed system offers predictable and steady control behavior and has a simple and efficient mechanical and control architecture.

</details>


### [215] [Generative Action-Chunk Sampling for Adaptive Stiffness Control in Physical Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25284)
*Aoi Otake,Ferdinand Hartmann,Ko Igari,Shingo Murata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于生成式动作块采样的自适应刚度框架，通过采样动作块之间的变异性动态调节机器人关节刚度与阻尼，在物理人机协作中平衡辅助与顺从，在协作搬运任务中取得0.95的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理人机协作要求机器人在人类意图明确时提供辅助，而在未来动作存在多种可能性时保持顺从。传统固定刚度方法无法根据人类意图的不确定性动态调整，需要一种能在线感知人类意图不确定性并自适应调节刚度的机制。

Method: 提出自适应刚度框架：基于RGB图像和外部关节力矩估计，策略从观察条件先验中采样多个未来动作块；利用采样动作块之间的变异性持续调节关节刚度与阻尼——变异性越大则机器人越顺从以引导人类，变异性越小则提供更坚定的辅助。

Result: 在包含四个可能方向的真实协作搬运任务中，所提方法平均成功率达0.95，优于固定刚度消融（0.83）和确定性基线（0.69）。在方向判定附近，动作块采样的变异性增加，控制器相应降低刚度。

Conclusion: 生成式策略采样动作的变异性可作为在线控制信号，用于平衡物理人机交互中的辅助与顺从，为自适应刚度调节提供了新的有效途径。

Abstract: Physical human-robot collaboration requires a robot to provide assistance when human intention is clear while remaining compliant when several future motions are plausible. We present an adaptive stiffness framework based on generative action-chunk sampling. Conditioned on an RGB image and external joint-torque estimates, the policy samples multiple future action chunks from an observation-conditioned prior. Variation among the sampled action chunks is used to continuously adapt joint stiffness and damping. Greater variation makes the robot more compliant to facilitate human guidance, whereas lower variation provides firmer assistance. In a real-world collaborative transport task with four possible directions, the proposed method achieved an average success rate of 0.95, compared with 0.83 for a fixed-stiffness ablation and 0.69 for a deterministic baseline. Near direction determination, variation among the sampled action chunks increased and the controller accordingly reduced stiffness. These results suggest that variation among actions sampled by a generative policy can serve as an online control signal for balancing assistance and compliance in physical human-robot interaction.

</details>


### [216] [RAEM: Robust Autonomous Exploration for Multi-Floor Environments with a Quadruped Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25366)
*Zikang Yuan,Yuan Ren,Yian Wang,Yixue Wang,Enze Fang,Xuewei Zhang,Junda Cheng,Chi Chen,Chin-Pang Ho,Lijun Zhu,Shaohang Xu,Kwang-Ting Cheng,Xin Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RAEM，一种面向四足机器人在多楼层环境中自主探索的鲁棒框架。RAEM采用混合局部-全局可通行性表示：局部断层扫描图和分类局部3D网格图用于在线地形分析与连通性评估，同时增量构建高程感知的全局拓扑图以支持高效的跨楼层探索规划。此外引入楼梯中心对齐策略和双路径搜索机制，解决爬楼时的偏航突变和全局拓扑局部断开时的路径恢复问题。仿真与真实实验验证了其在多楼层结构中（包括连续探索五层楼梯间）的鲁棒性和计算稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地面机器人探索方法多依赖平面可通行性表示，无法充分建模多楼层建筑的叠层结构与跨楼层连通性；基于断层扫描的表示虽能有效建模多楼层导航，但维护全局断层图会带来巨大的在线计算开销；同时楼梯井中稀疏碎片化的LiDAR观测会降低局部可通行性估计质量，导致视点放置不规则和临时拓扑断连。

Method: RAEM提出混合局部-全局可通行性表示：局部使用断层扫描图与显式分类的局部3D网格图进行在线地形分析和连通性评估；全局则通过从局部空间表示中增量构建高程感知的全局拓扑图，实现高效的跨楼层探索规划。此外，设计了楼梯中心对齐策略以减少爬楼过程中的突发偏航变化，以及双路径搜索机制用于在全局拓扑局部断开时恢复引导路径。

Result: 大量仿真与真实世界实验表明，RAEM在多楼层结构中实现了鲁棒且计算稳定的自主探索，包括能够连续探索一个五层楼的楼梯间结构。

Conclusion: RAEM通过混合局部-全局表示有效解决了多楼层环境探索中平面表示的局限性和全局断层图的高计算开销问题，同时通过楼梯中心对齐和双路径搜索机制增强了爬楼场景下的稳定性与鲁棒性，为四足机器人多楼层自主探索提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose RAEM, a robust autonomous exploration framework for quadruped robots operating in multi-floor environments. Most existing ground-robot exploration approaches rely on planar traversability representations, which cannot adequately represent the overlapping structures and cross-floor connectivity of multi-floor buildings. Although tomography-based representations provide effective traversability modeling for multi-floor navigation, maintaining a global tomography map incurs substantial computational overhead for online exploration with frequent replanning. Moreover, sparse and fragmented LiDAR observations in stairwells can degrade local traversability estimation, leading to irregular viewpoint placement and temporary topological disconnections. To address these challenges, RAEM adopts a hybrid local-global traversability representation, in which a local tomography map and an explicitly categorized local 3D grid map are used for online terrain analysis and connectivity evaluation, while an elevation-aware global topological graph is incrementally constructed from these local spatial representations for efficient cross-floor exploration planning. We further introduce a staircase center alignment strategy to reduce abrupt yaw variations during climbing and a dual path searching mechanism to recover guidance paths when the global topology is locally disconnected. Extensive simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate robust and computationally stable autonomous exploration across multi-floor structures, including continuous exploration of a five-floor stairwell.

</details>


### [217] [A Taxonomy of Construction Task Activities for Robot Workers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25395)
*Sadman Sakib,Zhangyi None Peng,Yujie Pang,Yu Otsuki,Mohammad Abdullah Al Faruque*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TARCAT，一个基于职业活动构建的施工机器人动作分类体系，从91个O*NET任务和30个教学视频中衍生出41个动作原语（分为12组、3大类），支持将参数化原语序列组合为可复用技能，用于组织演示、明确机器人需求，并在DOBOT CR3机械臂上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作模型为机器人提供更广泛的操作能力，但建筑施工部署需要精确掌握工人活动及其对应的机器人能力清单。需要一个职业接地（occupation-grounded）的分类体系来作为通用词汇表，连接人类工作分析与通用施工机器人开发。

Method: 从7个高就业施工职业的91个O*NET任务以及30个物理工作的教学视频中提取并定义分类体系；定义41个动作原语、12个动作组和3个动作类别；提供将参数化原语序列组合为可复用技能的机制。

Result: 构建了TARCAT分类体系，在DOBOT CR3机械臂配合CRAFT手上演示了选定的动作原语，并公开了标注数据（GitHub链接）。

Conclusion: TARCAT为分析人类工作与开发通用施工机器人提供了共享词汇表，可用于组织示范数据、明确机器人能力需求和支持编码智能体对技能库的检索与扩展。

Abstract: Recent vision-language-action models offer a path toward robots with broader repertoires than conventional task-specific systems. Construction deployment, however, requires a precise inventory of worker activities and the capabilities needed to execute them. We present TARCAT, an occupation-grounded taxonomy derived from 91 O*NET tasks across seven high-employment construction occupations and 30 instructional videos of physical work. TARCAT defines 41 action primitives in 12 groups and three classes and provides a mechanism for composing parameterized primitive sequences into reusable skills. This human-interpretable structure can organize demonstrations, specify robot requirements, and support coding agents that retrieve and extend skill libraries. We also demonstrate selected primitives on a DOBOT CR3 arm with a CRAFT hand. TARCAT thereby provides a common vocabulary for analyzing human work and developing general-purpose construction robots. Annotations are available at https://github.com/AICPS/TARCAT-Taxonomy.

</details>


### [218] [LAC: Linear and Angular Compliance for Humanoid Whole-body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25405)
*Yang Liu,Zhongkai Gu,Wei Zhu,Mitsuhiro Hayashibe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LAC是一个全身控制器，通过数据增强和教师-学生强化学习，同时实现人体上半身受到外力作用时的线性和角向柔顺响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的人形机器人任务涉及与物体和人类的物理交互，但现有控制器要么将外力视为干扰而拒绝，要么仅限制在特定身体部位的柔顺性并忽略角效应，缺乏能够同时处理线性与角向柔顺的通用全身控制方案。

Method: 首先，将从人类交互数据中提取的接触帧上施加采样的力和力偶事件，合成大规模增强数据集；在每个接触环节，在指令刚度下，外力与被动屈从运动链产生的虚拟力矩驱动虚拟导纳；随后通过教师-学生强化学习训练单一策略来跟踪外力作用下的柔顺运动。

Result: 大量仿真和真实世界实验表明，该方法能在上半身各处实现对外部力的全身柔顺响应，在两个刚度指令的全范围内呈现单调调节，并适用于遥操作的移动操作任务。

Conclusion: LAC提供了一种通用的全身柔顺控制框架，克服了现有方法在全身性与角向柔顺上的局限，并通过统一的策略实现了对外部力和力偶的灵活响应，为真实人形机器人物理交互任务提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Real-world humanoid tasks involve physical interaction with objects and humans, yet current controllers either reject external forces as disturbances or restrict compliance to limited body links while ignoring angular effects. We present LAC, a general whole-body controller that simultaneously realizes commanded Linear and Angular Compliance for wrenches applied to the upper body. First, we synthesize whole-body compliant responses into a large-scale augmented dataset. Sampled force and couple events are imposed on contact frames extracted from human interaction data. At each contact link, the external force and a virtual torque from the passively yielding kinematic chain drive a virtual admittance under the commanded stiffness. Subsequently, teacher-student reinforcement learning trains a single policy to track the compliant motions under external wrenches. Finally, extensive simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate whole-body compliant responses to wrenches across the upper body, monotonic modulation over the full range of both stiffness commands, and applicability to teleoperated loco-manipulation tasks. Project website: https://lac-humanoid.github.io/

</details>


### [219] [SUPER ODOMETRY 2.0: Resilient Odometry via Hierarchical Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25427)
*Shibo Zhao,Sifan Zhou,Yuchen Zhang,Ji Zhang,Chen Wang,Wenshan Wang,Sebastian Scherer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Super Odometry，一个自适应的多传感器融合框架，通过分层架构集成四个核心模块（自适应特征选择、自适应状态方向选择、自适应引擎选择以及基于学习的惯性里程计），能够在烟雾、沙尘暴、雪地、低光等严重环境退化条件下保持鲁棒的里程计性能。该系统在200公里和800小时的多平台机器人验证中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有里程计系统在严重的传感器退化（如烟雾、沙尘暴、雪地、低光条件）和极端环境下往往难以正常工作，威胁自主机器人的安全性和功能性。因此需要一种能够动态适应不同退化程度的传感器融合框架。

Method: 采用分层结构集成四个核心模块：1) 自适应特征选择（低层级适应性）；2) 自适应状态方向选择；3) 自适应引擎选择；4) 基于学习的惯性里程计（高层级适应性）。该惯性里程计在超过100小时异构机器人平台数据上训练，并将IMU提升至与相机和LiDAR同等重要的地位，作为外部传感器失效时的可靠后备方案。

Result: 在200公里、800小时的运行中，分别部署于空中、轮式、足式机器人平台上，涵盖了多种传感器配置、环境退化条件和激进运动模式，均验证了系统有效性。

Conclusion: Super Odometry标志着向全退化环境下安全、长期机器人自主性的重要一步，能在各种极端环境条件下保持可靠里程计性能。

Abstract: Resilient and robust odometry is crucial for autonomous systems operating in complex and dynamic environments. Existing odometry systems often struggle with severe sensory degradations and extreme conditions such as smoke, sandstorms, snow, or low-light conditions, threatening both the safety and functionality of robots. To address these challenges, we present Super Odometry, a sensor fusion framework that dynamically adapts to varying levels of environmental degradation. Super Odometry employs a hierarchical structure to integrate four core modules from lower-level to higher-level adaptability including adaptive feature selection, adaptive state direction selection, adaptive engine selection, and a novel learning- based inertial odometry. The inertial odometry, trained on over 100 hours of heterogeneous robotic platforms, captures comprehensive motion dynamics. Super Odometry elevates the inertial measurement unit (IMU) to equal importance with camera and LiDAR within the sensor fusion framework, providing a reliable fallback when exteroceptive sensors fail. Super Odometry has been validated across 200 kilometers and 800 operational hours on a fleet of aerial, wheeled, and legged robots, under diverse sensor configurations, environmental degradation, and aggressive motion profiles. It marks an important step towards safe and long-term robotic autonomy in all-degraded environments.

</details>


### [220] [Towards safe and optimal flight: Viability Kernel MPC for Fully Actuated Multirotor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25459)
*Massimiliano Bertoni,Alberto Piccina,Gianni Lunardi,Elias Fontanari,Andrea Del Prete,Angelo Cenedese,Giulia Michieletto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在MPC框架内为全驱动多旋翼生成安全位姿轨迹的方法，结合生存性理论和数据驱动方法，通过动态计算的轴对齐包围盒实现避障，并在倾斜六旋翼仿真中验证了实时性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业空中机器人导航需要在非结构化环境中提供安全保证，同时优化性能和计算效率，现有方法往往依赖详尽的离线可达性分析，计算成本高且难以满足实时需求。

Method: 在模型预测控制（MPC）框架内，融合生存性理论（viability theory）与数据驱动方法生成安全位姿轨迹；通过动态计算的轴对齐包围盒（AABB）强制执行避障约束，提供形式化安全保证，无需进行穷举离线可达性分析。

Result: 在倾斜六旋翼（tilted hexarotor）上的数值仿真验证了方法的有效性，表明该方法能在杂乱环境中成功完成导航任务，并具备实时计算性能。

Conclusion: 该方法在保证形式化安全性的同时实现了实时计算能力，为工业空中机器人在非结构化环境中的安全导航提供了一种高效可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Industrial aerial robotics demands safety guarantees for navigation in unstructured environments while optimizing performance and computational efficiency. This paper presents a method for generating safe pose trajectories for fully actuated multirotors within a Model Predictive Control (MPC) framework, leveraging both viability theory and data-driven methods. Obstacle avoidance is enforced through dynamically computed axis-aligned bounding boxes, providing formal safety guarantees without exhaustive offline reachability analysis. Numerical simulations on a fully actuated tilted hexarotor validate the approach, demonstrating successful navigation in cluttered environments with real-time computational performance.

</details>


### [221] [Transient multimode heat transfer of an industrial automated tape laying process under rapidly changing conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25470)
*Bernhard Rameder,Hubert Gattringer,Andreas Müller,Ronald Naderer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向工业自动铺带（ATL）工艺的瞬态热传递模型，通过解析视角因子方法修正辐射热流的系统高估，并利用混合对流（Richardson数）提升不同工艺速度下的精度，在工业产线上验证的整体偏差仅1.08%（NRMSE）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统复合材料制造中的热模型存在局限，特别是采用1.5D简化时会对辐射热流产生系统性高估，导致热状态估计精度不足，进而影响原位固结质量和最终零件品质。

Method: 建立求解热导方程的瞬态热模型，耦合平流、导热、对流和辐射；创新性地采用解析视角因子方法考虑有限发射器和胶带宽度以修正辐射热流；引入基于Richardson数的局部混合对流评估；将ATL系统建模为移动胶带基材和红外热源两个交互子系统；胶带采用两节点模型解析热面与监测面之间的物理相位差；采用高阶隐式积分方案的单片式求解策略保障高动态下的数值稳定性。

Result: 在工业ATL产线上进行验证，在快速速度和电流调制条件下，模型预测的整体偏差仅1.08%（NRMSE）。

Conclusion: 该框架为热状态估计提供了高保真、基于物理的基础，支持一致的原位固结并提升零件质量，弥补了传统热模型在精度和稳健性方面的不足。

Abstract: This work presents a transient heat-transfer model of an industrial automated tape laying (ATL) process designed to overcome the limitations of conventional thermal models in composite manufacturing. The model solves the heat-conduction equation with coupled advection, conduction, convection, and radiation. A key innovation is the implementation of an analytical view factor approach that accounts for finite emitter and tape widths, thereby correcting systematic overestimations of radiative heat flux inherent in 1.5D simplifications. Furthermore, a local convection assessment incorporates mixed convection effects characterized by the Richardson number, ensuring accuracy across a wide range of process speeds. The ATL system is represented by two interacting subsystems: the moving tape substrate and the infrared heat sources. The tape is discretized using a two-node model that resolves the physical phase shift between the heated and monitored surfaces. Numerical stability under high dynamics is ensured by a monolithic solution strategy using a high-order implicit integration scheme. Model predictions were validated on an industrial ATL line, demonstrating an overall deviation of only 1.08% (NRMSE) under rapid velocity and current modulations. This framework provides a high-fidelity, physics-based foundation for thermal state estimation, supporting consistent in-situ consolidation and improved part quality.

</details>


### [222] [Dynamic Modeling of a Welding Torch Umbilical and Its Impact on Robot Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25509)
*Nicolas Gautier,Yves Guillermit,Mathieu Porez,Fabien Rousset,Damien Chablat*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种焊接脐带缆（umbilical）的约束多体动力学模型，将其表示为通过具有弹性和耗散效应的被动关节连接的刚性体串联链，并通过运动学约束引入远端锚点的指定运动，将动力学投影到允许速度子空间上进行降维求解，显式恢复脐带缆对机器人的反作用力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着轻量化和协作机器人在工业中的广泛部署，焊接机器人外部脐带缆的动态影响会显著影响机器人运动和驱动力，因此需要建立有效的脐带缆动力学模型来分析和补偿这种影响。

Method: 将焊接脐带缆建模为一系列刚性体通过带弹性和耗散效应的被动关节串联而成的多体系统；通过完整运动学约束引入远端锚点的指定运动；将动力学方程投影到允许速度子空间上，得到无需拉格朗日乘子的高效公式化表达；显式恢复脐带缆对机器人施加的反作用力（wrench）。

Result: 通过一个平面案例研究验证了所提出的建模和求解方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 该约束多体动力学模型能够高效地描述焊接脐带缆的动态行为，并显式计算其对机器人的反作用力，为轻量化/协作焊接机器人的运动控制和力补偿提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Robotic welding is widely used in industrial manufacturing, where the welding torch is often connected to the generator through an external umbilical. With the increasing deployment of lightweight and collaborative robots, the dynamic influence of this umbilical can significantly affect the robot motion and the actuation forces. This paper proposes a constrained multibody dynamic model of a welding umbilical, represented as a serial chain of rigid bodies interconnected by passive joints with elastic and dissipative effects. Prescribed motions at the distal anchor point are introduced through holonomic kinematic constraints. The equations of motion are reduced by projecting the dynamics onto the subspace of admissible velocities, yielding an efficient formulation free of Lagrange multipliers. The reaction wrench exerted by the umbilical on the robot is explicitly recovered. A planar case study illustrates the approach.

</details>


### [223] [A Tendon-Driven Five-Fingered Hand with Distributed Tactile Perception for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25547)
*Huayang Chen,Longhui Qin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种具有分布式触觉感知的腱驱动五指灵巧手，采用软硬混合结构兼顾柔顺性与操作力，五指远节和中节指骨分布双模态触觉传感元件，可同时检测静态接触和动态力变化，并通过计数手势、指-拇指捏持、物体抓取和抓瓶触觉记录等实验验证了驱动-感知集成系统的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将具身人工智能技术应用于人形机器人进行复杂操作时，灵巧手是核心限制因素，其性能受制于灵巧性和触觉感知能力的不足。现有灵巧手难以同时满足柔顺性、操作力和触觉感知的需求。

Method: 提出一种腱驱动的五指灵巧手新型设计方案，采用软硬混合结构以兼顾柔顺性与操作力；在全部五指的远节和中节指骨上分布双模态触觉传感元件，实现对静态接触和动态力变化的同时检测，构建驱动-感知集成系统。

Result: 通过计数手势、指-拇指捏持、物体抓取以及抓瓶触觉记录等操作实验，验证了所提出的驱动-感知集成系统的可行性和有效性。

Conclusion: 软硬混合结构结合分布式双模态触觉感知的腱驱动五指灵巧手，能够为复杂操纵任务提供所需的柔顺性、操作力与触觉反馈，为具身智能在人形机器人灵巧操作方面的应用提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: To apply the techniques of embodied artificial intelligence to human-oid robots for complex manipulations, dexterous robotic hands are indispensable, which are restricted by the dexterity and tactile perception capability. In this work, we proposed a novel design of tendon-driven five-fingered hand with dis-tributed tactile perception. With a soft-rigid-hybrid structure employed, both compliance and operational force are endowed to the hand. Dual-modality tactile sensing elements are distributed on the distal and middle phalanges of all five fingers, enabling the simultaneous detection of static contact and dynamic force variations. Manipulation experiments, including counting gestures, finger-to-thumb pinching, object grasping, and bottle-grasp tactile recording, demonstrate the feasibility of the integrated actuation-perception system.

</details>


### [224] [ConfAL-WM: Confidence-Guided Active Learning for Action-Conditioned World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25572)
*Xiang Liu,Sen Cui,Changshui Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ConfAL-WM，一种基于置信度引导的主动学习框架，用于对具身世界模型（EVAC）进行后训练，通过在潜在空间中预测密集置信度图并聚合为任务、帧和补丁级别分数，实现高效数据选择与局部训练增强，在RoboTwin2.0上提升了后训练效率和预测质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作条件下的世界模型是具身预测、规划与合成数据生成的重要基础，但在新任务和新场景分布下，其误差往往集中在局部的时空区域（如机械臂、被操作物体、接触区域、遮挡物体等）。如何在有限的预算下针对这些关键区域进行高效的后训练，是一个亟待解决的问题。

Method: 基于EVAC世界模型，在UNet解码器特征上附加轻量级置信度探针，在潜在空间预测密集置信度图，并将其聚合为任务级、帧级和补丁级三种粒度的分数。训练流程分为三步：（1）用少量目标域数据重训置信度探针并预热EVAC；（2）进行任务级预筛选以分配采样预算；（3）对选定数据进行重训练，并可选用帧级或补丁级加权增强。

Result: 在RoboTwin2.0上的实验表明：置信度引导的数据选择提升了后训练效率；与标量奖励、进度和基于评判器的评分基线相比，密集的帧级和补丁级加权进一步提升了预测质量与具身轨迹一致性。

Conclusion: ConfAL-WM证明了置信度引导的主动学习能够有效提升具身世界模型的后训练效率与预测质量，尤其通过局部（帧/补丁级）的密集加权增强了关键时空区域的建模能力。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models have become an important foundation for embodied prediction, planning, and synthetic data generation, but their errors under new task and scene distributions are often concentrated in localized spatiotemporal regions such as robot arms, manipulated objects, contact areas, and occluded objects. This paper presents ConfAL-WM, a confidence-guided active learning framework for post-training embodied world models. Built upon EVAC, we attach a lightweight confidence probe to UNet decoder features and predict dense confidence maps in the latent space. These maps are aggregated into task-, frame-, and patch-level scores, enabling both efficient data selection and localized training enhancement. Our pipeline first retrains the confidence probe and warms up EVAC with a small subset of target-domain data, then performs task-level prescreening to allocate sampling budgets, and finally applies selected-data retraining with optional frame or patch weighted data enhancement. Experiments on RoboTwin2.0 show that confidence-guided selection improves post-training efficiency, while dense frame and patch weighting further enhances prediction quality and embodied trajectory consistency compared with scalar reward, progress, and judge-based scoring baselines. A quick visual overview of this work is available at https://ConfAL-WM.github.io.

</details>


### [225] [RA-VLA: Retrieval-Augmented VLA for Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25585)
*Sanghwan Jang,Minjin Jeon,Minsoo Kim,Seongjin Choi,Dongha Kim,Hwanjo Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RA-VLA，一个检索增强的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）框架，通过行为对齐的上下文检索和接地执行流水线，解决VLA模型在新任务分布下的脆弱性问题，在不训练的条件下实现高效的机器人操作适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在通用机器人操作中表现优异，但在面对新任务分布时表现出显著的脆弱性（brittleness）。虽然上下文模仿学习（ICIL）提供了一种免训练的替代方案，但现有框架存在适应瓶颈（adaptation bottleneck），难以将专家上下文有效转化为可执行动作。这一瓶颈源于浅层的检索机制和固有的行为惯性（behavioral inertia），使策略过度锚定在预训练先验上。

Method: RA-VLA框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 行为对齐的上下文检索（behavior-aligned context retrieval），用于获取与当前任务功能语义匹配的专家示范；(2) 接地执行流水线（grounded execution pipeline），确保模型对功能线索的忠实遵循。整体采用可扩展架构设计，在保持推理效率的同时实现任务适应。

Result: 在LIBERO基准测试和真实世界UR5e机器人环境上的实验表明，RA-VLA在成功率和计算效率上均优于现有方法，实现了优越的任务适应性能。

Conclusion: RA-VLA为免训练的机器人适应建立了一个稳健的框架，通过解决检索浅层性和行为惯性问题，有效提升了VLA模型在新任务分布下的泛化能力与部署效率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models provide a versatile foundation for general robotic manipulation, yet they exhibit significant brittleness when confronted with novel task distributions. While In-Context Imitation Learning (ICIL) offers a training-free alternative, existing frameworks suffer from an adaptation bottleneck that hinders the effective translation of expert context to executable actions. This failure originates from superficial retrieval mechanisms and an inherent behavioral inertia that anchors the policy to its pre-trained priors. To address these limitations, we present RA-VLA, a retrieval-augmented VLA framework that integrates behavior-aligned context retrieval with a grounded execution pipeline. By enforcing faithful adherence to functional cues within a scalable architecture, RA-VLA facilitates seamless task adaptation while preserving inference efficiency. Our empirical evaluations across the LIBERO benchmark and a real-world UR5e environment demonstrate that RA-VLA achieves superior success rates and computational efficiency, establishing a robust framework for training-free robotic adaptation.

</details>


### [226] [Advantage-Driven Explicit Memory for Social Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25610)
*Yeonsoo Park,Mattia Racca,Guillaume Bono,Steeven Janny,Gianluca Monaci,Tomi Silander,Christian Wolf*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种配备非参数显式记忆的社交导航智能体，通过在策略网络之外显式索引导致关键事件的先前步骤，将部分行为外包给显式记忆，从而缓解参数化策略表示学习的负担，并在部署期间持续收集数据以提升对分布外（OOD）情形的泛化能力，特别改善了对稀疏高成本失败（如人类碰撞）的保留与应对，同时部分缓解仿真到现实的迁移（sim-to-real）问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典模仿学习或强化学习将智能体行为完全存储在策略网络参数中，对表示学习算法造成沉重负担；且社交导航中存在稀疏、高成本的关键失败事件（如人类碰撞）难以通过参数化策略有效捕捉和保留，同时训练环境与真实部署间存在分布偏移。

Method: 提出带有非参数显式记忆的导航智能体，该记忆显式索引导致关键事件的先前步骤。利用RL智能体的优势信号来挖掘稀有高影响事件；将显式记忆集成进循环PPO（recurrent PPO）架构中，使用隐藏状态进行记忆检索以捕捉连续时空动态；允许智能体在部署期间的测试回合中收集数据，实现一种持续学习；在仿真中结合照片级逼真渲染和非视觉人群模拟进行训练。

Result: 实验表明该方法提升了智能体保留稀疏高代价失败（如人类碰撞）的能力，对分布外的社会行为具有鲁棒性，并部分解决了sim-to-real差距问题，因为部分决策基于真实数据。

Conclusion: 非参数显式记忆与参数化策略结合的方式有效缓解了表示学习负担，通过外包行为到显式记忆和部署期间持续学习，显著增强了智能体在OOD场景下的泛化能力与对稀有高影响事件的处理能力。

Abstract: Robot policies are predominantly learned with classical parametric variants of imitation learning or RL, where training stores the agent's behavior exclusively in the policy's network parameters, putting a heavy burden on the representation learning algorithm. We propose a new navigation agent equipped with non-parametric memory which explicitly indexes prior steps leading to critical events. The advantages are twofold: first, it allows the policy to outsource some of its behavior into an explicit memory; second, it encourages a form of continual learning by allowing an agent to collect data from its testing episodes during deployment and therefore to better generalize to OOD situations. In the context of social navigation, we show that this improves the agent's capability to retain sparse, high-cost failures, such as human collisions. If the policy is trained in simulation, this also naturally addresses the sim-to-real gap, partially, by basing some of the decision making on real data. We integrate the explicit memory into a recurrent PPO architecture and use hidden states for memory retrieval to capture continuous spatiotemporal dynamics. The goal of exploiting rare, high-impact events is achieved by leveraging the RL agent's advantage signals. We train our agent in simulation with a combination of photorealistic rendering and non-visual crowd simulation and show that the agent is robust with respect to OOD social behavior.

</details>


### [227] [Leveraging Inter-object Affordances for Efficient Planning in Contact-rich Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25641)
*Pouya P. Niaz,Justus Piater,Alejandro Agostini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出U-TAMP方法，利用视觉语言模型生成对象间可操作性（affordance）抽象表征，以处理接触密集型机器人任务中异构对象的物理交互约束，显著提升规划成功率和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统TAMP方法在真实世界中应用受限，因为其通常假设简化对象模型，忽略对接触密集型任务执行至关重要的物理属性；此外，运动规划中常使用亚符号推理，导致规划时间剧增且成功率降低。

Method: 提出Unified TAMP (U-TAMP)方法，核心思想是定义以对象为中心的抽象执行约束。利用视觉语言模型(VLM)生成对象间可操作性的抽象表征，刻画抓取和支撑等物理交互约束，用于丰富U-TAMP的规划领域，以处理具有可变物理属性的对象。

Result: 在模拟厨房桌面整理场景中实验验证，与原始U-TAMP及最先进的基于VLM的规划器相比，该方法显著提高了规划成功率，并将规划时间提升1到2个数量级。

Conclusion: 通过VLM生成的对象级物理交互约束抽象，可以有效弥补传统TAMP在异构对象物理属性建模方面的不足，在保证高成功率的同时大幅提升规划效率。

Abstract: Traditional task-and-motion planning (TAMP) approaches primarily focus on defining sequences of actions along with the necessary geometric and kinematic constraints to execute long-horizon tasks. However, their applicability in real-world settings is limited, as they typically assume simplified object models that overlook key physical properties critical for the successful execution of contact-rich tasks. Moreover, they often use sub-symbolic reasoning during motion planning, which drastically increases planning time and decreases overall success rates. We propose a method that leverages a TAMP approach, defining object-centric abstractions of execution constraints, called Unified TAMP (U-TAMP), to execute robotic tasks involving interactions among objects with heterogeneous shapes, sizes, and materials. Using a Vision-Language Model (VLM), we generate abstractions of inter-object affordances for characterizing physical interaction constraints between objects in contact-rich tasks, such as grasp and support constraints. These constraints are used to enrich the U-TAMP planning domain to deal with objects with variable physical properties. We perform experiments in simulated kitchen table organization scenarios and compare our results with those of the original U-TAMP, as well as a state-of-the-art VLM-based planner that leverages common sense knowledge of objects' affordances for plan generation. Our approach achieves significantly higher planning success rates and improves planning times by one to two orders of magnitude compared to other methods.

</details>


### [228] [EgoNav: Bridging Learned Waypoints and Geometry-Aware Local Control for Robust Indoor Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25642)
*Jing Wang,Shiqi Zhao,Hairong Qu,Peng Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoNav是一个分层导航系统，基于轻量拓扑地图做图像目标导航，通过从语义分割的可通行区域生成候选航点并结合学习到的航点进行几何安全、方向一致性和先验保真度评分，再用自适应局部路径规划器执行，在仿真和实体人形机器人上均优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于轻量拓扑地图的图像目标导航系统中，学习到的航点预测器可能产生违反几何约束或偏离全局路径的目标点；同时执行这些航点需要具备避障能力的局部规划器，但现有系统要么缺少局部规划器，要么依赖无法适应狭窄空间的固定参数。

Method: EgoNav采用分层架构：(1) 从语义分割的可通行区域生成候选航点；(2) 将候选航点与学习到的航点进行评分，综合考虑几何安全性、方向一致性和对学习先验的保真度；(3) 使用自适应局部路径规划器执行精炼后的航点，并根据精炼结果动态调整规划器参数。

Result: 在Habitat-sim仿真环境和物理人形机器人上的实验表明，EgoNav在成功率与路径效率两方面均一致地优于当代基线方法。

Conclusion: EgoNav通过将学习航点预测器的导航直觉与语义分割候选生成、自适应局部规划相结合，在保留学习先验的同时有效解决了几何约束违反和狭窄空间适配问题，验证了该分层方案在实际部署中的有效性。

Abstract: Image-goal navigation using lightweight topological maps is a practical paradigm for indoor robot deployment: the map requires only geotagged images, and localization relies on visual matching rather than precise pose estimation. However, learned waypoint predictors can produce targets that violate geometric constraints or deviate from the global path. Executing these waypoints safely further requires a local planner capable of collision avoidance, yet existing systems either lack one or rely on fixed parameters that cannot adapt to confined spaces. To address these limitations while retaining the navigational intuition of the learned predictor, we present EgoNav, a hierarchical system that implements this idea by generating candidates from semantically segmented traversable regions and scoring them alongside the learned waypoint for geometric safety, directional coherence, and fidelity to the learned prior. An adaptive local path planner then executes the refined waypoint with parameters modulated based on the refinement outcome. Experiments in Habitat-sim and on a physical humanoid robot show that EgoNav consistently outperforms contemporary baselines in both success rate and path efficiency.

</details>


### [229] [GaussianDream++: Efficient 3D Gaussian World Modeling for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25659)
*Yuqing Jiang,Zijian Zhang,Weitao Zhou,Jiawei Wang,Junjie He,Lei Yang,Haifang Qing,Si Liu,Ding Zhao,Ping Luo,Haibao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GaussianDream方法，通过在VLA主干中直接插入世界状态令牌和世界预测令牌，配合仅训练阶段使用的世界表示头，将当前高斯重建与未来高斯预测作为3D监督信号，提升策略的3D结构化感知与物理动态建模能力。推理时移除所有辅助模块，仅保留20个世界令牌，实现高效闭环控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略在语言条件机器人操控中取得了进展，但动作模仿目标仅提供对度量3D结构和短期物理演化的弱监督。现有方法要么只增强当前场景的3D接地，要么在RGB或潜在空间建模未来动态且部署开销大，需要一种既提供3D监督又保持推理高效的机制。

Method: 将世界状态令牌和世界预测令牌直接插入VLA主干；训练时用世界表示头将这些令牌解码为当前世界与耦合的未来预测（基于共享高斯基元）；通过静态-动态分解保持持久结构并聚焦交互相关区域的残差运动；推理时移除头、渲染器、辅助目标和VGGT/TGE路径，仅保留20个世界令牌。

Result: 在LIBERO上达到98.6%，在LIBERO-Plus上达到87.8%，在相机和布局偏移下取得明显增益；真实机器人实验中平均成功率从基线π_{0.5}的29.2%提升至52.5%，同时保持高效闭环控制。

Conclusion: 训练时的当前高斯重建与未来高斯预测可作为有效的3D监督信号，且通过紧凑的策略原生令牌设计，可以在推理时实现零额外解码开销的高效部署，显著提升VLA在分布偏移下的鲁棒性和真实机器人操控成功率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies have advanced language-conditioned robotic manipulation, yet action-imitation objectives provide only weak supervision for metric 3D structure and short-horizon physical evolution. Geometry-enhanced policies mainly improve current-scene grounding, whereas predictive policies often model future dynamics in RGB or latent spaces and may incur substantial deployment cost. GaussianDream demonstrates that training-time current Gaussian reconstruction and future Gaussian prediction provide effective 3D supervision, but its dense VGGT/TGE-based prefix jointly carries state, dynamics, and action-conditioning information. We present \textbf{\methodname}, a compact, policy-native extension that inserts \textbf{World State Tokens} and \textbf{World Prediction Tokens} directly into the VLA backbone. A training-only \textbf{World Representation Head} decodes these tokens into a Current World and coupled Future Prediction over shared Gaussian primitives, while static--dynamic factorization preserves persistent structure and focuses residual motion on interaction-relevant regions. At inference, the head, renderer, auxiliary objectives, and VGGT/TGE pathway are removed, leaving only 20 world tokens without online Gaussian decoding or rollout. \method achieves \textbf{98.6\%} on LIBERO and \textbf{87.8\%} on LIBERO-Plus, with clear gains under Camera and Layout shifts. Real-robot experiments further improve average success from 29.2\% to 52.5\% over reproduced $π_{0.5}$ while maintaining efficient closed-loop control.

</details>


### [230] [PRISM: Projection-Integrated Sampling-Based MPC with Bayesian Cost Tuning for Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25666)
*Alinjar Dan,Iryna Hurova,Karl Kruusamäe,Arun Kumar Singh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PRISM是一个投影集成的采样型模型预测控制（MPC）框架，用于复杂双机械臂操作。它利用GPU加速物理仿真器作为在线世界模型，通过QP引导的控制采样策略将轨迹探索与运动学可行性解耦，实现实时或近实时的双臂协调操作，并在仿真和真实UR5e机械臂上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在杂乱、接触密集的环境中，双臂操作仍然具有挑战性，因为它需要协调的运动生成、交互感知的规划以及在紧密运动学约束下的可靠执行。

Method: 提出PRISM框架，核心包括：（1）QP引导的控制采样策略，将采样的关节速度轨迹投影到满足位置、速度、加速度和加加速度界限以及初始速度边界条件的运动集合上；（2）自定义ADMM/Bregman-splitting QP求解器，利用关节独立可分离性和可复用矩阵分解实现高效在线求解；（3）使用贝叶斯优化离线调整任务成本权重，减少手动参数选择。整体以GPU加速物理仿真器作为在线世界模型进行rollout评估。

Result: 在PerAct^2任务的多个变体（障碍约束球运输、托盘运输、立方体交接和箱子搬运）上评估，相比代表性采样型基线方法，PRISM展现出更强的鲁棒性和更高的任务成功率，同时保持实时或近实时执行。在双UR5e机械臂上成功实现仿真到真机的迁移。

Conclusion: PRISM展示了基于物理仿真在线规划在接触密集双臂操作中的实际应用潜力，通过投影策略在保证运动学可行性的同时实现广泛探索，为双臂操作提供了一种高效且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Bimanual manipulation in cluttered, contact-rich environments remains challenging because it requires coordinated motion generation, interaction-aware planning, and reliable execution under tight kinematic constraints. We present PRISM, a projection-integrated sampling-based Model Predictive Control (MPC) framework that uses a GPU-accelerated physics simulator as an online world model for complex dual-arm manipulation.
  The main algorithmic contribution is a QP-guided control sampling strategy that decouples trajectory exploration from kinematic feasibility. At each MPC step, sampled joint-velocity trajectories are projected onto the set of motions satisfying joint position, velocity, acceleration, and jerk bounds, together with an initial-velocity boundary condition, before rollout evaluation. This enables broad yet feasible exploration of coordinated bimanual behaviors. To support efficient online execution, we derive a custom ADMM/Bregman-splitting QP solver that exploits joint-wise separability and reusable matrix factorizations. We further use Bayesian optimization to tune task-cost weights offline, reducing manual parameter selection.
  We evaluate PRISM on challenging variants of PerAct$^{2}$ tasks, including obstacle-constrained ball transport, tray transport, cube handover, and box lifting. Experiments show improved robustness and task success relative to representative sampling-based baselines, while maintaining real-time or near-real-time execution. We also demonstrate successful sim-to-real transfer on dual UR5e manipulators, highlighting the practical potential of physics-based online planning for contact-rich bimanual manipulation. Project details, including code and supplementary videos, are available at \href{https://sites.google.com/view/prismbimanual}{\texttt{https://sites.google.com/view/prismbimanual}}.

</details>


### [231] [Opportunities of Self Supervised Learning for GNSS: Evaluation of a Deep Learning-Enhanced PVT Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25674)
*Thomas Barbero,Bertrand Ekambi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种深度学习增强的PVT（位置、速度、时间）算法，用于缓解密集城区多路径干扰。通过监督学习联合预测距离修正和不确定性，并利用基于JEPA的自监督预训练提升表征质量，在多样驾驶场景下显著提升PVT精度，尤其在未见过的恶劣城市场景中表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集城区中多路径干扰严重影响GNSS定位精度，传统PVT算法难以有效缓解。现有方法缺乏对未标注数据的利用，泛化能力有限。作者旨在通过深度学习与自监督预训练相结合，提升恶劣城市场景下的定位精度与泛化性能。

Method: 提出深度增强的PVT算法，包含两个关键组件：(1) 监督学习目标同时预测距离修正量及其不确定性；(2) 基于JEPA（联合嵌入预测架构）的自监督预训练阶段，利用未标注GNSS数据提升表征质量，增强模型对未知场景的泛化能力。

Result: 在多样化的驾驶场景数据集上评估，算法显著改善了PVT精度，特别是在未见过的恶劣城市场景中，性能提升尤为明显。

Conclusion: 研究证明了利用未标注GNSS数据进行自监督预训练可以有效提升深度学习模型在GNSS多路径误差缓解任务中的泛化性能，为密集城区环境下的高精度定位开辟了新途径。

Abstract: This work proposes a Deep Learning Enhanced PVT algorithm to mitigate multipath interference in dense urban areas. A supervised objective jointly predicts range corrections and uncertainty, while a JEPA-based self-supervised pretraining stage improves representation quality. The algorithm is evaluated over diverse driving scenarios, substantially improving PVT accuracy, particularly for unseen harsh urban conditions. These results highlight the potential of unlabelled GNSS data to improve generalization performance.

</details>


### [232] [LM-X: Explainable Action Modeling with Progress, Event, and Uncertainty Prediction for Generalist Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25757)
*Jin Lou,Jingxuan Zhu,Andong Chen,Xupeng Wang,Yuan Xu,Yuexuan Li,Xingdong Zhu,Zhijie Zhu,Yingwei Ji,Wenpeng Nie,Jingyi Li,Liangliang Chen,Jinyan Liu,Zhiqi Song,Jidong Zhang,Hongming Li,Yuchen Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LM-X框架，通过在任务、事件和运动三个时间尺度上显式监督预测信号（RTG、ETG和异方差动作流）来增强通用视觉-语言-行动（VLA）策略，使解释性内生于控制过程而非事后生成。在20,000+小时真实机器人轨迹上训练后，LM-X在50个随机困难RoboTwin2.0任务上达到74.1%成功率（对比GR00T N1.7的55.4%），在7个真实机器人任务上达到68.6%（对比50.7%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用VLA策略主要通过短期动作预测来学习长期行为，存在两个耦合瓶颈：单个动作目标必须隐式吸收任务进度、中间意图和局部可靠性等多种信息，而这些控制状态在执行过程中是隐藏的。受生物感觉运动控制的功能原理启发，作者希望将多尺度预测状态显式化，以增强控制并同时提供可解释的内部估计。

Method: LM-X在任务、事件和运动三个尺度上组织预测，不声称具有解剖对应性。在线发射三种显式监督信号并直接调节动作生成：(1) return-to-go (RTG)衡量可见任务进度；(2) event-to-go (ETG)识别下一个语义转换；(3) 异方差动作流通过传播方差估计局部可靠性。训练流程包括：先在5个任务上进行受控预训练门控验证设计（完整模型比纯动作基线提升16.0个百分点，比最强单头变体提升10.8个百分点），再在20,000+小时真实机器人轨迹（包含1,000+小时失败策略回放）上训练。

Result: LM-X在50个随机困难RoboTwin2.0任务上达到74.1%成功率，对比GR00T N1.7的55.4%；在7个真实机器人任务上达到68.6%，对比50.7%。RTG能追踪语义进度和可见回归，而方差在犹豫和振荡控制期间上升，表明模型能反映内部状态的不确定性。

Conclusion: 显式的多时间尺度预测状态可以增强控制性能，同时暴露可解释的内部估计。LM-X证明将解释内生于控制过程（而非事后生成）是可行且有效的，为通用VLA策略的设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Generalist vision--language--action (VLA) policies learn long-horizon behavior mainly through short-horizon action prediction and reveal little beyond sampled commands. This creates two coupled bottlenecks: a single action target must implicitly absorb task progress, intermediate intent, and local reliability, while these control states remain hidden during execution. Inspired by functional principles of biological sensorimotor control, we introduce LM-X , which organizes prediction across task, event, and motor scales without claiming anatomical correspondence. Three explicitly supervised signals are emitted online and directly condition action generation: return-to-go (RTG) measures visible task progress, event-to-go (ETG) identifies the next semantic transition, and heteroscedastic action flow estimates local reliability through propagated variance. Explanation is therefore intrinsic to control rather than generated post hoc.
