Before a costly 20-day pretraining run on 64 NVIDIA B200 GPUs, a controlled five-task pretraining gate verifies the design: the complete model improves success by 16.0 points over the action-only backbone and by 10.8 points over the strongest single-head variant. We then train LM-X on more than 20,000 hours of real-robot trajectories, including over 1,000 hours of failed policy rollouts. LM-X achieves 74.1\% across 50 randomized-hard RoboTwin2.0 tasks versus 55.4\% for GR00T N1.7, and 68.6\% versus 50.7\% across seven real-robot tasks. RTG tracks semantic progress and visible regression, while variance rises during hesitation and oscillatory control. These results show that explicit multi-timescale predictive state can strengthen control while exposing interpretable internal estimates.

</details>


### [233] [TacForcing: Streaming Action Generation with Execution-Time Tactile Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25798)
*Jianbo Zhou,Boyuan Zhao,Yuzheng Zhang,Yiyang Chen,Wenxin Chen,Qiuyue Li,Xiangyang Gu,Yuhan Cao,Xiao Xia,Yanzhe Hu,Zhijie Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TacForcing，一种流式动作生成框架，通过在执行过程中引入触觉反馈来增强接触丰富的操作任务。它用流式动作专家替代标准动作专家，并引入执行感知触觉注意力（EATA）来减少触觉采集与动作执行之间的时间错配，在六个模拟和三个真实世界接触丰富操作任务中分别达到65%和69%的平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于块（chunk）的视觉-语言-动作模型在执行前预测完整动作块，导致在执行过程中触觉条件信息过时。现有触觉反应式方法通常依赖单独的控制器，增加了架构和训练复杂度。因此需要一种能在执行过程中有效利用触觉反馈的流式动作生成框架。

Method: TacForcing包含两个关键设计：（1）用流式动作专家替代标准动作专家，直接基于执行过程中不断演进的触觉观测生成动作，无需额外的高频反应式控制器；（2）引入执行感知触觉注意力（EATA）机制，将触觉条件限制在即将执行的动作上，从而减少触觉采集与动作执行之间的时间不匹配。

Result: 在六个模拟UniVTAC任务上平均成功率达65%，在三个真实世界接触丰富操作任务上平均成功率达69%，均优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: TacForcing通过流式动作生成框架有效地将执行过程中的触觉反馈整合到动作生成中，避免了传统基于动作块方法的触觉信息过时问题，同时省去了独立反应式控制器的复杂度，在模拟和真实场景中均展现出优于基线的性能。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires adapting to contact states that can evolve substantially within an action horizon. However, chunk-based vision-language-action models predict complete action chunks from observations collected before execution, leaving tactile conditioning stale during execution. Existing tactile-reactive approaches typically rely on separate high-frequency controllers, which increase both architectural and training complexity. In this paper, we introduce TacForcing, a streaming action-generation framework that effectively incorporates execution-time tactile feedback. Instead of employing a separate reactive controller, TacForcing replaces the standard action expert with a streaming action expert to generate actions conditioned on the evolving tactile observations acquired during execution. TacForcing also introduces Execution-Aware Tactile Attention (EATA), which restricts tactile conditioning to actions nearing execution, thereby reducing the temporal mismatch between tactile acquisition and action execution. Across six simulated UniVTAC tasks and three real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks, TacForcing achieves average success rates of 65% and 69%, respectively, outperforming strong baselines in both settings.

</details>


### [234] [AGRO-Nav: Autonomous Graph-based Orchard Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25799)
*Ho Young Yun,Jaemin Yu,Duksu Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AGRO-Nav是一个面向果园环境的自动化全局路径规划框架，通过从SLAM点云中提取树行线构建稀疏拓扑图，结合Dijkstra搜索和Theta*任意角度路径及B样条平滑，实现无需人工航点的行中心导航，在真实果园和Isaac Sim仿真中均显著优于A*、Theta*及RANSAC基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 果园中平行的树行构成半结构化环境，形成天然驾驶通道，但行间净空狭窄、树冠密集，几何无关的网格规划器容易偏离行中心，导致碰撞树干或树冠的风险。现有方法缺乏针对果园结构的全局规划能力，需要一种利用环境结构信息的自动规划框架。

Method: 从SLAM点云中的树干聚类拟合树行线，基于这些行线构建包含行内和行间连通性的稀疏拓扑图（无需手动航点）；在该图上使用Dijkstra算法求解全局路线，起点和终点通过任意角度Theta*路径段连接，最后用三次B样条进行平滑处理。

Result: 在真实果园试验中，AGRO-Nav的行中心跟随平均误差约0.08 m，远低于A*（0.31 m）和Theta*（0.43 m）最短路径基线，且规划速度快约4至5倍；在Isaac Sim中误差最低，且在树密度降至70%时仍保持稳定，而RANSAC基线性能退化。

Conclusion: AGRO-Nav生成的轨迹为直线行中心段加受控转弯，适合差速驱动和四轮转向平台，为果园自动化导航提供了一种高效、鲁棒且无需人工干预的全局规划解决方案。

Abstract: Orchards form semi-structured environments in which parallel tree rows create natural driving corridors, yet narrow inter-row clearance and dense foliage lead geometry-agnostic grid planners to drift off the row center and risk trunk or canopy contact. We present AGRO-Nav, an automated framework for static graph-based global planning in orchards. From tree-row lines fitted to trunk clusters in a SLAM point cloud, it builds, without any manual waypoints, a sparse topological graph of intra- and inter-row connectivity; a global route is then found by Dijkstra search on this graph, connected to the start and goal by any-angle Theta* segments, and smoothed with a cubic B-spline. In real-orchard trials, AGRO-Nav follows the row center with a mean error of about 0.08 m, far below the A* (0.31 m) and Theta* (0.43 m) shortest-path baselines, while planning roughly four to five times faster. In Isaac Sim, it attains the lowest error among A*, Theta*, and a reproduced RANSAC midline baseline and remains stable as tree density drops to 70%, where the RANSAC baseline degrades. The resulting trajectories---straight row-centered segments joined by controlled turns---suit differential-drive and four-wheel-steering platforms.

</details>


### [235] [Anytime Global Tensor Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25830)
*Sai Coumar,An T. Le,Zachary Kingston*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文推广了全局张量运动规划（GTMP）方法，使其相邻层边可通过任意黑盒局部规划器实现，并提出了两种任意时刻策略：带随机重启的Anytime GTMP和带信息扩张的AO-GTMP，分别保证覆盖所有同伦类几乎必然以及收敛到最优代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GTMP此前受限于特定类型的局部规划器，无法灵活利用多种局部规划手段（如样条、采样规划、轨迹优化、生成采样等）。本文旨在推广GTMP框架，使其兼容任意黑盒局部规划器，并提供具备理论保证的任意时刻求解策略，以提升其在拓扑多样解生成和最优性方面的能力。

Method: 将GTMP推广为相邻层边由任意黑盒局部规划器实现的通用框架；提出两种任意时刻策略：(1) Anytime GTMP，采用固定预算下随机重启，几乎必然覆盖所有同伦类；(2) AO-GTMP，采用信息扩张与递增预算，收敛到最优代价。理论上证明单次采样图可覆盖所有容许δ-清晰代表的有界长度端点固定同伦类；每层额外采样使逐层遗漏概率指数下降，而更强的局部规划器仅使所需层数亚线性减少。

Result: 在操作臂（manipulation）基准上达到最先进水平；在二维导航任务中能返回批量拓扑多样的解，而基于信息的基线方法仅集中在少数（一或两类）同伦类上。

Conclusion: 推广后的GTMP框架在保持张量批处理高效性的同时，兼容任意局部规划器，既能提供拓扑多样化解集，又能通过不同任意时刻策略平衡覆盖率与最优性，具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: Global Tensor Motion Planning (GTMP) solves motion planning with batched tensor operations over a layered multipartite graph. We generalize GTMP so that adjacent-layer edges are realized by any black-box local planner (e.g., linear interpolation, splines, sampling-based planning, trajectory optimization, or generative sampling). We provide two anytime policies on top of this generalization: Anytime GTMP with random restarts at a fixed budget, which covers every homotopy class almost surely, and AO-GTMP with informed expansion with growing budgets, which converges to the optimal cost. We prove that a single sampled graph covers every endpoint-fixed homotopy class admitting a \(δ\)-clear representative of bounded length. We also prove that additional samples per layer reduce the per-layer miss probability exponentially, whereas stronger local planners reduce the required layer count only sublinearly. On manipulation benchmarks the method matches state-of-the-art performance, and on 2D navigation it returns batches of topologically diverse solutions, while the informed baselines concentrate on one or two classes.

</details>


### [236] [MA-VLA: Multi-Arm Vision-Language-Action Model for Collaboration and Compositional Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25864)
*Zaibin Zhang,Junlan Xiao,Zhongbo Zhang,Yifan Wang,Li Kang,Yiran Qin,Changxing Xia,Heng Zhou,Talas Fu,Enshen Zhou,Ruimao Zhang,Zhenfei Yin,Huchuan Lu,Lijun Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MA-VLA，一种通过原子动作分配实现多臂协作的统一框架。它将协作行为分解为中级原子提示并分配给各机械臂，支持显式子目标规范与跨任务组合复用；通过Arm Shuffle训练时置换策略实现角色无关的指令遵循，从而实现多臂组合泛化，在训练集不包含的协作模式下仍能成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在多臂协作中通常将语言视为单一全局指令，缺乏显式的臂级行为分配与组合机制，导致模型难以泛化到训练中未出现的协作模式。多臂协作已成为具身操作的核心能力，因此需要更结构化、可组合的臂级行为分配方法。

Method: MA-VLA将协作行为分解为中级原子提示并逐一分配给各机械臂；引入Arm Shuffle训练策略，在训练中对每只手臂的观测、状态和分配的原子提示进行置换，以强制模型遵循角色无关的指令；构建了测试时协作模式在训练集中缺失的基准数据集。

Result: 在仿真与真实世界评估中，先前的SOTA VLA模型在未见协作模式下大多失败，而MA-VLA持续成功，验证了结构化、逐臂原子动作分配在多臂具身系统中实现可扩展泛化的有效性。

Conclusion: 结构化、逐臂的原子动作分配为多臂具身系统的可扩展泛化提供了一条实用路径，代码、模型与数据已开源。

Abstract: Multi-arm collaboration is becoming a core capability in embodied manipulation. Recent vision-language-action (VLA) models integrate perception, language, and control, but most represent language as a single global instruction and do not provide an explicit mechanism for assigning and composing arm-specific behaviors. This design limits transfer to collaboration patterns that differ from those observed during training. We present MA-VLA, a unified framework for multi-arm collaboration via atomic action assignment. MA-VLA decomposes cooperative behavior into mid-level atomic prompts and allocates them to individual arms, enabling explicit subgoal specification and compositional reuse across tasks. To reduce reliance on fixed execution roles, we introduce Arm Shuffle, a training-time permutation of the observation, state, and assigned atomic prompts for each arm. This permutation enforces role-agnostic instruction following and supports recomposition into unseen coordination patterns, which we term multi-arm compositional generalization. We also construct a benchmark in which test-time collaboration patterns are absent in training set. Across simulation and real-world evaluations, prior state-of-the-art VLAs largely fail under these unseen collaborations, while MA-VLA consistently succeeds. These results indicate that structured, per-arm atomic action assignment offers a practical route to scalable generalization in multi-arm embodied systems. Code, models, and data are available at https://github.com/zhangzaibin/future-robots

</details>


### [237] [VISTA: Visually Inferred Spatial ConTact Attention for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25872)
*Jiayi Chen,Wenlong Dong,Yan Huang,Xianglin Chen,Zijian Lin,Jiaqi Yin,Yushan Liu,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VISTA-Policy是一个模仿学习框架，利用视觉形变场(VDF)——柔性夹爪的3D位移表示——作为高维视觉物理反馈信号，替代传统的触觉/力传感器，实现接触丰富的灵巧操作。框架包含物理感知编码引擎、能量聚合去噪机制和形变增强策略网络三个核心模块，在跨尺度物体抓取、瓶盖旋拧和书法书写任务上超越了纯视觉的3D Diffusion Policy和触觉基线模型，展现了良好的分布外泛化能力和对动态扰动的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的操作任务需要精确的交互反馈，但纯视觉模仿学习在遮挡或细微交互场景下提供的是间接模糊的接触信息；而专用触觉/力传感器虽能提供丰富接触信息，却引入了硬件复杂度、校准需求和部署成本。作者希望在不增加硬件负担的前提下获得高维接触反馈信号，从而弥合视觉与触觉之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出VISTA-Policy框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) Physics-Aware Encoding Engine，用于实时解码视觉形变场(VDF)；(2) Energy Aggregation Denoising Mechanism，用于从噪声中分离真实的交互信号；(3) Deformation-Augmented Policy Network，通过增量夹爪动作实现精确的闭环纠错。VDF作为柔性夹爪的3D位移表示，充当高维视觉物理反馈。

Result: 在跨尺度物体抓取、瓶盖旋拧和书法书写三个精细操作任务上，VISTA-Policy均优于强纯视觉基线3D Diffusion Policy和触觉基线模型。框架展现出对未见物体尺度的显著分布外泛化能力，并对动态扰动具有良好的鲁棒性，同时保持了部署的耐久性和成本效益。

Conclusion: VISTA-Policy通过利用柔性夹爪自身的视觉形变作为高维接触反馈，提供了一条无需额外触觉传感器硬件即可实现通用精细操作的有效路径，在非结构化环境中展示了成本效益高且耐用的解决方案，为大规模部署提供了可行性。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires precise interaction feedback. While vision-centric imitation learning is prevalent, external visual observations provide indirect and ambiguous cues about contact states, particularly under occlusion or subtle object--gripper interactions; dedicated tactile or force sensors can provide rich contact information but introduce additional hardware complexity, calibration requirements, and deployment costs. To bridge this gap, we propose VISTA-Policy, an imitation learning paradigm that utilizes the Visual Deformation Field (VDF), a 3D displacement representation of a compliant gripper, as high-dimensional visuo-physical feedback. The framework integrates: 1) a Physics-Aware Encoding Engine for real-time VDF decoding; 2) an Energy Aggregation Denoising Mechanism to isolate true interaction signals; and 3) a Deformation-Augmented Policy Network with incremental gripper actions for precise closed-loop correction. Extensive evaluations on Cross-Scale Object Grasping, Cap Unscrewing, and Calligraphy Writing demonstrate that VISTA-Policy outperforms the strong pure-vision baseline 3D Diffusion Policy and the tactile baseline. VISTA-Policy further demonstrates substantial out-of-distribution generalization to unseen object scales and robustness against dynamic disturbances, offering a durable and cost-effective route toward general-purpose fine-grained manipulation in unstructured environments. Project videos and supplementary materials are available at: https://sites.google.com/view/vista-policy.

</details>


### [238] [Low-Resolution Perception for Robotic Packing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25874)
*Giuseppe Fabio Preziosa,Federico Vignoni,Chiara Castellano,Marco Faroni,Andrea Maria Zanchettin,Paolo Rocco*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于机器人装箱的可扩展感知框架，在低成本低分辨率深度传感条件下，通过重建线索驱动下一个视角选择、抓取证据更新物体稳定性估计，联合决策何时采集和何时抓取，验证了低分辨率感知的实用性与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人装箱任务需要可扩展的感知能力，但高分辨率深度传感成本高昂。低分辨率感知虽然廉价，却面临重建质量下降和视角选择效率低的问题，亟需一种在低分辨率下仍能保持任务相关几何信息、避免冗余视角的感知框架。

Method: 提出一个联合框架：(i) 重建线索驱动下一个最优视角(NBV)选择，明确避免冗余视角同时保留任务相关几何；(ii) 抓取证据更新每个物体的稳定性估计，两者联合决定下一步获取什么视角以及何时执行抓取。验证分两步：在极低分辨率下对效用函数进行消融研究，以及跨策略的端到端评估。

Result: 在极低分辨率条件下，效用函数的消融研究表明各组件对感知质量均有贡献；端到端评估跨多种策略验证了低分辨率感知作为机器人装箱实用且可扩展选项的有效性。

Conclusion: 低分辨率深度感知结合重建线索驱动的NBV与抓取证据更新的稳定性估计，是机器人装箱中一种实用且可扩展的感知方案，为低成本工业应用提供了可行性依据。

Abstract: This work tackles the problem of scalable perception for robotic packing with low-cost, low-resolution depth sensing. We propose a framework where reconstruction cues drive next-view selection and grasp evidence updates a per-object stability estimate, jointly deciding what to acquire next and when to grasp. During the reconstruction, a low-resolution Next Best View (NBV) strategy explicitly avoids redundant views while preserving task-relevant geometry. We validate the approach in two steps: (i) an ablation study of the utility function under very low resolution, and (ii) a full end-to-end evaluation across policies, showing how low-resolution perception is a practical, scalable option for robotic packing.

</details>


### [239] [A Statistical Audit of Physical AI Benchmark Redundancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25940)
*Zaruhi Navasardyan,Hrant Davtyan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个51个模型×12个物理AI基准的稀疏矩阵，通过测量基准间的信息冗余，发现基准存在显著重叠；将替代基准对合并后，22/51个模型的排名发生显著变化。进一步通过贪心算法筛选出4个基准子集，保留全部12个基准78.5%的效用，并基于该子集拟合Bradley-Terry排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前物理AI模型在不同报告中使用的基准套件各不相同，导致模型-基准矩阵是稀疏的，且基准之间的关联性从未被量化。缺乏对基准冗余度的测量，使得排名结果的可靠性存疑。

Method: 从51个基准和152个模型的注册表中，依据报告密度选取12个物理AI基准和51个模型，结合模型卡片、基准论文及官方协议下的自跑评估构建分数矩阵。使用信息共享度量检测基准冗余，并提出结合分数离散度与未被已选基准解释方差的效用函数，采用贪心算法进行基准选择，最后在最优子集上拟合Bradley-Terry排名模型。

Result: 定量证明了物理AI基准之间存在显著冗余；将两对替代基准合并后，等权平均下51个模型中有22个排名变动3位以上；贪心筛选出的4基准子集保留了全部12个基准78.5%的效用。

Conclusion: 该基准选择流程仅需具有足够重叠的基准级分数即可运行，且不限于物理AI领域，具有通用性。

Abstract: Physical AI models are evaluated on suites of benchmarks that differ across model reports, leaving the model-by-benchmark matrix sparse and the relationship between benchmarks unmeasured. We construct a matrix of 51 models on 12 physical AI benchmarks, selected from a registry of 51 benchmarks and 152 models by reporting density, combining scores from model cards and benchmark papers with our own evaluation runs under each benchmark's official protocol. We measure how much information the benchmarks share and show quantitative evidence of Redundancy. Redundancy affects reported rankings: collapsing the two substitute pairs into single columns moves 22 of 51 models by three or more places under an equally weighted average. We then select benchmarks greedily under a utility combining score dispersion with variance not explained by the already-selected set, and obtain a four-benchmark subset retaining 78.5\% of the utility of all 12, on which we fit a Bradley--Terry ranking. The procedure requires only benchmark-level scores with sufficient overlap and is not specific to physical AI.

</details>


### [240] [DESCENT: Directed Edge Scene Encoding for Airport Surface Movement Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26002)
*Alexander Prutsch,David Schinagl,Horst Possegger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DESCENT，一种基于Transformer的架构，用于机场场面运动预测任务。通过潜在可达集（PRS）上下文采样机制和检测Transformer解码器，在Amelia-10基准上显著优于现有方法，尤其在安全关键场景中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着商用航空交通密度不断增加，提高地面运行安全性至关重要。虽然运动预测在自动驾驶领域已被广泛研究，但在机场场面运动场景中的应用仍未被充分探索，需要一个能处理异构动力学和严格拓扑约束的高效准确预测方法。

Method: 提出DESCENT架构，包含两个核心组件：(1) 潜在可达集（PRS）上下文采样机制，能够自适应地在不同运行阶段采集机场环境上下文；(2) 基于检测Transformer的解码器，用于生成精确的轨迹预测。

Result: 在Amelia-10基准上的广泛评估表明，DESCENT相比最先进的基线方法取得了显著的性能提升，尤其在安全关键场景中，领域感知采样提供了关键的长时程上下文以保证安全导航。

Conclusion: DESCENT通过结合PRS上下文采样和Transformer解码器，有效填补了机场场面运动预测领域的空白，为提升地面运行安全性提供了可行的技术方案，在安全关键场景中尤其具有优势。

Abstract: Advanced automation is a key technology for enhancing the safety of ground operations amidst the increasing density of commercial air traffic. While motion forecasting is a well-studied task in autonomous driving, its application to airport surface movements remains underexplored. To enable efficient and accurate prediction in this domain, we propose DESCENT, a transformer-based architecture designed to handle heterogeneous dynamics and strict topological constraints. Our approach features a Potential Reachable Set (PRS) context sampling mechanism that adaptively collects airfield environment context across diverse operational phases. Combined with a detection transformer-based decoder, DESCENT generates accurate trajectory forecasts. Extensive evaluations on the Amelia-10 benchmark demonstrate significant performance improvements over state-of-the-art baselines. These gains are especially pronounced in safety-critical scenarios, where our domain-aware sampling provides critical long-horizon context necessary for safe navigation.

</details>


### [241] [Phantom Navigator: Stealthy and Precise Unmanned Aerial Vehicle Redirection with Real-Time Tracking and GPS Spoofing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26011)
*Haocheng Meng,Shaocheng Luo,Songqiao Xie,Miroslav Pajic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Phantom Navigator，一种结合离线可达性分析与在线闭环隐蔽执行层的UAV重定向攻击方法，通过LiDAR-相机检测、跟踪与欺骗堆栈，实现将无人机隐蔽且精确地误导至指定欺骗目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UAV重定向攻击（物理捕获、通信劫持、传感器欺骗）存在可靠性差、精度低和隐蔽性不足的问题：物理拦截成本高、仅一次机会且附带损害风险大；网络攻击需要深层技术专长和加密信道访问；传感器欺骗难以达到将UAV导向指定目标所需的精度和鲁棒性。因此需要一种更可靠、精确且隐蔽的攻击方案。

Method: 该方法包含两个核心组成部分：（1）离线预重定向可达性分析，提供可行的重定向范围的高保真估计；（2）在线闭环隐蔽执行层，确保实际场景中重定向成功。此外构建了配备LiDAR-相机检测、跟踪和欺骗堆栈的物理攻击平台，实现实时识别、姿态估计以及目标欺骗信号的计算。

Result: 在真实世界案例研究中验证了重定向方法及攻击实现的有效性，展示了该攻击能够隐蔽且精确地将受害UAV重定向至指定位置。

Conclusion: Phantom Navigator通过结合离线分析和在线执行的方式克服了现有UAV重定向攻击的局限性，实现了高可靠性、高精度和高隐蔽性的无人机重定向攻击。

Abstract: Redirecting unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) from their intended mission trajectories has been an active area of research. However, existing UAV redirection attacks lack reliability, precision, and covertness for a targeted diversion. They primarily rely on physical capture, communication hijacking, or sensor spoofing. Yet, physical interception is costly, offers only a single opportunity for success, and poses a high risk of collateral damage; network-based attacks demand deep technical expertise and access to encrypted communication channels; and sensor spoofing techniques typically fall short in achieving the accuracy and robustness required to steer a UAV toward a specified target. Consequently, we propose Phantom Navigator, a UAV redirection attack to mislead drones to a designated spoofing target, covertly and precisely. Our approach combines offline pre-redirection reachability analysis, which provides high-fidelity estimates of achievable redirect ranges, with an online closed-loop, stealthy execution layer that ensures successful redirection in practice. Based on this approach, we build a physical attack platform equipped with a LiDAR--camera detection, tracking, and spoofing stack that performs real-time identification, pose estimation, and computation of targeted spoofing signals to covertly and accurately redirect victim UAVs to a designated location. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our redirection methodology and the attack implementation in real-world case studies.

</details>


### [242] [When Obstacles Bend: Modeling Vegetation Deformation in the context of Field Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26050)
*Muhammad Hsaeeb Zaar Khizar,Tom Montagnon,Roland Lenain,Romuald Aufrère,Johann Laconte*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出通过植被的固有机械特性（而非依赖具体机器人的可通行性指标）来表征植被，结合变形测量与接触力数据估计力学参数，实现基于环境内在属性的植被感知导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可通行性评估从机器人视角定义，将环境评估与机器人自身动力学耦合，导致结果难以跨平台迁移，且不能直接反映植被本身的属性——而植被属性才是农业和环境监测应用中交互与潜在损伤的真正来源。

Method: 将变形测量与接触力数据相结合，估计植被的底层机械参数，从而重建植被对交互的响应，实现独立于具体平台的植被力学特性表征。

Result: 提出了一种基于植被固有机械属性的表征方法，实现了不依赖平台特定指标的植被感知导航。

Conclusion: 通过从平台依赖的可通行性指标转向平台无关的植被内在机械属性，该方法克服了传统方法的跨平台迁移限制，为农业和环境监测等应用中的植被交互提供了更本质的评估框架。

Abstract: Autonomous robots operating in natural environments must often interact with vegetation rather than simply avoid it. In this context, traversability is typically defined from the robot's perspective, by measuring how a specific platform responds when moving through the environment. While practical, this viewpoint entangles the assessment of the environment with the robot's own dynamics, making the resulting characterization difficult to transfer across different platforms. More importantly, it does not directly reflect the properties of the vegetation itself, which are the true source of interaction and potential damage in applications such as agriculture and environmental monitoring. To address this limitation, we propose to characterize vegetation through its intrinsic mechanical properties, independently of any specific robot. By combining deformation measurements with contact force data, we estimate the underlying mechanical parameters and reconstruct the vegetation's response to interaction. This enables vegetation-aware navigation based on intrinsic environmental properties rather than platform-dependent metrics.

</details>


### [243] [$R^3$: Training Robots to Reason in Natural Language via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26053)
*Lehong Wu,Yuxiao Qu,Zheyuan Hu,Ivan Zhang,Limin Wei,Zackory Erickson,Aviral Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出R3，一种将现成VLM转化为机器人推理器的训练方法，通过中期训练VLM学习专家生成的推理轨迹，再利用基于rubric的强化学习从离线动作数据中优化推理器，使自由形式语言推理在测试时引导底层操纵策略，显著提升长时程操作任务的探索和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言推理使基础模型能在难题上投入更多测试时间计算，但该方法能否改进机器人操纵尚不清楚。长时程操作任务需要跟踪部分进度、推理物体关系、从错误中恢复并引导噪声低层策略，现有方法缺乏有效的推理机制。

Method: R3采用两阶段训练方案：首先在专家生成的推理轨迹上进行中期训练，使VLM初始化所需的推理风格；然后利用基于单步rubric的强化学习，从离线动作数据中优化推理器。与以往主要将结构化轨迹作为辅助监督的方法不同，R3训练自由形式语言推理以在测试时为动作提供指导。

Result: 在Language Table和模拟双臂杂货打包两个基准上，R3在探索能力、跨未见任务泛化方面均有提升，显著优于仅指令的模仿学习基线。分析表明自由形式语言推理可作为一种测试时计算机制来引导低层策略。

Conclusion: R3证明了自由形式语言推理能够作为测试时计算机制有效引导低层操纵策略，为在机器人操纵中利用语言推理提升长期任务性能提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Reasoning in language allows foundation models to spend more test-time compute on hard problems, such as those requiring decomposition, constraint tracking, and prediction of future consequences. Whether this mechanism can improve robotic manipulation remains unclear, where long-horizon tasks require tracking partial progress, reasoning about object relations, recovering from mistakes, and steering noisy low-level policies. In this paper, we study whether VLMs can be trained to reason directly in natural language to guide low-level manipulation policies. We introduce $R^3$, a simple post-training recipe that turns off-the-shelf VLMs into robotic reasoners: it first mid-trains a VLM on expert-generated reasoning traces to initialize the desired reasoning style, then improves the reasoner with single-step rubric-based RL from offline action data. Unlike prior robotic reasoning methods that mostly use structured traces as auxiliary supervision, $R^3$ trains free-form language reasoning to produce test-time guidance for action. We instantiate $R^3$ on Language Table and simulated bimanual grocery packing, two controlled testbeds for studying robotic reasoning and long-horizon manipulation. $R^3$ improves exploration and generalization across unseen tasks and significantly outperforms instruction-only imitation learning baselines on both benchmarks. Our analyses suggest that free-form language reasoning can function as a test-time compute mechanism for steering low-level policies. Our project page is available at https://robotic-reasoner.github.io/.

</details>


### [244] [One Policy, Many Embodiments: Unified Camera-Centric Action Geometry Pre-training for Heterogeneous Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26058)
*Xiaomi Embodied Intelligence Team,University of Macau,:,Shaoqing Xu,Fang Li,Guozhi Zhan,Zhixiang Duan,Yuhan Wang,Yuechen Luo,Shengyin Jiang,Hanbing Li,Zhiying Du,Longlong Wang,Longmei Jiang,Weixiang Liang,Ying Gong,Yong Pan,Ziping Zhao,Zhiyuan Chen,Yangwei You,Kun Ma,Qinyuan Liu,Hangjun Ye,Zhi-xin Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UCAG-P提出了一种以相机为中心的统一定位动作公式（camera-centric unified action formulation），将异构的具身数据在结构上对齐到一个共享的几何动作空间，使统一的VLA策略能够跨不同机器人形态、相机配置和动作空间进行联合学习，训练数据达6.37K小时，单检查点在多个基准上取得显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展通用视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略的主要瓶颈在于具身数据的固有异构性，涵盖不同的机器人形态、相机配置和低级动作空间。现有方法通过显式动作重定向、人-机器人视频合成或数据集特定适配分支来应对这种不匹配，但从根本上阻碍了统一策略的联合学习。

Method: UCAG-P采用相机中心化的统一定位动作公式，将异构具身数据集在结构上对齐到共享的几何动作空间。它不将机器人特定指令作为共享策略目标，而是通过图像和相机坐标系中相机可观测的锚定运动（anchor motion）来表示操作任务，将机械臂、人形机器人和人类手视为同一动作模式的多种具身体现。一个几何条件化的动作翻译器（geometry-conditioned action translator）将预测运动与目标具身运动学结合，生成可执行的指令，从而实现解耦架构。

Result: UCAG-P在4.03K小时的机器人和仿真数据以及2.34K小时的人类演示数据上训练后，单个检查点在无需基准特定微调的情况下，于LIBERO上达到98.3%，RoboTwin Easy和Hard上分别达到88.7%和89.2%，LIBERO-Plus上零样本达到82.0%，RoboCasa GR-1上达到62.0%。

Conclusion: UCAG-P通过解耦的架构设计，使共享VLA策略能够学习可迁移的操作几何信息，同时保留具身特定的可控性，有效解决了异构具身数据下的统一策略学习挑战，并在多个基准上展示了强大的泛化能力。

Abstract: Scaling generalist vision-language-action (VLA) policies is severely bottlenecked by the inherent heterogeneity of embodied data, which spans diverse robot morphologies, camera configurations, and low-level action spaces. Existing paradigms typically address this mismatch through explicit action retargeting, human-to-robot video synthesis, or dataset-specific adaptation branches, fundamentally hindering the joint learning of a unified policy. We introduce UCAG-P, a camera-centric unified action formulation that structurally aligns heterogeneous embodied datasets into a shared geometric action space. Rather than treating robot-specific commands as the shared policy target, UCAG-P represents manipulation through camera-observable anchor motion in image and camera-frame coordinates, treating robot arms, humanoids, and human hands as different embodiments of a common action schema. A geometry-conditioned action translator combines predicted motion with target-embodiment kinematics to produce executable controls. The resulting decoupled architecture allows a shared VLA policy to learn transferable manipulation geometry while retaining embodiment-specific controllability. UCAG-P is trained on 4.03K hours of robot and simulation data and 2.34K hours of human demonstrations. A single checkpoint reaches 98.3% on LIBERO, 88.7% and 89.2% on RoboTwin Easy and Hard, 82.0% zero-shot on LIBERO-Plus, and 62.0% on RoboCasa GR-1, without benchmark-specific fine-tuning.

</details>


### [245] [VirTooS: A ROS 2 - Unity Virtualization Toolkit for Fleet Management of Autonomous Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26066)
*Andrea Drudi,Lorenzo Pichierri,Andrea Testa,Giuseppe Notarstefano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了VirTooS，一个基于Python/C#的工具包，利用ROS 2和Unity游戏引擎实现自主移动机器人（AMR）车队的混合现实虚拟仿真，支持虚拟与真实传感器、分布式机器人实验及容器化部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向自主移动机器人车队的仿真工具需要兼具真实感、可扩展性和混合现实能力，以支持车队管理任务的开发与验证。

Method: 采用Python/C#构建VirTooS工具包，基于ROS 2和Unity引擎实现混合现实环境；提供虚拟场景定制功能；支持虚拟和真实传感器（如LiDAR）用于地图构建与安全导航；基于ChoiRbot框架设计分布式实验专用例程，并附带容器化部署套件。

Result: 通过任务分配问题的激励性实验展示了真实与虚拟机器人之间的无缝交互，验证了工具包在虚拟环境中的可行性；源码将公开在GitHub上。

Conclusion: VirTooS提供了一套可定制、可扩展且易于部署的混合现实仿真方案，适用于AMR车队管理任务的开发与评估。

Abstract: In this paper, we present VirTooS, a Python/C# toolkit designed to implement fleet-management tasks on teams of Autonomous Mobile Robots (AMRs). VirTooS leverages the Robot Operating System (ROS) 2 and Unity game engine to provide realistic, scalable virtual experiments in a mixed-reality environment. The toolbox allows users to easily generate and customize virtual scenarios for realistic simulations. Virtual and real sensors as, e.g., LiDARs, can be exploited to map and safely navigate in the mixed-reality environment. To enable distributed robotics experiments, we propose a set of tailored routines leveraging the ChoiRbot framework. As a motivating example, we show a set of experiments for task assignment problems in a virtual environment, allowing seamless interaction among real and virtual robots. Moreover, the package comes with a containerized suite to easily deploy it on different machines. The source code will be made publicly available on GitHub.

</details>


### [246] [Gating Before Commitment: Anticipating Intent Divergence to Prevent Post-Interaction Decision Failures in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26074)
*Cong Xu,Ravi Sankar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种语言引导的意图模块决策层，通过意图-几何散度评分和门控机制在路径规划提交前拦截错误机动。在离线越野偏离场景和四个碰撞片段的重放中，门控是唯一能修复规划的层级（主案例在漂移后72ms触发、出走廊前161ms触发），校准后误触发率从每5.9分钟9次降至每分钟0.341次。消融实验显示完整评分在部署条件下对五个失败中的四个检测最快，但不确定性否决与几何规则的作用也存在边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆交互中意图误判会导致反复出现的规划失败，需要一种在规划提交前、走廊包络上游的决策层来防止错误机动进入执行阶段。

Method: 该方法包含三个组件：语言引导的意图模块读取结构化描述符；计算平滑的意图-几何散度评分；在机动提交前对规划进行门控。设计上位于走廊包络上游，采用冻结的公开实现进行重放验证，并通过预注册的重设计（将评分不确定性视为弃权而非半个冲突）进行校准，辅以两项消融实验界定模型贡献。

Result: 在十个重放中门控层均将轨迹保持在走廊内（主案例：漂移后72ms触发，比走廊出口早161ms）；校准将误触发从每5.9分钟9次降至每分钟0.341次。消融表明：部署资格下完整评分对五个失败中四个检测最快，未否决规则下为三个；丢弃置信度项代价是两个检测的损失；在等误报条件下，几何规则在域内轨道上将检测率提高三倍以上。

Conclusion: 证据支持门控机制的有效性，模型被证明的作用为：在所示失败中提供最快检测，以及对几何规则提供不确定性否决（veto）。

Abstract: Intent misinterpretation during vehicle interactions causes recurring planning failures. We study a decision layer in which a language-guided intent module reads structured descriptors, computes a smoothed intent-geometry divergence score, and gates the planned maneuver before commitment, upstream of a corridor envelope. On a replayed off-road departure and four crash clips under a frozen, disclosed implementation, gating is the only layer that repairs the plan: on the main case it fires 72 ms after the drift onset but 161 ms before the corridor exit, keeping the trajectory in the corridor in all ten replays. The first calibration draws nine false triggers in 5.9 minutes, each from scoring uncertainty as half a conflict; a preregistered redesign treating uncertainty as abstention cuts this to 0.341 per minute. Two ablations bound the model's contribution: the full score detects fastest on four of five failures under the deployed eligibility, three of five against the unvetoed rule (000871 by one cycle; 000228 by a pre-onset fire on an uncertain stretch that five clips cannot classify as signal or coincidence; dropping the confidence term costs two detections), while on in-domain tracks at equal false positives the geometric rule more than triples its detection. The evidence supports the gating mechanism; the model's demonstrated roles are the fastest detection on these failures and an uncertainty veto on the geometric rule.

</details>


### [247] [Fast Generative Grasping via Lie Group-Constrained MeanFlow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26076)
*S. Talha Bukhari,Yi Wei,Ruiqi Ni,Zachary Kingston,Aniket Bera*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于MeanFlow的快速生成式抓取合成方法，在乘积李群SO(3)×ℝ³上通过代数半群一致性条件与黎曼条件流匹配结合，实现≤5次网络评估即可生成可靠抓取，在ACRONYM数据集上达到与SOTA扩散/流模型相当的抓取性能，推理延迟达毫秒级（最高39倍加速），并可直接迁移到真实机器人抓取中无需额外训练或域适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式机器人抓取合成需要学习多模态分布，扩散和流模型虽灵活可泛化，但其迭代式多步采样过程耗时，阻碍了机器人在时间关键场景中的实时操作需求。现有方法难以在保持生成质量的同时实现快速推理。

Method: 将MeanFlow方法扩展到乘积李群G=SO(3)×ℝ³上：(1) 训练目标将纯代数的半群一致性条件与Riemannian Conditional Flow Matching相结合；(2) 该结合将平均速度锚定到数据分布，使得训练过程在流匹配的框架内由半群一致性条件提供约束；(3) 通过李群约束的MeanFlow公式，显著减少采样的迭代步数。

Result: 在ACRONYM数据集上，该方法仅需≤5次网络评估即可生成可靠抓取，抓取生成性能与SOTA扩散和流模型相当，同时推理延迟达到毫秒级（最高39倍加速）。此外，方法无需额外训练或域适应即可直接应用于真实机器人抓取，且在观测噪声下表现出鲁棒的抓取合成能力。

Conclusion: 本文证明了将MeanFlow与李群上的半群一致性约束相结合，可以在保持生成抓取质量的前提下大幅减少采样步数并加速推理，实现了从仿真到真实世界的直接迁移，为实时生成式机械臂抓取提供了一种高效且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Grasp synthesis is a core task in robotic manipulation, for which the solution typically forms a multimodal distribution rather than a point estimate. Generative robotic grasping aims to learn this distribution with deep generative models such as diffusion and flow-based approaches. The iterative nature of such generative models makes them flexible and generalizable; however, multi-step sampling impedes the time-critical operation required in robotics. We devise an approach to fast generative grasping based on MeanFlow on the product Lie group $\mathcal{G} = \mathrm{SO}(3) \times \mathbb{R}^3$. The training objective couples a purely algebraic semigroup consistency condition with Riemannian Conditional Flow Matching on $\mathcal{G}$ that anchors the average velocity to the data distribution. The resulting Lie Group-constrained MeanFlow formulation samples reliable grasps in $\leq 5$ network evaluations, matching the grasp generation performance of state-of-the-art diffusion and flow-based models on the ACRONYM dataset at millisecond-scale inference latency (up to $39\times$ speed-up). We further demonstrate that the approach directly translates to real-world robotic grasping without additional training or domain adaptation, exhibiting robust grasp synthesis under observation noise.

</details>


### [248] [Zero-WAM: In-Context World-Action Modeling from Human Videos for Open-Ended Task Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26103)
*Jiaming Zhou,Qihang Zhang,Gangwei Xu,Cunxin Fan,Yujie Zhao,Ruilin Wang,Yiming Luo,Shuai Yang,Xing Zhu,Yujun Shen,Junwei Liang,Yinghao Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Zero-WAM，一种因果视频-动作模型，通过人类视频作为上下文提示实现机器人操作任务的零样本跨任务泛化。模型利用自动生成的HumanGen数据集（74.2K人类-机器人ICL对，覆盖8.6K任务），并引入上下文未来块预测（IFP）目标进行训练。在RoboTwin 2.0仿真中，七个未见任务上平均成功率达47.0%，较最强视频动作基线提升29.5个百分点；在真实世界评估中能泛化到多物体场景、长程操作和细粒度插入等未见任务配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本跨任务泛化是机器人学习中的核心挑战。大型语言模型通过上下文学习（ICL）无需更新参数即可执行新任务，受此启发，作者希望将该范式引入机器人操作领域。关键在于找到操作任务的自然规范形式——语言缺乏足够的视觉线索，而人类视频能提供丰富的任务演进视觉信息。同时需要解决任务丰富的配对人类-机器人数据稀缺的问题。

Method: 提出Zero-WAM因果视频-动作模型，以人类视频作为上下文提示执行未见任务。关键技术包括：(1) 自动管道将任务采样的机器人轨迹转换为语义匹配的人类视频，构建HumanGen数据集；(2) 提出上下文未来块预测（IFP）目标，抑制从已见任务中学到的捷径，迫使策略从视频提示中提取任务信息；(3) 采用因果视频-动作架构实现人类视频引导下的动作生成。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0仿真中七个未见任务上平均成功率为47.0%，较最强视频动作基线绝对提升29.5个百分点。在真实世界评估中，Zero-WAM成功跟随人类视频指导，泛化到多物体场景、长程操作和细粒度插入等未见任务配置。

Conclusion: 本文证明将LLM中的上下文学习范式（通过上下文指定任务，无需参数更新）迁移到机器人操作是可行的，人类视频作为操作任务的上下文规范比语言更有效。自动数据管道解决了人类-机器人配对数据稀缺的瓶颈，IFP训练目标有效防止了策略对已见任务的过拟合。

Abstract: Zero-shot cross-task generalization, where a policy must execute manipulation tasks never seen during training, remains a central challenge in robot learning. In large language models, a novel task can be performed simply by specifying it in the context, without any parameter update. This form of in-context learning (ICL) turns generalization into a problem of task specification. To achieve cross-task generalization, we bring this paradigm to robotic manipulation, and argue that the natural task specification for manipulation is a human video: unlike language, it provides rich visual cues about the intended task evolution. We present Zero-WAM, a causal video-action model that executes unseen tasks by following in-context human video guidance. To address the scarcity of task-rich paired human-robot data, we propose an automatic pipeline that converts task-sampled robot trajectories into semantically matched human videos, yielding HumanGen, a dataset of 74.2K human-robot ICL pairs across 8.6K tasks. For model training, we further introduce an in-context future chunk prediction (IFP) objective that suppresses shortcuts learned from seen tasks and forces the policy to draw task information from the video prompt. On seven unseen tasks in RoboTwin 2.0 simulation, Zero-WAM achieves a 47.0% average success rate, an absolute improvement of 29.5 percentage points over the strongest video-action baseline. In real-world evaluations, it follows human video guidance to generalize to unseen task configurations involving multi-object scenes, long-horizon manipulation, and fine-grained insertion.

</details>


### [249] [SkyDrive: Learning to Drive in a New City from Aerial Traffic Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25142)
*Weijiang Xiong,Lan Feng,Alexandre Alahi,Nikolas Geroliminis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SkyDrive框架，利用无人机空中交通监控为新环境中的自动驾驶轨迹规划器提供高效、可扩展的监督数据，通过137小时航拍素材提取65万个驾驶样本构建基准，实验证明有限的空中监督（每地点30分钟）能显著缓解跨城市域差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模仿学习的自动驾驶规划器在零样本迁移到新环境时性能严重退化，因为存在交通规则、道路布局和驾驶行为的域偏移。传统适应方法需要资源密集型的车载数据采集，成本高昂且效率低。作者旨在寻找一种可扩展且高效的替代数据来源，以支持自动驾驶系统在新城市的快速适应。

Method: SkyDrive框架利用无人机空中交通监控来提供监督信号。与车载数据采集仅记录本车及其周围环境不同，空中平台能同时观察大量道路使用者且视野范围更广，使每辆车都能成为带真实驾驶行为的样本来源。作者基于137小时航拍监控素材，提取65万个驾驶样本构建了用于轨迹规划器和运动预测器的基准数据集。

Result: 零样本实验显示多个模型存在显著的跨城市域差距，但这些差距可以通过有限的空中监督得到缓解，例如每地点仅30分钟监控即可显著改善性能。这表明空中交通监控作为数据源具有高效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 空中交通监控是适应新城市自动驾驶系统的高效且可扩展的数据来源，为缓解自动驾驶跨域迁移问题提供了新的数据获取途径。数据和代码将公开。

Abstract: Autonomous driving has made remarkable progress through imitation learning with massive human demonstration data. However, a trained planner often degrades severely when applied to a new environment zero-shot, because of domain shifts in traffic regulations, road layout and driving behaviors. Therefore, adapting a trajectory planner to a new city typically requires resource-demanding local data collection with a vehicle sensor suite. In this work, we show that driving behavior can be learned from a scalable and efficient alternative. We introduce \emph{SkyDrive}, a framework that utilizes drone-based traffic monitoring to provide efficient supervision for autonomous driving agents in a new environment. While vehicle-based data collection logs the ego and its surroundings, an aerial platform naturally observes many road users simultaneously over an extended field of view. As a result, every vehicle can be a data source with grounded driving behavior, effectively scaling up the amount of supervision. Based on 137 hours of aerial traffic monitoring footage, we extract 650K driving samples and construct a benchmark for trajectory planners and motion predictors. Zero-shot experiments with multiple models reveal significant cross-city domain gaps, but many of them can be alleviated by limited supervision from the sky, e.g., 30 minutes of monitoring per location. Our findings show that aerial traffic monitoring is an efficient and scalable data source for adapting autonomous driving systems in new cities. Data and code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [250] [GaussVLA: Geometry-Aware Spatial Reasoning for Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24959)
*Md Selim Sarowar,Md Tanvir Islam,Sungho Kim,Sangtae Ahn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GaussVLA，一种基于Mamba的视觉-语言-动作模型，通过高斯空间分词器(GST)将语义和深度特征提升为紧凑的3D高斯token，并结合深度感知链式思考(DA-CoT)进行非自回归几何推理，在LIBERO上以200M参数达到93.5%平均成功率，Spatial套件100%成功率，比SpatialVLA相对提升19.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型将视觉观察编码为扁平的2D patch token，缺乏内在几何结构；稠密单目深度注入的是逐像素标量值，既不含表面朝向也不含几何置信度，导致策略的空间结构化推理能力受限。

Method: GaussVLA包含两个核心模块：(1) 高斯空间分词器(GST)，将冻结的语义特征和深度特征提升为紧凑的3D高斯token，并通过学习查询池化几何显著区域；(2) 深度感知链式思考(DA-CoT)，在语言和流时间条件化下执行结构化的非自回归几何推理。整体架构基于Mamba。

Result: 在仿真(LIBERO)和真实世界评估中表现优异：LIBERO上平均成功率93.5%，Spatial套件达到100.0%成功率，仅需200M参数；相比SpatialVLA相对平均成功率提升19.7%，且参数效率显著更高。

Conclusion: GaussVLA通过3D高斯token化和深度感知推理有效增强了VLA模型的空间操作能力，在保持参数高效的同时实现了领先的性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models encode visual observations as flat 2D patch tokens that carry no intrinsic geometric structure, and augmenting them with dense monocular depth injects per-pixel scalar values that encode neither surface orientation nor geometric confidence. This leaves the policy with limited structured spatial reasoning for action prediction. We propose GaussVLA, a Mamba-based VLA that incorporates two custom modules: Gaussian Spatial Tokenizer (GST) to lift frozen semantic and depth features into compact 3D Gaussian tokens, pools geometrically salient regions with learned queries, and \emph{Depth-Aware Chain-of-Thought (DA-CoT)} that performs structured, non-autoregressive geometric reasoning under language and flow-time conditioning. Across both simulation and real-world evaluations, GaussVLA demonstrates strong spatial-manipulation performance while remaining parameter-efficient. On LIBERO, it achieves 93.5% average success and 100.0% success on the Spatial suite with only 200M parameters, improving over SpatialVLA by 19.7% relative average success while remaining significantly more parameter-efficient.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [251] [FAMPWQ: Fisher Information-based Adaptive Mixed Precision Weight Quantization for Effective LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24945)
*Gongwei Lee,Ji Liu,Juncheng Jia,Ji Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FAMPWQ，一种基于Fisher信息度量的自适应混合精度权重量化方法，通过强化学习分配逐层位宽，在保持LLM推理性能的同时实现高效部署，实验表明其优于7种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在资源受限设备上部署困难，虽然量化是有效方法，但传统量化方案因均匀位宽或简单启发式敏感性评估导致严重的性能退化，亟需更精细的自适应量化策略。

Method: 首先提出基于Fisher信息度量的系统模型，用于衡量逐层对量化的敏感性；其次设计基于强化学习的位宽分配器，根据Fisher敏感性度量生成自适应位宽分配策略。

Result: 在7个模型和5个基准上的广泛实验表明，FAMPWQ在PPL（最多降低3.39）、准确率（最多提高6.87%）和LLM-as-a-judge对比（最高76%胜率）方面显著优于7种基线方法。

Conclusion: FAMPWQ通过Fisher信息度量和强化学习驱动的自适应混合精度量化，在商品GPU上实现了高效且保持性能的LLM推理，验证了逐层自适应位宽分配策略的有效性。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed remarkable achievements of Large Language Models (LLMs) in multiple domains, while the excessive resource requirements of LLMs hinder the deployment on resource-constrained devices. Although model quantization stands out as an effective approach, conventional quantization approaches typically incur severe performance degradation due to uniform bit-width or simple heuristic sensitivity evaluation. In this paper, we propose a novel Fisher information-based Adaptive Mixed Precision Weight Quantization approach, i.e., FAMPWQ, which performs layer-adaptive weight quantization for effective LLM inference on commodity GPUs. First, we propose a system model with a novel Fisher information metric to measure the layer-wise sensitivity to quantization. Second, we propose a reinforcement learning-based bit-width allocator in FAMPWQ, which generates an adaptive bit-width allocation strategy based on the Fisher information sensitivity metric. Extensive experiments on 7 models and 5 benchmarks demonstrate that FAMPWQ significantly outperforms 7 baseline approaches in terms of PPL (up to 3.39 smaller), accuracy (up to 6.87% higher), and LLM-as-a-judge comparison (up to 76% win rate).

</details>


### [252] [MacroAgent: Regularity-Aware Macro Legalization with LLM-Agent-Designed Contour Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24946)
*Jiaxi Jiang,Xufeng Yao,Yuxuan Zhao,Yuntao Lu,Peiyu Liao,Zuodong Zhang,Yibo Lin,Bei Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MacroAgent框架，利用大语言模型(LLMs)发现多种有效的启发式规则感知轮廓算法，通过聚类、轮廓生成、模板匹配和簇间优化四个阶段解决VLSI设计中宏单元合法化(宏对齐)问题，在布局规则性、线长和鲁棒性上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宏单元在现代VLSI设计中占据核心区域的大部分，其位置对最终设计质量(QoR)有显著影响，宏单元合法化是决定宏位置的最终步骤。然而现有方法存在三个主要缺陷：鲁棒性不足、计算成本高昂、忽略宏之间的规则性（对齐/均匀分布），因此需要一种更高效且规则感知的宏合法化框架。

Method: MacroAgent采用四阶段框架：(1)聚类——将宏单元分组；(2)轮廓生成——利用LLMs发现多种有效的启发式规则感知轮廓算法；(3)模板匹配——为宏匹配适当的放置模板；(4)簇间优化——对簇之间进行精细化调整。核心创新在于利用LLM自动发现和生成规则感知的轮廓算法，而非人工手工设计启发式方法。

Result: 在TILOS和Chipyard基准上相比SOTA宏合法化方法，布局规则性提升2至8倍，布线线长降低3%-5%，拥塞相当且鲁棒性显著提升。端到端评估（Cadence Innovus P&R）显示：相比DREAMPlace宏合法化基线，布线线长降低2.9%，TNS改善68.3%；集成到Innovus宏放置流程中布线线长额外降低1.8%。

Conclusion: MacroAgent通过引入LLM自动发现启发式规则感知算法，有效解决了宏合法化中鲁棒性、成本和规则性之间的权衡问题。实验结果验证了规则性的提升能够转化为实际的PPA（功耗、性能、面积）收益，且集成到工业流程中仍有显著改善，展示了LLM辅助EDA算法设计的潜力。

Abstract: Macros constitute a large part of the core area in modern very large-scale integration (VLSI) designs. Moreover, macro positions have a significant impact on the final quality of result (QoR), and macro legalization is typically the final step in determining the macro positions. However, existing approaches related to macro legalization either lack robustness or incur substantial computational costs or neglect the regularity between macros. To address these limitations, we introduce MacroAgent. The novel framework is a four-stage approach: clustering, contour generation, template matching, and inter-cluster refinement. We propose leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs) to discover multiple, effective heuristic regularity-aware contour algorithms. This framework successfully generates robust and effective algorithmic solutions for macro legalization. Compared with state-of-the-art macro legalization works, experimental results on TILOS and Chipyard benchmarks demonstrate a 2 to 8 fold improvement in layout regularity, a 3% to 5% reduction in routed wirelength with comparable congestion after global routing, and significantly better robustness with an acceptable runtime. Furthermore, end-to-end evaluation through Cadence Innovus place-and-route confirms that the regularity improvements translate into tangible PPA gains, including 2.9% lower routed wirelength and 68.3% TNS improvement over the DREAMPlace macro legalization baseline; it also achieves 1.8% lower routed wirelength when integrated into the Innovus macro placement flow.

</details>


### [253] [CAT-GS: Balanced Multimodal Learning via Calibrated Gating and Fusion Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24947)
*Mahir Shahriar Tamim,Sharjil Khan,Md. Samiul Alim,Tanvir Ahmed Khan,Shafin Rahman,Nabeel Mohammed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CAT-GS，一种基于神经动力学的优化控制器，通过温度缩放与EMA校准、边缘阈值门控策略、梯度预算重归一化以及融合层PCGrad四种机制，解决多模态端到端训练中的模态不平衡、不稳定门控和融合干扰三个耦合失效模式，在多个音视频及三模态基准上优于或持平最强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态神经网络的端到端训练常出现不稳定的神经动力学，表现为三种耦合的失效模式：模态不平衡（单一分支主导梯度优化）、不稳定门控（噪声置信度导致模态选择波动）和融合干扰（模态特异性梯度在共享融合层冲突），这些严重制约学习效果。

Method: CAT-GS在反向传播阶段运作，不修改模型架构、融合模块或任务损失。具体包括：(1) 通过温度缩放和EMA平滑校准教师派生可靠性；(2) 采用边缘阈值策略在"热身dropout、弱模态优先、弱偏置混合"三种模态间切换；(3) 通过封顶梯度预算重归一化（capped gradient-budget renormalization）稳定激进门控下的梯度幅值；(4) 在共享融合瓶颈处应用融合专属PCGrad以减少跨模态破坏性干扰。

Result: 在CREMA-D、AV-MNIST、VGGSound（双模态）、UR-FUNNY（三模态）、CG-MNIST（合成数据）以及AVE和CMU-MOSI（跨域基准）上评估，CAT-GS在融合多模态准确率上优于或匹配OGM-GE、G²D和UMT等强不平衡感知基线，并展现出更平滑的门控行为和更少的融合梯度冲突。

Conclusion: CAT-GS作为一种不侵入模型结构的训练期优化控制器，能有效稳定多模态学习的神经动力学，缓解模态不平衡与融合梯度冲突，在多种模态设置和数据集上均取得领先或相媲美的性能，同时提升门控稳定性。

Abstract: End-to-end training of multimodal neural networks often exhibits unstable neural dynamics characterized by three coupled failure modes that degrade learning: (i) modality imbalance, where one branch dominates gradient-based optimization; (ii) unstable gating, where noisy confidence cues induce erratic modality selection; and (iii) fusion interference, where modality-specific gradients conflict at the shared fusion layer. We propose CAT-GS (Calibrated, Adaptive, Thresholded Gating with Fusion Surgery), a neural dynamics-based optimization controller for intelligent computing applications. CAT-GS operates during backpropagation without modifying model architectures, fusion modules, or task losses. Through calibration of teacher-derived reliability via temperature scaling and EMA smoothing, CAT-GS stabilizes neural dynamics using a margin-thresholded policy to switch between warm-up dropout, weak-modality prioritization, and weak-biased blending, stabilizes gradient magnitudes under aggressive gating via capped gradient-budget renormalization, and applies fusion-only PCGrad to reduce destructive cross-modal interference at the primary shared bottleneck. We evaluate CAT-GS on audio--visual multimodal pattern recognition benchmarks (CREMA-D, AV-MNIST, and VGGSound), a tri-modal setting (UR-FUNNY), controlled synthetic data (CG-MNIST), and additional cross-domain benchmarks (AVE and CMU-MOSI). CAT-GS improves or matches fused multimodal accuracy against strong imbalance-aware baselines (including OGM-GE, G$^2$D, and UMT) across settings, and yields smoother gating behavior with fewer conflicting fusion gradients.

</details>


### [254] [Demystifying Reinforcement Learning Post-Training of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24949)
*Donovan Clay,Saket Gollapudi,Sankar Harilal,Min Jang,Jacob Morrison,Sewoong Oh,Natasha Jaques*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解构了RL后训练算法，在受控简化环境中逐一检验各步骤，揭示了RL训练结果如何受基础模型先验分布、奖励信号粒度、提示分布多样性和模型规模等因素影响，并通过策略输出分布的熵作为透镜比较预训练、SFT和RL后训练各阶段对模型确定性的塑造作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL后训练虽已成为提升LLM能力（推理、数学、编码等）的强大框架，但经典RL背后的原理对许多研究者和从业者而言仍是"黑箱"。本文旨在通过受控实验拆解RL后训练的各步骤，澄清其底层机制，降低NLP社区使用RL的门槛。

Method: 在受控且简化的环境中，使用带有可验证奖励（Verifiable Rewards）的RL，通过逐步隔离RL后训练算法的各机制步骤，系统性地考察四个因素对训练结果的影响：基础模型的先验分布、奖励信号的粒度、提示分布的多样性、以及模型规模。同时，以策略输出分布的熵为分析工具，比较预训练、SFT和RL后训练三个阶段所学到的分布差异。

Result: 研究发现：'伪奖励'（spurious rewards）的效果取决于后训练所使用的提示分布；RL后训练的成功取决于基础模型是否已在目标行为上分配了足够的概率质量，这与经典RL中的探索概念相关联；各阶段（预训练、SFT、RL）对模型确定性的塑造方式不同，可在熵的尺度上加以区分。

Conclusion: 本文为NLP社区提供了一个RL后训练的入门指南（primer），揭示了影响后训练成功的关键因素及其相互作用，帮助研究者将RL作为工具箱中的有效工具加以使用。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training has emerged as a powerful framework for enhancing the capabilities of large language models (LLMs), enabling impressive reasoning, math, and coding capabilities. Yet for many researchers and practitioners, the principles behind classical RL remain a "black box". In this work, we deconstruct the RL post-training algorithm, investigating each step to clarify what is actually happening beneath the surface. By isolating the mechanics of RL with Verifiable Rewards in a controlled and simplified environment, we examine how RL outcomes are shaped by the base model's prior distribution, the granularity of the reward signal, the diversity of the prompt distribution, and model scale. We use the entropy of the policy's output distribution as a lens to compare the distributions learned through pretraining, SFT, and RL post-training, revealing how each stage shapes model certainty. Our investigation sheds light on how these choices interact to affect post-training success. For example, we show that the effect of so-called 'spurious rewards' depends on the prompt distribution used for post-training. We also provide insight into why the success of RL post-training depends on whether the base model already places sufficient probability mass on the desired behavior, linking it to the classical concept of exploration in RL. Ultimately, we provide this primer as a resource to those in the NLP community wishing to incorporate RL as a tool in their toolbox.

</details>


### [255] [AFDBench: A Reasoning-First AI Scientist for NationalWeather Service Forecast Discussions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24954)
*Manmeet Singh,Somnath Luitel,Prabhjot Singh,Manraaj Banga,Naveen Sudharsan,Josh Durkee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AFDBench，这是首个用于评估生成式气象推理的基准，包含7,732条来自美国国家气象局（NWS）13个办公室的专家撰写的区域天气预报讨论（AFD），配套来自Google WeatherNext 2的真实AI气象预报数据及三个互补评估指标（Met-Align数值准确性、Style-Align专业语域遵循度、Input-Grounding数据保真度）。零样本评估显示开源LLM在专业语域和输入数据忠实度上表现不佳；通过GRPO强化学习（针对温度精确性、天气学正确性和格式合规性的领域奖励），7B参数模型在1,033条来自两个未见NWS办公室的留出样本上，将Style-Align从0.318提升至0.619，Input-Grounding从0.881提升至0.940。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在高风险气象文本生成中会产生数值幻觉，对气象传播构成风险。现有工作缺乏针对生成式气象推理的评估基准，且开源模型无法以专业气象学家的语域写作或忠实使用输入数据，因此需要构建专门的基准和训练方法来解决这一问题。

Method: 作者构建了AFDBench基准，涵盖7,732条由NWS 13个办公室的专家撰写的AFD文本以及真实的AI气象预报输入数据，提出三个评估指标：Met-Align（数值准确性）、Style-Align（专业语域一致性）和Input-Grounding（输入数据忠实度）。在此基础上，采用GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）强化学习算法，针对温度准确性、天气系统正确性和格式合规性设计领域特定的奖励函数，对7B参数的LLM进行微调训练。

Result: 在1,033条来自两个NWS未见办公室的留出测试样本上，GRPO将近乎翻倍地提升了Style-Align（从0.318提升至0.619），并将Input-Grounding从0.881提升至0.940。零样本评估表明开源LLM的Style-Align仅为约0.33，Input-Grounding约为0.88，无法以专业NWS语域写作或忠实使用输入数据。

Conclusion: 强化学习能够有效地教会7B参数的小型语言模型以专业气象学家的方式写作并忠实解读AI气象数据，验证了在专业领域通过GRPO训练模型在生成式气象推理任务中的可行性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) hallucinate numerical values when generating high-stakes meteorological text, posing risks for weather communication. We present AFDBench, an AI meteorologist that generates professional Area Forecast Discussions (AFDs) by reasoning through structured AI weather forecast data from Google's WeatherNext 2. We introduce AFDBench, the first benchmark for evaluating generative meteorological reasoning, comprising 7,732 expert written discussions from 13 National Weather Service (NWS) offices paired with real AI weather forecast inputs, and three complementary metrics: Met-Align (numerical accuracy), Style-Align (professional dialect adherence), and Input-Grounding (fidelity to source weather data). Zero-shot evaluations reveal that open-source LLMs achieve low Style-Align (~0.33) and moderate Input-Grounding (~0.88), failing to write in the professional NWS register or faithfully use their input data. We apply Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with domain-specific rewards targeting temperature accuracy, synoptic correctness, and format compliance. On 1,033 held-out samples from two unseen NWS offices, GRPO nearly doubles Style-Align from 0.318 to 0.619 and improves Input-Grounding from 0.881 to 0.940, demonstrating that reinforcement learning teaches a 7B-parameter model to write like a professional meteorologist and faithfully interpret AI weather data.

</details>


### [256] [Why and When Neural Networks Improve Local Approximation in Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24963)
*Chengkuo Bian,Pengcheng Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文澄清了神经代理模型在无导数优化中效果矛盾的根源：决定代理模型是否有效的三个因素分别是"角色"（代理提出候选方案而非替代梯度）、"半径"（模型仅在局部邻域内可靠）和"空间"（代理只能加速基方法本可取得的进展），而非训练所报告的拟合精度。实验表明，守护式辅助将高精度求解实例从67提升到84，而梯度替代则降至65。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中，神经代理模型在无导数优化中的表现相互矛盾：同一类模型在某些求解器上减少了评估次数，在其他求解器上却无效甚至有害。作者认为这些矛盾源于未能明确区分决定代理模型效用的三个关键因素，而训练曲线上报告的拟合精度并不能解释这些差异。该研究旨在澄清这些因素，为神经代理模型在无导数优化中的合理使用提供理论指导。

Method: 作者提出"半径感知的局部泛化"（radius-aware local generalisation）概念，将其与经典的全线性（fully linear）条件建立联系。在固定代理模型类别、训练流程和基方法的条件下，设计实验分别检验三个因素：1）角色——对比代理提出候选方案（守护式辅助）与代理替代梯度两种情况；2）半径——研究模型在优化路径邻域内的误差行为；3）空间——对比基方法仍有较大进步空间与进步空间受限的场景。

Result: 在117个基准实例上，守护式辅助将高精度求解实例从67提升至84，而梯度替代将其降至65；从训练损失中移除梯度项使代理接受率从0.703暴跌至0.148；1000次配对比较跨越十个噪声水平，结果显示不存在噪声阈值，只有提前停止的基方法表现出差异。相同的因素限制了增益：基于模型的信赖域求解器（进步空间小）在附加相同代理后从88降至86，而发布的插值软件保持领先达到103；在蒙特卡洛库存模型中，修复接受界面值得10.40成本单位，而代理本身贡献为0.00。

Conclusion: 神经代理模型在无导数优化中的效用由角色、半径和空间三个因素共同决定，而非拟合精度。守护式辅助（代理提出候选方案供真实目标确认）有益，而替代梯度有害；模型仅在有限邻域内可靠，其误差不随邻域收缩而消失也不随邻域扩张而存活；代理只能加速基方法本身能取得的进展。这些因素同时界定了代理模型的增益上限。

Abstract: Published experience with neural surrogates in derivative-free optimisation is contradictory: the same family of models that cuts the evaluation count of one solver leaves another unchanged, or makes it worse. We show that the contradiction dissolves once three factors are stated, and that these, rather than the fit accuracy a training curve reports, are what delimit when a learned local model pays. Role: a surrogate that proposes candidates the true objective must still approve helps, while one that replaces a gradient the solver depends on hurts. Radius: a model fitted to an optimisation path is reliable only inside a bounded neighbourhood, and its error neither vanishes as that neighbourhood shrinks nor survives its growth. Room: a surrogate can only accelerate progress the base method is still able to make. We formalise radius-aware local generalisation, relate it to the classical fully linear condition, and test each factor with the surrogate class, training pipeline and base method held fixed. Over 117 benchmark instances safeguarded assistance raises the instances solved to high accuracy from 67 to 84 while gradient replacement lowers them to 65; removing the gradient term from the training loss cuts surrogate acceptance from 0.703 to 0.148; and 1000 paired comparisons over ten noise levels show no noise threshold, only a base method that stops early. The same factors bound the gain: a model-based trust-region solver, which leaves little room, drops from 88 to 86 when the identical surrogate is attached, and released interpolation software stays ahead at 103, and on a Monte-Carlo inventory model repairing the acceptance interface is worth 10.40 cost units against 0.00 for the surrogate.

</details>


### [257] [Physics-Informed Error Field Learning: A Post-Training Optimization Framework for Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24970)
*Jiuyun Sun,Yong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PIEFL框架，通过引入辅助误差网络学习当前解与精确解之间的误差场，将学习的误差修正与主网络预测结合，避免对整个解空间的持续优化，在不改变主网络架构的前提下作为通用后训练优化策略提升PINNs的求解精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINNs在优化后期，参数更新带来的精度提升逐渐减少而计算成本不断增加，传统单网络持续逼近解空间的方法效率低下，亟需一种能在不增加过多计算开销的情况下提升精度的策略。

Method: PIEFL框架首先在主网络达到满意精度后引入辅助误差网络，通过物理约束推导误差控制方程，使误差网络学习当前近似解与精确解之间的偏差，最终将误差修正与主网络预测结合以提高精度。该框架无需修改主网络架构，可作为通用后训练优化策略应用于现有PINN模型。

Result: 在代表性PDE上的数值实验表明，在相同计算预算下PIEFL取得了更高的求解精度，验证了该框架在提升PINN性能方面的有效性。

Conclusion: PIEFL通过将学习目标从解场转移到误差场，避免了整个解空间的持续优化，将计算资源聚焦于修正现有预测误差，是一种高效、通用且兼容现有PINN模型的精度提升策略。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) have emerged as an important class of numerical methods for solving partial differential equations (PDEs). However, during the late-stage optimization process, further parameter updates often yield diminishing accuracy improvements while increasing computational costs. To address this issue, this paper proposes a Physics-Informed Error Field Learning (PIEFL) framework for PINNs. Unlike conventional approaches that continuously approximate the solution field using a single network, PIEFL introduces an auxiliary error network after the primary network achieves satisfactory accuracy and shifts the learning objective from the solution field to the error field. By deriving error control equations under physical constraints, the error network learns the discrepancy between the current approximation and the exact solution, and the learned error correction is combined with the primary prediction to improve solution accuracy. The proposed framework avoids continuous optimization of the entire solution space and focuses computational resources on correcting existing prediction errors. Moreover, PIEFL requires no modification to the primary network architecture, making it compatible with existing PINN models and applicable as a general post-training optimization strategy. Numerical experiments on representative PDEs demonstrate that PIEFL achieves higher solution accuracy under the same computational budget, validating its effectiveness in improving the performance of PINNs.

</details>


### [258] [Resource-Efficient Pruning for Transformer via Low-Rank Importance Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24973)
*Peng Liu,Huibing Zeng,Yiqun Zhang,Yang Yi,Jigang Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: REP-LIE是一种在微调过程中实现资源高效剪枝的新方法，利用LoRA低秩矩阵的梯度来估计权重重要性，无需全梯度计算，并通过引入稳定性分数进行迭代剪枝，避免全参数微调，在中等规模编码器模型和大规模生成模型上取得竞争性性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模Transformer预训练语言模型的计算和内存成本高昂，尤其在资源受限环境中部署困难。传统剪枝方法依赖全梯度重要性估计且需要先微调模型，资源消耗不可接受。

Method: REP-LIE利用LoRA低秩矩阵的梯度估计权重重要性（无需全梯度计算），引入稳定性分数应对重要性估计的固有随机性，据此迭代剪枝不重要参数，并通过轻量级更新对剪枝后模型进行微调，避免全参数优化。

Result: 在中等规模编码器模型和大规模生成模型（LLaMA-7B和Mistral-7B）上的广泛实验表明，REP-LIE与现有方法相比仍能达到竞争性性能。

Conclusion: REP-LIE通过LoRA梯度与稳定性分数相结合，在微调过程中实现资源高效剪枝，既降低了训练成本又保持了模型性能，为资源受限环境下的模型部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: With the rapid development of large-scale pre-trained language models based on Transformer architectures, their high computational and memory costs have become a major obstacle to deployment, especially in resource-constrained environments. Traditional pruning methods typically depend on full gradient-based importance estimation, and they necessitate prior finetuning of the model to achieve satisfactory performance. This process often results in intolerable resource consumption. This paper proposes REP-LIE, a new approach to enable resource-efficient pruning during the process of finetuning. REP-LIE leverages the gradients of LoRA low-rank matrices to estimate the importance of weights without requiring full gradient computation. To address the inherent randomness in importance estimation, a stability score is introduced, serving as the basis for iterative pruning of unimportant model parameters. The pruned model is further finetuned through lightweight updates, eliminating the need for full-parameter optimization in the process of finetuning. Extensive experiments on both medium-scale encoder models and large-scale generative models (LLaMA-7B and Mistral-7B) demonstrate that REP-LIE still achieves competitive performance compared to existing approaches.

</details>


### [259] [Clearing the Underbrush: AI-Enhanced RF Interference Suppression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24974)
*Rahul Jain,Pierre Trepagnier,Rick Gentile,Joey Botero,Alexia Schulz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在先前基于自回归Transformer的AI干扰抑制方法基础上，引入有限标量量化（FSQ）分词器层以提升结构化干扰抑制性能并保持低延迟，同时探索多种推理优化技术以加速推理而不显著损失精度。实验以数字调制射频信号为目标信号、数字电视OFDM信号为干扰，结果表明该方法实现了低延迟和优于传统技术及先前AI方法的干扰抑制效果，并通过PESQ等音频指标验证了其优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的干扰抑制方法在处理结构化干扰时性能有限，而基于AI的方法能够同时联合考虑目标信号和混合信号以实现更优性能。作者希望在先前工作的基础上进一步提升干扰抑制性能，同时保持低延迟，并探索推理加速方法，使AI干扰抑制在实际部署中更具可行性。

Method: 在已有的自回归Transformer模型中添加有限标量量化（FSQ）分词器层，以改善干扰抑制性能并最小化延迟；同时实验多种推理优化技术，在尽量不损失精度的前提下加速推理过程。实验场景设定为目标信号为数字调制射频信号，结构化干扰为数字电视信号（典型的OFDM传输）。

Result: 该方案实现了低延迟和相比传统技术及先前AI方法更高的干扰抑制性能，通过PESQ等音频指标证明了AI方法的优越性，并探索了多种操作相关应用场景。

Conclusion: 基于FSQ分词器的自回归Transformer方法在结构化干扰抑制方面优于传统技术和现有AI方法，同时保持良好的延迟性能；推理优化技术能够在不显著损失精度的前提下提升推理速度，该方法在多种实战场景中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: AI-based structured interference rejection has grown more popular because deep learning approaches can outperform traditional methods by jointly considering the signal of interest (SOI) and the signal mixture (SOI plus interference). This work builds on a previous AI-enabled approach utilizing autoregressive transformer-based models by adding a Finite Scalar Quantization (FSQ) tokenizer layer which aims to improve the interference rejection performance while keeping overall latency to a minimum. Additionally, we experiment with other inference optimization techniques with the goal of speeding up inference without much accuracy loss. We explore this space with an experiment where the SOI is a digitally modulated radio frequency (RF) signal and the structured interference is a digital television signal, an extremely common type of Orthogonal Frequency-Division Multiplexing (OFDM) transmission. Our results achieve low latency and increased interference rejection over traditional techniques and prior work with other AI-enabled methods. We demonstrate the benefits of the AI-enabled approaches via audio metrics such as Perceptual Evaluation of Speech Quality (PESQ). Additionally, we explore a variety of applications and detail how our interference rejection algorithm may be used in operationally-relevant scenarios.

</details>


### [260] [MSR-IVA: Masked Structural Residual Independent Vector Analysis for State-Aware Fusion of Structural MRI and Dynamic Functional Network Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24978)
*Victor Solomon,Zening Fu,Rafal Angryk,Vince D. Calhoun,Jingyu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了掩码结构残差IVA（MSR-IVA），一种状态感知的多模态融合框架，用于结合sMRI和dFNC数据。该方法通过共享结构表示加上状态特异残差适配和掩码机制，解决了多状态结构分解不一致及部分受试者未表达所有动态状态的问题，在ADNI队列上相比基线显著改善了源耦合并减少了不匹配依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: sMRI与dFNC的多模态融合可以揭示大脑结构与动态功能状态变化之间的关系。然而，当同一个结构潜在表示与多个状态耦合时，分别对每个状态应用IVA会产生不相关的结构分解，而强制使用相同分解又会抑制状态特异的关联。此外，并非每个受试者都表达每个动态状态，这使得融合问题更加复杂。

Method: 提出MSR-IVA框架，其核心思想包括：(1) 共享结构表示，捕捉跨状态一致的结构成分；(2) 状态特异的残差适配（residual adaptations），允许各状态在共享基础上进行差异化调整；(3) 掩码机制（masks），处理部分受试者未表达某动态状态的不完整情况。

Result: 在ADNI队列上，MSR-IVA相比独立成对IVA基线将匹配源耦合提高了6.5%，并将不匹配依赖性降低了15.7%。在同时表达两种状态的受试者中，MSR-IVA的跨状态结构源平均绝对相关系数为0.9177，而无共享时为0.2978，证明其实现了受控的结构共享，既保持了源对应关系又允许状态特异适应。

Conclusion: MSR-IVA通过受控结构共享机制有效平衡了跨状态的一致性需求与状态特异性需求，在保持源对应关系的同时允许状态特异适应，为多状态多模态脑影像融合提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal fusion of structural MRI (sMRI) and dynamic functional network connectivity (dFNC) can reveal how brain structure relates to changing functional states. When the same structural latent representation is coupled with multiple states, applying independent vector analysis (IVA) separately to each state can produce unrelated structural decompositions, while forcing identical decompositions may suppress state-specific relationships. In addition, not every subject expresses every dynamic state. We propose masked structural residual IVA (MSR-IVA), a state-aware framework that combines a shared structural representation with state-specific residual adaptations and masks for incomplete state expression. On an Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative cohort, MSR-IVA improved matched source coupling by 6.5% and reduced unmatched dependence by 15.7% relative to the independent pairwise IVA baseline. Among subjects expressing both states, mean absolute cross-state structural source correlation was 0.9177 for MSR-IVA versus 0.2978 for no sharing, demonstrating controlled structural sharing that preserves source correspondence while allowing state-specific adaptation.

</details>


### [261] [D$^3$-MOPD: Adaptive Dynamic Domain ScheDuling for Efficient Multi-Teacher Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24987)
*Zechen Sun,Zhiwei Zhang,Fei Zhao,Juntao Li,Mu Chuan,Huayu Deng,Guojian Zhan,Wenliang Chen,Yao Hu,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出D³-MOPD（动态领域调度多教师on-policy蒸馏），一种零开销调度器，利用训练中已有的per-domain反向KL信号在线调整领域混合比例，解决了固定领域数据混合导致的计算浪费和欠训练问题。在Qwen3.6-35B-A3B学生模型上，相比vanilla-MOPD将平均学生-教师性能差距的收敛从63%提升到97%，并以约3倍减少的rollout步数达到相同峰值性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MOPD方法在训练前固定per-domain数据混合比例，但不同领域收敛速度差异显著：有的领域早早收敛，有的领域在整个训练预算中持续提升。固定的混合比例会在快速收敛的领域上浪费算力，同时欠训练慢收敛的领域，导致整体性能次优。

Method: D³-MOPD利用训练中每个域已经产生的per-domain反向KL散度信号，通过一个运行在训练进程之外、异步执行的off-process watcher，周期性跟踪每个域的KL轨迹，估计剩余提升空间（headroom）和当前改进速率，据此在线调整领域采样比例。该调度器不改变核心训练循环，零额外计算开销，并可自然扩展到任意数量的领域。

Result: 在Qwen3.6-35B-A3B学生模型（由四个领域专家教师蒸馏）上，D³-MOPD关闭了97%的平均学生-教师性能差距（vanilla MOPD仅为63%）；以约3倍的rollout步数减少达到相同峰值性能；并在七个基准中的三个上超越了专家教师。

Conclusion: 动态领域调度能有效利用不同领域收敛速度的差异，将计算资源动态分配给仍具改进空间的领域，从而显著提升多教师蒸馏的效率和最终性能。并且领域越多样、收敛模式差异越大，调度器的收益越明显。

Abstract: Multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) distills several domain-expert teachers into a single student by minimizing per-domain reverse-KL divergence on the student's own rollouts. Existing approaches typically fix the per-domain data mixture before training, overlooking the fact that different domains converge at substantially different rates: some plateau early while others continue to improve throughout the training budget. A fixed mixture therefore wastes compute on fast-converging domains and undertrains slower-converging ones. To address this, we propose D$^3$-MOPD (Dynamic Domain ScheDuling for MOPD), a zero-overhead scheduler that repurposes the per-domain reverse-KL signal already produced during training to adapt the domain mixture online. Running asynchronously outside the training process, an off-process watcher periodically tracks each domain's KL trajectory, estimates remaining headroom and current improvement rate, and accordingly adjusts the domain sampling ratios without altering the core training loop. Our D$^3$-MOPD scales naturally to arbitrary numbers of domains, and the expected benefit grows as more domains introduce more diverse convergence patterns for the scheduler to exploit. On a Qwen3.6-35B-A3B student distilled from four domain-expert teachers, D$^3$-MOPD closes 97% of the average student-to-teacher performance gap, compared with 63% for vanilla MOPD, reaches the same peak performance with an approximately 3$\times$ reduction in rollout steps, and surpasses the specialist teachers on three of seven benchmarks.

</details>


### [262] [Rollout-Decoded Reconstruction for Long-Horizon Prediction in Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25017)
*Rishi Shah,Rishav Shrestha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Rollout-Decoded Reconstruction (RDR)方法，通过让潜在世界模型在训练时像评估时一样进行自由滚动解码，并惩罚与真实观测的重构误差，弥合了训练与部署之间的差距。在Kuramoto-Sivashinsky混沌方程上，RDR将有效预测时间从3.87±0.23提升到6.97±0.42时间单位（1.80×提升），在10/10预注册配置中均保持优势，且不增加任何参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在世界模型在训练时解码器基于锚定在观测上的潜变量进行训练，但部署时却要在模型自身的自由滚动输出上运行数百步，远超最后一个观测点。这种训练与部署之间的分布差距（train-deploy gap）导致模型在自由滚动时性能显著退化，本文旨在通过让训练过程与评估过程一致来弥合这一差距。

Method: RDR只增加一个损失项：在训练期间让潜在世界模型以与评估完全相同的方式自由运行，对每个滚动步的潜变量进行解码，并计算解码结果与真实ground truth之间的重构误差作为额外惩罚。该方法不增加任何参数，仅在训练时消耗计算资源，当权重设为零时退化为标准目标函数，因此所有对比实验都是单标志A/B测试。

Result: 在混沌的Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程上，RDR将有效预测时间从3.87±0.23提升到6.97±0.42时间单位，参数保持193,568不变，实现1.80×的提升。该提升在未用于选择的种子上得到确认，并在全部10/10个预注册配置中保持优势，提升比范围为1.71-2.50×。

Conclusion: RDR通过一个简洁的损失项有效弥合了潜在世界模型训练与部署之间的分布差距，在混沌动力学系统上取得了显著且可复现的性能提升。作者同时保持审慎态度：优势随潜宽度的增长趋势是描述性的而非因果结论，两个经典任务上的控制实验仍是初步结果，表明研究广度有限但针对单一系统证据充分。

Abstract: A latent world model trains its decoder on latents anchored to observations, then deploys it on the model's own free-running rollout, hundreds of steps past the last observation. Rollout-Decoded Reconstruction (RDR) closes this gap with a single loss term that free-runs the model during training exactly as evaluation will, decodes every rollout latent, and penalizes reconstruction error against ground truth. The term adds no parameters, costs training-time compute only, and reduces to the standard objective at weight zero, so every comparison in this paper is a one-flag A/B. On the chaotic Kuramoto-Sivashinsky equation, RDR raises valid prediction time (the time to first crossing of normalized error 0.5) from $3.87 \pm 0.23$ to $6.97 \pm 0.42$ time units at an identical 193,568 parameters, a $1.80\times$ improvement confirmed on seeds never used in selection and holding in 10 of 10 preregistered configurations at ratios of 1.71-2.50$\times$. The results come from a single system; a sweep in which the advantage grows with latent width is descriptive, and control experiments on two classic tasks are preliminary.

</details>


### [263] [On the Representational Geometry of Dynamic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25034)
*Richard F. M. Lim,Ruriko Yoshida*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从几何视角研究了神经架构在动态规划（DP）目标上长度泛化失败的原因。作者证明每个有限min-plus DP都等价于DAG上的最短路径、热带多项式及其扩展Newton多面体三者构成同构半环，并通过两种结构性反例（维度约简的非单射性与非封闭性、串并联组合无法构建所有DAG拓扑）揭示了长度泛化困难的几何本质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准神经架构在动态规划目标的长度泛化上常常失败，但此前缺乏对这种现象的结构性理解。作者希望从几何角度揭示这一困难的本质根源，从而为设计更好的架构提供理论指导。

Method: 作者建立了图（DAG）、多项式（热带多项式）与多面体（扩展Newton多面体）三者之间的同构半环关系，分别在形式多项式和计算函数两个层面进行连接，并通过这些操作来刻画所有结构冗余。在此基础上，通过构造结构性反例来检验决策边界从长度T推广到T+1的可能性。

Result: 作者给出两个结构性否定结果：其一，半环中两种天然的降维方式（将变量设为两个恒等元）既非单射也并非始终在DP类内封闭；其二，串并联组合无法从更小的子DAG构造出所有DAG拓扑，甚至所有仅涉及终端的操作也无法捕获全部DP组合。

Conclusion: 长度泛化失败并非偶然的计算困难，而是源于DP自身的代数结构特征——其维度约简机制的非良性和组合操作的拓扑不完整性共同构成了根本性的几何障碍。

Abstract: Standard neural architectures often fail to generalize to longer inputs for dynamic programming (DP) targets. We investigate what makes this hard geometrically. Every finite min-plus DP is a shortest path on a DAG, which is equivalently a tropical polynomial whose extended Newton polyhedron encodes the decision boundary of which path wins. We prove these three descriptions (graph, polynomial, polyhedron) form isomorphic semirings at two levels --- formal polynomials and their computed functions --- connected by operations that characterize all structural redundancies. We then address the length-generalization question geometrically: does the decision boundary at length $T$ decide the boundary at $T+1$? We present two structural negatives. The semiring's two native ways to reduce dimension (setting a variable to each identity) are neither injective nor always closed within the DP. Series and parallel composition fail to construct all DAG topologies from smaller sub-DAGs, and even all terminal-only operations do not capture all DP compositions.

</details>


### [264] [DeMMO: Longitudinal and Cross-Disease Modelling of Digital Mobility Outcomes via Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25073)
*Menghui Zhou,Zhipeng Yuan,Vitaveska Lanfranchi,Po Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DeMMO是一个可解释的纵向、多疾病、多结局学习框架，用于从可穿戴传感器衍生的数字移动性结局（DMOs）中联合建模多种疾病与多个临床结局的进展。其核心技术贡献是自动跨疾病、跨结局关系学习机制，无需配对参与者即可实现选择性信息共享，在Mobilise-D大规模多中心数据集上优于九个强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DMO研究通常一次只研究一种疾病、一次访问，无法建模多种疾病中多元DMO与多个临床结局之间随时间的联合演化关系；而现有的时序多任务框架只能建模单一疾病内的进展，无法跨疾病联合建模多个预测结局，尤其当疾病队列不共享参与者时。

Method: DeMMO将每个疾病-结局目标表示为纵向DMO系数矩阵，结合时序正则化与稳定的、访问特定的特征选择。核心机制是自动跨疾病与跨结局关系学习，直接从纵向映射中学习带符号的关系，实现无需配对参与者的选择性信息共享。

Result: 在Mobilise-D数据集（24个统一真实世界DMO、5次访视、多种行动受限疾病）上，DeMMO在整体和结局特定的预测性能上均达到最优，显著优于九个强基线（包括线性、纵向和深度回归模型）。稳定性选择进一步识别出可靠的纵向DMO模式用于临床验证。

Conclusion: DeMMO有效解决了跨疾病、跨结局联合纵向建模的难题，通过自动关系学习实现选择性信息共享，为多疾病情境下的数字移动性结局监测和疾病进展评估提供了一种可解释且高性能的方案。

Abstract: Digital mobility outcomes (DMOs) derived from wearable sensors characterise mobility in daily life and offer a promising means of monitoring disease progression. Yet most DMO studies examine one disease at one visit; they do not model how multivariate DMO relationships with multiple clinical outcomes evolve jointly across diseases. Technically, existing temporal multi-task frameworks can model progression within an individual disease, but they do not jointly model multiple prediction outcomes across diseases, particularly when disease cohorts do not share participants. To address these gaps, we propose DeMMO, an interpretable framework for longitudinal, multi-disease, and multi-outcome learning. DeMMO represents each disease-outcome objective by a longitudinal DMO coefficient matrix and combines temporal regularisation with stable and visit-specific feature selection. Its central technical contribution is an automatic cross-disease and cross-outcome relation-learning mechanism that learns signed relations directly from these longitudinal mappings, enabling selective information sharing without paired participants. We evaluate DeMMO on the recently released, large-scale, multicentre Mobilise-D dataset, which provides a new opportunity to study 24 harmonised real-world DMOs over five visits across multiple mobility-limiting conditions. Against nine strong linear, longitudinal, and deep-regression baselines, DeMMO achieves the best overall and outcome-specific prediction performance, with significant improvements over the strongest baselines. Stability selection further identifies reliable longitudinal DMO patterns for subsequent clinical validation and disease monitoring. The implementation code and experimental results are available at https://github.com/menghui-zhou/DeMMO.

</details>


### [265] [NVExplain: Explaining Time Series Forecasting with Latent Trajectory Analysis and Structure-Preserving Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25080)
*Muyan Anna Li,Manikandan Ravikiran,Aditi Gautam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种模型无关的可解释性框架，通过语义流（semantic flow）将预测结果归因到时间上相关的历史滞后值，构建滞后-预测水平归因矩阵，并提供结构保持扰动和稀疏局部代理模型以生成人类可读、时间上连贯的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测模型在高风险场景中广泛使用，但现有事后解释方法往往忽略时间依赖性，且无法提供针对具体预测水平的解释。

Method: 将预测建模为潜在轨迹，引入语义流量化内部表征中信息随时间的演化；聚合语义流构建滞后-预测水平归因矩阵；通过结构保持扰动和稀疏局部代理模型生成可读且时间连贯的解释。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，语义流变体在忠实度上达到或优于标准事后基线方法，且计算效率显著更高；稳定性分析表明解释具有鲁棒性，并识别出需要谨慎应用解释的区间。

Conclusion: 所提出的语义流可解释性框架在忠实度、稳定性和计算效率之间取得了良好权衡，为时间序列预测提供了更可靠的可解释工具。

Abstract: Time series forecasting models are widely used in high-stakes settings, yet their predictions remain difficult to interpret because existing post-hoc methods often ignore temporal dependence and fail to provide horizon-specific explanations. We propose a model-agnostic explainability framework that explains forecasting predictions by attributing each forecast horizon to temporally relevant historical lags. The framework models forecasting as a latent trajectory and introduces semantic flow to quantify how information evolves across time in the model's internal representations. By aggregating semantic flow, it constructs a lag-horizon attribution matrix that captures horizon-resolved temporal influence. To improve explainability, we further generate structure-preserving perturbations and fit sparse local surrogate models, producing human-readable and temporally coherent explanations. We evaluate the method using faithfulness and stability diagnostics across multiple benchmark datasets. Results show that the semantic-flow variant achieves competitive or superior faithfulness compared to standard post-hoc baselines, while being substantially more computationally efficient. Stability analysis further demonstrates that the explanations are robust and identifies regimes where interpretation should be applied with caution.

</details>


### [266] [The Frame Kernel Method for Multiscale Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25084)
*Branden Frieden,Ryan Whitehead,M. Keith Ballard,Robert M. Kirby,Varun Shankar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种原生多尺度算子学习方法，用于多尺度偏微分方程数值求解器的代理建模。核心创新是新型多尺度核框架函数逼近技术，将算子学习转化为输入/输出函数的框架系数映射学习，并支持后验多尺度分解。方法适用于张量积网格和点云，在挑战性问题上显著优于流行的神经算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多尺度PDE的数值求解计算成本高昂，而现有代理建模方法（如神经算子）在处理固有尺度分离的问题时精度有限，且缺乏可解释的多尺度分解能力。

Method: 提出一种新型多尺度核框架（multiscale kernel frame）函数逼近技术，将算子学习问题转化为学习输入函数框架系数到输出函数框架系数的映射。在泛化阶段，该技术自动实现输出函数的多尺度分解。方法同时支持张量积网格和点云两种数据表示。

Result: 提供了框架逼近的插值证明、误差估计和数值收敛率。在文献中具有挑战性的多尺度PDE代理建模问题上，该方法比流行的神经算子（neural operators）显著更准确，且能够提供后验多尺度分解。

Conclusion: 新方法在多尺度PDE代理建模中兼具高精度和可解释性，通过多尺度分解能力超越了传统神经算子的局限，为多尺度科学计算提供了更可靠的替代方案。

Abstract: We present a natively multiscale operator learning method for the surrogate modeling of (numerical solvers for) multiscale partial differential equations (PDEs). The primary novelty of our method lies in a novel multiscale kernel frame function approximation technique. Leveraging this new kernel frame technique, we cast the operator learning problem as one of learning frame coefficients of output functions as a function of frame coefficients of input functions. The generalization step then automatically allows for a multiscale decomposition of the output functions. Our method is applicable to both tensor-product grids and point clouds. We present interpolation proofs, error estimates, and numerical convergence rates for our frame approximation. We the demonstrate the applicability of our method for the surrogate modeling of inherently multiscale PDEs. The new multiscale frame kernel method is significantly more accurate than popular neural operators on challenging problems from the literature, while simultaneously admitting an a posteriori multiscale decomposition upon generalization.

</details>


### [267] [The Von-Neumann State-Space Transformer for neural decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25088)
*Morteza Sarafyazd*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出von-Neumann State-Space Transformer (VN-SST)，一种内存增强型Transformer，其前馈层通过低秩指令库为每个token合成专用算子，在神经解码和语言建模任务上展现出显著的数据与参数效率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 皮层计算是低维的：少量潜在变量携带在神经群体活动中，驱动单个神经元的高维响应。作者旨在提升样本效率，即从有限数据和较小参数预算中实现良好的解码性能。标准Transformer的前馈层对所有token应用相同算子，这不符合神经计算的分层指令-执行范式。

Method: 受冯·诺依曼架构启发，VN-SST的前馈块由一个低秩指令库构成：共享基础算子加上少量可学习的低秩指令，通过每token的代码合成该token实际使用的权重矩阵。该代码从携带的状态空间记忆的低维投影中读取，使慢速潜在轨迹充当指令指针，模拟低维动力学如何路由皮层计算。这不同于传统的软混合专家(soft MoE)——后者仅混合输出而非混合算子本身。

Result: 在三个运动皮层神经解码基准上，VN-SST远比现代Transformer更数据高效，能联合预测尖峰和行为解码。在最稀少的基准上以大幅优势胜出，在另外两个基准上领先，且能将更长的上下文转化为准确率上升而非下降。网络将大型指令库压缩到每token几个比特，表明程序容量充当控制通道而非精度杠杆。在同一模型上也展示了在LLM小文本基准上的参数效率优势。

Conclusion: VN-SST验证了"控制器解码指令、执行token专属算子"的冯·诺依曼式高效计算假说，为神经解码提供了一种通用且高效的架构机制，同时暗示该机制具有跨领域的普适性。

Abstract: Cortical computation is strikingly low-dimensional: a handful of latent variables, carried in a neural population's activity, steer the higher-dimensional responses of individual neurons. Our aim is sample efficiency-models that decode well from limited data and at small parameter budgets. In a standard Transformer layer, the feed-forward block applies the same operator to every token.
